# 子專案 2：編輯器畫面 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** 在子專案 1 的核心引擎之上，做出可以實際操作的麵包板網頁編輯器：放元件、手動接線、電路檢查、AI 標準佈線（動畫＋統計）、接線清單、比較模式、Undo/Redo、存檔與匯出，並提供網站版與單一 HTML 離線版。

**Architecture:** React + SVG 畫面層疊在核心引擎上。所有會改動電路的動作都是純函式指令（`(doc) => CommandResult`），Zustand store 管理文件、Undo 歷史與介面狀態；命中判定、吸附、放置判斷在格子座標上用純函式完成；佈線在內嵌 Web Worker 執行；板子、元件、導線只用 SVG 屬性繪製，匯出 SVG 時可以直接複製。

**Tech Stack:** React、TypeScript 7、Zustand、Tailwind CSS 4、Vite、vite-plugin-singlefile、Vitest 5 + Testing Library + jsdom、Playwright（Chromium）。

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-05-editor-ui-design.md`

## Global Constraints

- 子專案 1 的 `src/core`、`src/types`、`src/data`、`tests/`（既有檔案）不得修改。
- `src/app/model`、`src/app/commands`、`src/app/geometry`、`src/app/interaction/viewport.ts`、`src/app/interaction/pressPlan.ts`、`src/app/interaction/shortcuts.ts`、`src/app/routing/checklist.ts`、`src/app/persistence/projectIO.ts`、`src/app/persistence/browserStorage.ts`、`src/workers/routingProtocol.ts` 不得引用 React，也不得使用 `document`、`window`。
- 執行期不得載入任何外部資源（CDN、網路字型、遠端資料）。字型：`"Microsoft JhengHei", "PingFang TC", "Noto Sans TC", system-ui, sans-serif`。
- Web Worker 一律以 `?worker&inline` 匯入。
- 介面文字繁體中文，專有名詞保留英文。固定字串（必須逐字相同）：`Standard Routing Complete`、`No valid routing solution.`、`✓ Circuit Valid：電路檢查通過`、`電路檢查 Check Circuit`、`AI 標準佈線`、`比較`、`你的接線`、`接線清單`、`檢查結果`、`選取`。
- SVG：1 格 = 10 單位；板子外框 `0 0 640 190`；板子、元件、導線只用 SVG 屬性設定樣式，不用 CSS class。
- 數值：歷史上限 100 步；即時檢查防抖 150 ms；自動存檔防抖 1000 ms；長按 500 ms；點擊判定 5 px；滾輪每格 ×1.15；`viewport.scale` 範圍 0.5–8；比較模式並排門檻 1280 px；版面斷點 1024 px；寬度 < 1024 px 時可點擊元素至少 44 × 44 px；動畫每條 `min(250, 4000 ÷ 導線數)` ms。
- localStorage 鍵：`breadboard-lab:autosave`、`breadboard-lab:projects`、`breadboard-lab:project:<id>`。
- 畫面層的單元測試檔第一行為 `// @vitest-environment jsdom`，並在 `afterEach` 呼叫 `cleanup()`；需要全域 store 的測試在 `beforeEach` 呼叫 `resetEditor()`。
- 所有 `setPointerCapture`／`releasePointerCapture` 呼叫都包在 try/catch 中（合成事件或已結束的指標會丟例外）。

## Review Focus

- 瀏覽器裡的自動存檔損毀或格式不符（手動改過、舊版本）：啟動時要改用空白電路，不能白屏。→ Task 7 測試。
- 瀏覽器禁止使用 localStorage（隱私模式、被管理員封鎖）：編輯照常，儲存時顯示中文錯誤，不丟例外。→ Task 7 測試。
- AI 佈線計算中按 `Ctrl+Z` 或觸發任何編輯指令：必須被忽略，不能把文件弄亂。→ Task 8 測試。
- 專案名稱含 `/ \ : * ? " < > |`：匯出的檔名要把這些字元換成 `_`。→ Task 7 測試。
- 在同一個空孔上按下後原地放開、或拖回起點（起點 = 終點）：不能建立零長度導線。→ Task 5 測試。

`tests/app/model.test.ts`：

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { colorName, DEFAULT_WIRE_COLOR, WIRE_COLORS } from '../../src/app/model/colors';
import { nextComponentId, nextWireId } from '../../src/app/model/ids';
import { naturalCompare } from '../../src/app/model/naturalCompare';
import {
  BOARD_ID,
  circuitSignature,
  createProject,
  DEFAULT_PROJECT_NAME,
  emptyCircuit,
  isRoutingStale,
  stableStringify,
} from '../../src/app/model/projectFile';
import { circuit, comp, wire } from '../fixtures/build';

const NO_METRICS = { wireCount: 0, totalLength: 0, bends: 0, crossings: 0, overlaps: 0, shortJogs: 0 };

describe('project file', () => {
  it('creates an empty project with one MB-102 board', () => {
    const doc = createProject(undefined, new Date('2026-10-06T00:00:00Z'));
    expect(doc).toEqual({
      format: 'breadboard-lab',
      version: 1,
      name: DEFAULT_PROJECT_NAME,
      circuit: emptyCircuit(),
      routing: null,
      savedAt: '2026-10-06T00:00:00.000Z',
    });
    expect(doc.circuit.boards).toEqual([{ id: BOARD_ID, layoutId: 'MB102', x: 0, y: 0, groupId: null }]);
  });

  it('stringifies objects independent of key order and drops undefined', () => {
    expect(stableStringify({ b: 1, a: [2, { d: undefined, c: 3 }] })).toBe('{"a":[2,{"c":3}],"b":1}');
  });

  it('gives the same signature regardless of component order', () => {
    const a = circuit([comp('R1', 'resistor', 'A10'), comp('D1', 'led', 'C30')], [wire('m1', 'B10', 'B30')]);
    const reversed = { ...a, components: [...a.components].reverse() };
    expect(circuitSignature(reversed)).toBe(circuitSignature(a));
    const moved = { ...a, components: [comp('R1', 'resistor', 'A11'), a.components[1]] };
    expect(circuitSignature(moved)).not.toBe(circuitSignature(a));
  });

  it('marks routing as stale after the circuit changes', () => {
    const doc = createProject();
    expect(isRoutingStale(doc)).toBe(false);
    const routed = {
      ...doc,
      routing: { before: [], beforeScore: 0, metrics: NO_METRICS, score: 100, signature: circuitSignature(doc.circuit), checklist: {} },
    };
    expect(isRoutingStale(routed)).toBe(false);
    const edited = { ...routed, circuit: { ...routed.circuit, wires: [wire('m1', 'A10', 'A20')] } };
    expect(isRoutingStale(edited)).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('colors', () => {
  it('names the palette colours and falls back to 其他', () => {
    expect(WIRE_COLORS).toHaveLength(10);
    expect(colorName('#D32F2F')).toBe('紅');
    expect(colorName('#212121')).toBe('黑');
    expect(colorName('#123456')).toBe('其他');
    expect(DEFAULT_WIRE_COLOR).toBe('#d32f2f');
  });
});

describe('naturalCompare', () => {
  it('orders embedded numbers numerically', () => {
    expect(['NET_10', 'NET_2', 'GND', 'NET_1'].sort(naturalCompare)).toEqual(['GND', 'NET_1', 'NET_2', 'NET_10']);
  });
});

describe('ids', () => {
  it('picks the smallest free id for each category prefix', () => {
    const c = circuit(
      [comp('U1', '74LS00', 'F10'), comp('U3', '74LS04', 'F30'), comp('R1', 'resistor', 'A40')],
      [wire('m1', 'A50', 'A55')],
    );
    expect(nextComponentId(c, 'ic')).toBe('U2');
    expect(nextComponentId(c, 'resistor')).toBe('R2');
    expect(nextComponentId(c, 'led')).toBe('D1');
    expect(nextComponentId(c, 'dip-switch')).toBe('SW1');
    expect(nextWireId(c)).toBe('m2');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 實作**

`src/app/model/projectFile.ts`：

```ts
import type { Circuit, Wire } from '../../types/circuit';
import type { RoutingMetrics } from '../../types/routing';

export const PROJECT_FORMAT = 'breadboard-lab';
export const DEFAULT_PROJECT_NAME = '未命名電路';
export const BOARD_ID = 'b1';

export interface RoutingRecord {
  before: Wire[];
  beforeScore: number;
  metrics: RoutingMetrics;
  score: number;
  signature: string;
  checklist: Record<string, boolean>;
}

export interface ProjectFile {
  format: typeof PROJECT_FORMAT;
  version: 1;
  name: string;
  circuit: Circuit;
  routing: RoutingRecord | null;
  savedAt: string;
}

export function emptyCircuit(): Circuit {
  return {
    version: 1,
    boards: [{ id: BOARD_ID, layoutId: 'MB102', x: 0, y: 0, groupId: null }],
    components: [],
    wires: [],
  };
}

export function createProject(name: string = DEFAULT_PROJECT_NAME, now: Date = new Date()): ProjectFile {
  return { format: PROJECT_FORMAT, version: 1, name, circuit: emptyCircuit(), routing: null, savedAt: now.toISOString() };
}

/** 物件鍵排序後的 JSON：同樣的內容永遠得到同樣的字串（undefined 的欄位省略，和 JSON.stringify 一致）。 */
export function stableStringify(value: unknown): string {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(stableStringify).join(',')}]`;
  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
    const entries = Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
      .filter(([, v]) => v !== undefined)
      .sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0));
    return `{${entries.map(([k, v]) => `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${stableStringify(v)}`).join(',')}}`;
  }
  return JSON.stringify(value);
}

/** 元件與導線的內容簽章（依 ID 排序），用來判斷接線清單是否過期。 */
export function circuitSignature(circuit: Circuit): string {
  const byId = <T extends { id: string }>(list: T[]) => [...list].sort((a, b) => (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0));
  return stableStringify({ components: byId(circuit.components), wires: byId(circuit.wires) });
}

export function isRoutingStale(doc: ProjectFile): boolean {
  return doc.routing !== null && doc.routing.signature !== circuitSignature(doc.circuit);
}
```

`src/app/model/colors.ts`：

```ts
export interface WireColor {
  hex: string;
  name: string;
}

export const WIRE_COLORS: WireColor[] = [
  { hex: '#d32f2f', name: '紅' },
  { hex: '#212121', name: '黑' },
  { hex: '#f9a825', name: '黃' },
  { hex: '#2e7d32', name: '綠' },
  { hex: '#1565c0', name: '藍' },
  { hex: '#ef6c00', name: '橘' },
  { hex: '#f5f5f5', name: '白' },
  { hex: '#6a1b9a', name: '紫' },
  { hex: '#6d4c41', name: '棕' },
  { hex: '#9e9e9e', name: '灰' },
];

export const DEFAULT_WIRE_COLOR = '#d32f2f';

export function colorName(hex: string): string {
  return WIRE_COLORS.find((c) => c.hex.toLowerCase() === hex.toLowerCase())?.name ?? '其他';
}
```

```ts
const collator = new Intl.Collator('en', { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });

/** 自然排序：NET_2 排在 NET_10 前面。 */
export function naturalCompare(a: string, b: string): number {
  return collator.compare(a, b);
}
```

```ts
import type { Circuit } from '../../types/circuit';
import type { ComponentCategory } from '../../types/component';

export const ID_PREFIX: Record<ComponentCategory, string> = {
  ic: 'U',
  resistor: 'R',
  led: 'D',
  vcc: 'VCC',
  gnd: 'GND',
  'dc-power': 'PWR',
  'dip-switch': 'SW',
  pushbutton: 'BTN',
};

function nextFree(used: Set<string>, prefix: string): string {
  for (let n = 1; ; n++) {
    const id = `${prefix}${n}`;
    if (!used.has(id)) return id;
  }
}

export function nextComponentId(circuit: Circuit, category: ComponentCategory): string {
  return nextFree(new Set(circuit.components.map((c) => c.id)), ID_PREFIX[category]);
}

export function nextWireId(circuit: Circuit): string {
  return nextFree(new Set(circuit.wires.map((w) => w.id)), 'm');
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { boundsOf, componentCells } from '../../src/app/geometry/componentShape';
import { hitTest } from '../../src/app/geometry/hitTest';
import { rotateClockwise } from '../../src/app/geometry/rotate';
import { anchorCenteredAt, snapAnchor, trenchAnchorY } from '../../src/app/geometry/snap';
import { flippedPath, lPath } from '../../src/app/geometry/wirePath';
import { getComponentDefinition } from '../../src/core/circuit/library';
import { circuit, comp, hole, pt, wire } from '../fixtures/build';

const def = (id: string) => getComponentDefinition(id)!;
const xy = (points: Array<{ x: number; y: number }>) => points.map((p) => [p.x, p.y]);

describe('componentCells', () => {
  it('covers the pins and the body of a resistor', () => {
    const cells = componentCells(comp('R1', 'resistor', 'A20'));
    expect(cells).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(xy(cells)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([[20, 4], [21, 4], [22, 4], [23, 4], [24, 4]]));
  });

  it('includes the cells between LED legs', () => {
    expect(componentCells(comp('D1', 'led', 'C10', { span: 3 }))).toHaveLength(4);
  });

  it('covers the whole DIP rectangle', () => {
    expect(boundsOf(componentCells(comp('U1', '74LS00', 'F10')))).toEqual({ minX: 10, minY: 8, maxX: 16, maxY: 11 });
  });
});

describe('hitTest', () => {
  const c = circuit([comp('U1', '74LS00', 'F10'), comp('D1', 'led', 'C30', { span: 3 })], [wire('m1', 'A40', 'A50')]);

  it('prefers pins, then component areas, then wires, then holes', () => {
    expect(hitTest(c, hole('E10'))).toEqual({ kind: 'pin', componentId: 'U1', pinNumber: 14 });
    expect(hitTest(c, pt(12, 9))).toEqual({ kind: 'component', componentId: 'U1' });
    expect(hitTest(c, hole('C31'))).toEqual({ kind: 'component', componentId: 'D1' });
    expect(hitTest(c, hole('A45'))).toEqual({ kind: 'wire', wireId: 'm1' });
    expect(hitTest(c, hole('J60'))).toEqual({ kind: 'hole', point: hole('J60') });
    expect(hitTest(c, pt(20, 9))).toEqual({ kind: 'empty', point: pt(20, 9) });
  });
});

describe('snapping', () => {
  it('pins DIP packages across the trench at 0 and 180 degrees', () => {
    expect(trenchAnchorY(def('74LS00'), 0)).toBe(11);
    expect(trenchAnchorY(def('74LS00'), 180)).toBe(8);
    expect(trenchAnchorY(def('74LS154'), 0)).toBe(13);
    expect(trenchAnchorY(def('74LS154'), 180)).toBe(7);
    expect(trenchAnchorY(def('dipsw-8'), 0)).toBe(11);
    expect(trenchAnchorY(def('74LS00'), 90)).toBeNull();
    expect(trenchAnchorY(def('resistor'), 0)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('rounds to the nearest grid point and applies the trench rule', () => {
    expect(snapAnchor(def('resistor'), 0, { x: 12.4, y: 5.6 })).toEqual({ x: 12, y: 6 });
    expect(snapAnchor(def('74LS00'), 0, { x: 12.6, y: 3 })).toEqual({ x: 13, y: 11 });
  });

  it('centres parts on the pointer', () => {
    expect(anchorCenteredAt(def('74LS00'), 0, undefined, { x: 13, y: 5 })).toEqual({ x: 10, y: 11 });
    expect(anchorCenteredAt(def('resistor'), 0, undefined, { x: 32, y: 12 })).toEqual({ x: 30, y: 12 });
    expect(anchorCenteredAt(def('led'), 0, undefined, { x: 40, y: 12 })).toEqual({ x: 40, y: 12 });
    expect(anchorCenteredAt(def('vcc'), 0, undefined, { x: 5, y: 2 })).toEqual({ x: 5, y: 2 });
  });
});

describe('rotateClockwise', () => {
  it('turns a resistor about its centre', () => {
    const r = rotateClockwise(comp('R1', 'resistor', [30, 12]));
    expect(r.rotation).toBe(90);
    expect(r.anchor).toEqual({ x: 32, y: 10 });
  });

  it('turns a DIP to 90 degrees, then back across the trench at 180', () => {
    const quarter = rotateClockwise(comp('U1', '74LS00', 'F10'));
    expect(quarter).toMatchObject({ rotation: 90, anchor: { x: 11, y: 6 } });
    const half = rotateClockwise(quarter);
    expect(half.rotation).toBe(180);
    expect(half.anchor).toEqual({ x: 15, y: 8 });
  });
});

describe('wire paths', () => {
  it('builds L-shaped paths in either order', () => {
    expect(xy(lPath(hole('A10'), hole('C12'), true))).toEqual([[10, 4], [11, 4], [12, 4], [12, 5], [12, 6]]);
    expect(xy(lPath(hole('A10'), hole('C12'), false))).toEqual([[10, 4], [10, 5], [10, 6], [11, 6], [12, 6]]);
    expect(xy(lPath(hole('A10'), hole('A13'), false))).toEqual([[10, 4], [11, 4], [12, 4], [13, 4]]);
  });

  it('flips only wires with exactly one corner', () => {
    const l = { ...wire('m1', 'A10', 'C12'), path: lPath(hole('A10'), hole('C12'), true) };
    expect(xy(flippedPath(l)!)).toEqual([[10, 4], [10, 5], [10, 6], [11, 6], [12, 6]]);
    expect(flippedPath(wire('m2', 'A10', 'A20'))).toBeNull();
    expect(flippedPath(wire('m3', 'A10', [12, 3], 'C14'))).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`src/app/geometry/componentShape.ts`：

```ts
import { resolveFootprint } from '../../core/circuit/footprint';
import { getComponentDefinition } from '../../core/circuit/library';
import type { ComponentDefinition, ComponentInstance } from '../../types/component';
import type { GridPoint } from '../../types/geometry';

export interface Bounds {
  minX: number;
  minY: number;
  maxX: number;
  maxY: number;
}

export function definitionOf(instance: ComponentInstance): ComponentDefinition {
  const def = getComponentDefinition(instance.defId);
  if (!def) throw new Error(`Unknown component definition: ${instance.defId}`);
  return def;
}

/** 元件範圍：腳 ∪ 本體擋住的格點；two-lead 再加上兩腳連線上的格點。 */
export function componentCells(instance: ComponentInstance, def: ComponentDefinition = definitionOf(instance)): GridPoint[] {
  const fp = resolveFootprint(instance, def);
  const cells = new Map<string, GridPoint>();
  const add = (p: GridPoint) => {
    const key = `${p.x},${p.y}`;
    if (!cells.has(key)) cells.set(key, p);
  };
  for (const pin of fp.pins) add(pin.point);
  for (const cell of fp.blocked) add(cell);
  if (def.package.type === 'two-lead') {
    const [a, b] = fp.pins.map((p) => p.point);
    const steps = Math.max(Math.abs(b.x - a.x), Math.abs(b.y - a.y));
    for (let i = 1; i < steps; i++) {
      add({ boardId: a.boardId, x: a.x + Math.sign(b.x - a.x) * i, y: a.y + Math.sign(b.y - a.y) * i });
    }
  }
  return [...cells.values()];
}

export function boundsOf(points: Array<{ x: number; y: number }>): Bounds {
  return {
    minX: Math.min(...points.map((p) => p.x)),
    minY: Math.min(...points.map((p) => p.y)),
    maxX: Math.max(...points.map((p) => p.x)),
    maxY: Math.max(...points.map((p) => p.y)),
  };
}

export function boundsCenter(b: Bounds): { x: number; y: number } {
  return { x: (b.minX + b.maxX) / 2, y: (b.minY + b.maxY) / 2 };
}
```

`src/app/geometry/hitTest.ts`：

```ts
import { getNode } from '../../core/breadboard/naming';
import { getBoard } from '../../core/breadboard/registry';
import { resolveFootprint } from '../../core/circuit/footprint';
import { getComponentDefinition } from '../../core/circuit/library';
import type { Circuit } from '../../types/circuit';
import type { ComponentDefinition, ComponentInstance } from '../../types/component';
import type { GridPoint } from '../../types/geometry';
import { componentCells } from './componentShape';

export type Hit =
  | { kind: 'pin'; componentId: string; pinNumber: number }
  | { kind: 'component'; componentId: string }
  | { kind: 'wire'; wireId: string }
  | { kind: 'hole'; point: GridPoint }
  | { kind: 'empty'; point: GridPoint };

function samePoint(a: GridPoint, b: GridPoint): boolean {
  return a.boardId === b.boardId && a.x === b.x && a.y === b.y;
}

/** 依「腳 → 元件範圍 → 導線 → 孔 → 空白」判斷；後放的元件、後畫的導線在上層，優先命中。 */
export function hitTest(circuit: Circuit, point: GridPoint): Hit {
  const placed: Array<{ instance: ComponentInstance; def: ComponentDefinition }> = [];
  for (const instance of circuit.components) {
    const def = getComponentDefinition(instance.defId);
    if (def) placed.push({ instance, def });
  }
  placed.reverse();
  for (const { instance, def } of placed) {
    const pin = resolveFootprint(instance, def).pins.find((p) => samePoint(p.point, point));
    if (pin) return { kind: 'pin', componentId: instance.id, pinNumber: pin.pinNumber };
  }
  for (const { instance, def } of placed) {
    if (componentCells(instance, def).some((c) => samePoint(c, point))) return { kind: 'component', componentId: instance.id };
  }
  for (let i = circuit.wires.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    if (circuit.wires[i].path.some((p) => samePoint(p, point))) return { kind: 'wire', wireId: circuit.wires[i].id };
  }
  const boardInstance = circuit.boards.find((b) => b.id === point.boardId);
  const node = boardInstance ? getNode(getBoard(boardInstance.layoutId, boardInstance.id), point.x, point.y) : undefined;
  return node?.kind === 'hole' ? { kind: 'hole', point } : { kind: 'empty', point };
}
```

`src/app/geometry/snap.ts`：

```ts
import type { ComponentDefinition, ComponentInstance, Rotation } from '../../types/component';
import { boundsCenter, boundsOf, componentCells } from './componentShape';

/** DIP 在 0° / 180° 時 anchor（第 1 腳）的 y：讓兩排腳跨在凹槽上（規格第 6.3 節）。 */
export function trenchAnchorY(def: ComponentDefinition, rotation: Rotation): number | null {
  if (def.package.type !== 'dip') return null;
  const wide = def.package.rowSpacing === 6;
  if (rotation === 0) return wide ? 13 : 11;
  if (rotation === 180) return wide ? 7 : 8;
  return null;
}

export function snapAnchor(def: ComponentDefinition, rotation: Rotation, desired: { x: number; y: number }): { x: number; y: number } {
  return { x: Math.round(desired.x), y: trenchAnchorY(def, rotation) ?? Math.round(desired.y) };
}

/** 讓元件範圍的中心落在 point（格子座標，可為小數）的 anchor，已吸附。 */
export function anchorCenteredAt(
  def: ComponentDefinition,
  rotation: Rotation,
  span: number | undefined,
  point: { x: number; y: number },
  boardId = 'b1',
): { x: number; y: number } {
  const probe: ComponentInstance = { id: '__probe__', defId: def.id, boardId, anchor: { x: 0, y: 0 }, rotation, props: {} };
  if (span !== undefined) probe.span = span;
  const center = boundsCenter(boundsOf(componentCells(probe, def)));
  return snapAnchor(def, rotation, { x: point.x - center.x, y: point.y - center.y });
}
```

`src/app/geometry/rotate.ts`：

```ts
import type { ComponentDefinition, ComponentInstance, Rotation } from '../../types/component';
import { boundsCenter, boundsOf, componentCells, definitionOf } from './componentShape';
import { trenchAnchorY } from './snap';

/** 以元件範圍外接矩形的中心順時針轉 90°；新中心取最接近原中心的格點，平手時取較小者。 */
export function rotateClockwise(instance: ComponentInstance, def: ComponentDefinition = definitionOf(instance)): ComponentInstance {
  const rotation = ((instance.rotation + 90) % 360) as Rotation;
  const before = boundsCenter(boundsOf(componentCells(instance, def)));
  const turned: ComponentInstance = { ...instance, rotation };
  const after = boundsCenter(boundsOf(componentCells(turned, def)));
  const shift = (v: number) => Math.ceil(v - 0.5);
  const x = instance.anchor.x + shift(before.x - after.x);
  const y = instance.anchor.y + shift(before.y - after.y);
  return { ...turned, anchor: { x, y: trenchAnchorY(def, rotation) ?? y } };
}
```

`src/app/geometry/wirePath.ts`：

```ts
import { countBends } from '../../core/circuit/wireGeometry';
import type { Wire } from '../../types/circuit';
import type { GridPoint } from '../../types/geometry';

/** L 形路徑：horizontalFirst 為 true 時先水平再垂直。起終點同列或同欄時是直線。 */
export function lPath(start: GridPoint, end: GridPoint, horizontalFirst: boolean): GridPoint[] {
  const out: GridPoint[] = [start];
  let { x, y } = start;
  const stepX = () => {
    while (x !== end.x) {
      x += Math.sign(end.x - x);
      out.push({ boardId: start.boardId, x, y });
    }
  };
  const stepY = () => {
    while (y !== end.y) {
      y += Math.sign(end.y - y);
      out.push({ boardId: start.boardId, x, y });
    }
  };
  if (horizontalFirst) {
    stepX();
    stepY();
  } else {
    stepY();
    stepX();
  }
  return out;
}

/** 只有恰好一個轉角的導線可以翻轉，回傳另一個方向的 L 形；直線或多轉角的導線回傳 null。 */
export function flippedPath(wire: Wire): GridPoint[] | null {
  if (wire.path.length < 3 || countBends(wire.path) !== 1) return null;
  const horizontalFirst = wire.path[1].y === wire.path[0].y;
  return lPath(wire.start, wire.end, !horizontalFirst);
}
```

`tests/app/placement.test.ts`：

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { checkPlacement, isFreeHole } from '../../src/app/geometry/placement';
import { circuit, comp, hole, pt, wire } from '../fixtures/build';

const base = circuit([comp('U1', '74LS00', 'F10'), comp('R1', 'resistor', 'A30')], [wire('m1', 'C40', 'C45')]);

describe('checkPlacement', () => {
  it('accepts a part on free holes', () => {
    expect(checkPlacement(base, comp('D1', 'led', 'H40'))).toEqual({ ok: true });
  });

  it('rejects pins that are not on a hole', () => {
    expect(checkPlacement(base, comp('D1', 'led', [20, 9]))).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '第 1 腳沒有插在孔上' });
    expect(checkPlacement(base, comp('R2', 'resistor', 'A62'))).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '第 2 腳沒有插在孔上' });
  });

  it('rejects pins on another part, under a body, or on a wire end', () => {
    expect(checkPlacement(base, comp('D1', 'led', 'A34'))).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '第 1 腳會壓到 R1' });
    expect(checkPlacement(base, comp('D1', 'led', 'A31'))).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '第 1 腳會壓到 R1' });
    expect(checkPlacement(base, comp('D1', 'led', 'C44'))).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '第 2 腳的位置已經插了導線' });
  });

  it('rejects a body that covers another part', () => {
    // R2 的腳在 C19、C23，本體蓋住 C20–C22；D9 的兩腳 C21、C22 被本體蓋住。
    const withLed = circuit([comp('D9', 'led', 'C21')]);
    expect(checkPlacement(withLed, comp('R2', 'resistor', 'C19'))).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '會壓到 D9' });
  });

  it('ignores the part being moved', () => {
    expect(checkPlacement(base, comp('R1', 'resistor', 'A31'), 'R1')).toEqual({ ok: true });
  });

  it('rejects a span below the minimum, unknown parts and unknown boards', () => {
    expect(checkPlacement(base, comp('R2', 'resistor', 'A50', { span: 0 }))).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '兩腳距離太小' });
    expect(checkPlacement(base, comp('X1', 'nope', 'A50'))).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '未知的元件 nope' });
    expect(checkPlacement(base, { ...comp('D1', 'led', 'A50'), boardId: 'b9' })).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '找不到這塊麵包板' });
  });
});

describe('isFreeHole', () => {
  it('is true only for holes without pins, bodies or wire ends', () => {
    expect(isFreeHole(base, hole('A50'))).toBe(true);
    expect(isFreeHole(base, hole('E10'))).toBe(false);
    expect(isFreeHole(base, hole('A31'))).toBe(false);
    expect(isFreeHole(base, hole('C40'))).toBe(false);
    expect(isFreeHole(base, hole('C42'))).toBe(true);
    expect(isFreeHole(base, pt(20, 9))).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 實作**

`src/app/geometry/placement.ts`：

```ts
import { getNode, nodeIdOf } from '../../core/breadboard/naming';
import { getBoard } from '../../core/breadboard/registry';
import { effectiveSpan, resolveFootprint } from '../../core/circuit/footprint';
import { getComponentDefinition } from '../../core/circuit/library';
import type { Circuit } from '../../types/circuit';
import type { ComponentInstance } from '../../types/component';
import type { GridPoint } from '../../types/geometry';

export type PlacementCheck = { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: string };

/** 規格第 6.3 節：腳都在孔上；腳不落在其他元件的腳、本體或導線端點；本體不蓋到其他元件。 */
export function checkPlacement(circuit: Circuit, instance: ComponentInstance, ignoreId?: string): PlacementCheck {
  const def = getComponentDefinition(instance.defId);
  if (!def) return { ok: false, reason: `未知的元件 ${instance.defId}` };
  if (def.package.type === 'two-lead' && effectiveSpan(instance, def) < def.package.minSpan) {
    return { ok: false, reason: '兩腳距離太小' };
  }
  const boardInstance = circuit.boards.find((b) => b.id === instance.boardId);
  if (!boardInstance) return { ok: false, reason: '找不到這塊麵包板' };
  const board = getBoard(boardInstance.layoutId, boardInstance.id);
  const fp = resolveFootprint(instance, def);

  for (const pin of fp.pins) {
    const node = getNode(board, pin.point.x, pin.point.y);
    if (!node || node.kind !== 'hole') return { ok: false, reason: `第 ${pin.pinNumber} 腳沒有插在孔上` };
  }

  const occupiedBy = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const other of circuit.components) {
    if (other.id === ignoreId || other.id === instance.id) continue;
    const otherDef = getComponentDefinition(other.defId);
    if (!otherDef) continue;
    const otherFp = resolveFootprint(other, otherDef);
    for (const p of otherFp.pins) occupiedBy.set(nodeIdOf(p.point), other.id);
    for (const c of otherFp.blocked) occupiedBy.set(nodeIdOf(c), other.id);
  }
  const wireEnds = new Set(circuit.wires.flatMap((w) => [nodeIdOf(w.start), nodeIdOf(w.end)]));

  for (const pin of fp.pins) {
    const id = nodeIdOf(pin.point);
    const other = occupiedBy.get(id);
    if (other) return { ok: false, reason: `第 ${pin.pinNumber} 腳會壓到 ${other}` };
    if (wireEnds.has(id)) return { ok: false, reason: `第 ${pin.pinNumber} 腳的位置已經插了導線` };
  }
  for (const cell of fp.blocked) {
    const other = occupiedBy.get(nodeIdOf(cell));
    if (other) return { ok: false, reason: `會壓到 ${other}` };
  }
  return { ok: true };
}

/** 是孔，而且沒有元件腳、不在元件本體下、不是導線端點。 */
export function isFreeHole(circuit: Circuit, point: GridPoint): boolean {
  const boardInstance = circuit.boards.find((b) => b.id === point.boardId);
  if (!boardInstance) return false;
  const node = getNode(getBoard(boardInstance.layoutId, boardInstance.id), point.x, point.y);
  if (!node || node.kind !== 'hole') return false;
  for (const c of circuit.components) {
    const def = getComponentDefinition(c.defId);
    if (!def) continue;
    const fp = resolveFootprint(c, def);
    if (fp.pins.some((p) => nodeIdOf(p.point) === node.id) || fp.blocked.some((b) => nodeIdOf(b) === node.id)) return false;
  }
  return !circuit.wires.some((w) => nodeIdOf(w.start) === node.id || nodeIdOf(w.end) === node.id);
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  addWire,
  applyRoutingResult,
  clearChecklist,
  deleteComponent,
  deleteWire,
  flipWire,
  moveComponent,
  placeComponent,
  rotateComponent,
  setChecklistItem,
  updateComponentProps,
  type CommandResult,
} from '../../src/app/commands/commands';
import { circuitSignature, createProject, type ProjectFile } from '../../src/app/model/projectFile';
import { autoRoute } from '../../src/core/routing/autoRoute';
import type { Circuit } from '../../src/types/circuit';
import { circuit, comp, hole, pt, wire } from '../fixtures/build';
import { acceptanceCircuit } from '../fixtures/circuits';

const docWith = (c: Circuit): ProjectFile => ({ ...createProject(), circuit: c });

function ok(result: CommandResult): ProjectFile {
  if (!result.ok) throw new Error(`expected ok, got: ${result.reason}`);
  return result.doc;
}

describe('placeComponent', () => {
  it('places a part, snaps DIPs across the trench and selects it', () => {
    const result = placeComponent(createProject(), '74LS00', { x: 10, y: 3 });
    expect(result.ok && result.select).toEqual({ kind: 'component', id: 'U1' });
    expect(ok(result).circuit.components[0]).toMatchObject({ id: 'U1', defId: '74LS00', anchor: { x: 10, y: 11 }, rotation: 0 });
  });

  it('refuses a placement that collides with another part', () => {
    const doc = ok(placeComponent(createProject(), '74LS00', { x: 10, y: 11 }));
    expect(placeComponent(doc, '74LS04', { x: 10, y: 11 })).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '第 1 腳會壓到 U1' });
  });

  it('refuses unknown parts', () => {
    expect(placeComponent(createProject(), 'nope', { x: 10, y: 4 })).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '未知的元件 nope' });
  });
});

describe('moveComponent', () => {
  it('moves a part to a legal spot and refuses illegal ones', () => {
    const doc = docWith(circuit([comp('R1', 'resistor', 'A30')]));
    expect(ok(moveComponent(doc, 'R1', { x: 40, y: 4 })).circuit.components[0].anchor).toEqual({ x: 40, y: 4 });
    expect(moveComponent(doc, 'R1', { x: 20, y: 9 })).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '第 1 腳沒有插在孔上' });
    expect(moveComponent(doc, 'R9', { x: 40, y: 4 })).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '找不到元件 R9' });
  });
});

describe('rotateComponent', () => {
  it('rotates a resistor about its centre', () => {
    const doc = docWith(circuit([comp('R1', 'resistor', 'C20')]));
    expect(ok(rotateComponent(doc, 'R1')).circuit.components[0]).toMatchObject({ rotation: 90, anchor: { x: 22, y: 4 } });
  });

  it('refuses a rotation that would cover another part', () => {
    const doc = docWith(circuit([comp('R1', 'resistor', 'C20'), comp('D1', 'led', 'B22')]));
    expect(rotateComponent(doc, 'R1')).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '會壓到 D1' });
  });
});

describe('delete', () => {
  it('deletes components and wires and clears the selection', () => {
    const doc = docWith(circuit([comp('U1', '74LS00', 'F10')], [wire('m1', 'A30', 'A40')]));
    const noPart = deleteComponent(doc, 'U1');
    expect(noPart.ok && noPart.select).toBeNull();
    expect(ok(noPart).circuit.components).toEqual([]);
    expect(ok(deleteWire(doc, 'm1')).circuit.wires).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('reports unknown ids', () => {
    const doc = createProject();
    expect(deleteComponent(doc, 'U1')).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '找不到元件 U1' });
    expect(deleteWire(doc, 'm1')).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '找不到導線 m1' });
  });
});

describe('addWire', () => {
  it('creates an L-shaped manual wire and selects it', () => {
    const result = addWire(createProject(), hole('A10'), hole('C12'), true, '#212121');
    expect(result.ok && result.select).toEqual({ kind: 'wire', id: 'm1' });
    const w = ok(result).circuit.wires[0];
    expect(w).toMatchObject({ id: 'm1', color: '#212121', source: 'manual', start: hole('A10'), end: hole('C12') });
    expect(w.path.map((p) => [p.x, p.y])).toEqual([[10, 4], [11, 4], [12, 4], [12, 5], [12, 6]]);
  });

  it('refuses a zero-length wire', () => {
    expect(addWire(createProject(), hole('A10'), hole('A10'), true, '#212121')).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '起點和終點不能是同一個孔' });
  });

  it('refuses ends that are not free holes', () => {
    const doc = docWith(circuit([comp('U1', '74LS00', 'F10')]));
    expect(addWire(doc, hole('E10'), hole('A20'), true, '#212121')).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '起點要接在空孔上' });
    expect(addWire(doc, hole('A20'), pt(20, 9), true, '#212121')).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '終點要接在空孔上' });
  });
});

describe('flipWire', () => {
  it('flips a one-corner wire and refuses a straight one', () => {
    const doc = ok(addWire(createProject(), hole('A10'), hole('C12'), true, '#212121'));
    expect(ok(flipWire(doc, 'm1')).circuit.wires[0].path.map((p) => [p.x, p.y])).toEqual([[10, 4], [10, 5], [10, 6], [11, 6], [12, 6]]);
    const straight = docWith(circuit([], [wire('m1', 'A10', 'A20')]));
    expect(flipWire(straight, 'm1')).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '只有一個轉角的導線才能翻轉' });
  });
});

describe('updateComponentProps', () => {
  it('changes resistance and span', () => {
    const doc = docWith(circuit([comp('R1', 'resistor', 'C20')]));
    expect(ok(updateComponentProps(doc, 'R1', { resistance: 220 })).circuit.components[0].props.resistance).toBe(220);
    expect(ok(updateComponentProps(doc, 'R1', { span: 2 })).circuit.components[0].span).toBe(2);
  });

  it('refuses a span that collides, a span on a non two-lead part, and a non-positive voltage', () => {
    const doc = docWith(circuit([comp('R1', 'resistor', 'C20'), comp('D1', 'led', 'C25'), comp('U1', '74LS00', 'F40'), comp('V1', 'vcc', 'T+5')]));
    expect(updateComponentProps(doc, 'R1', { span: 5 })).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '第 2 腳會壓到 D1' });
    expect(updateComponentProps(doc, 'U1', { span: 2 })).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '只有兩腳元件可以調整兩腳距離' });
    expect(updateComponentProps(doc, 'V1', { voltage: 0 })).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '電壓必須大於 0' });
  });
});

describe('applyRoutingResult', () => {
  it('replaces the wires and records the routing', () => {
    const doc = docWith(acceptanceCircuit());
    const result = autoRoute(doc.circuit);
    const routed = ok(applyRoutingResult(doc, result, 12.5));
    expect(routed.circuit.wires.every((w) => w.source === 'auto')).toBe(true);
    expect(routed.routing).toEqual({
      before: doc.circuit.wires,
      beforeScore: 12.5,
      metrics: result.metrics,
      score: result.score,
      signature: circuitSignature(routed.circuit),
      checklist: {},
    });
  });

  it('refuses results that are not a full success', () => {
    const doc = docWith(acceptanceCircuit());
    const result = autoRoute(doc.circuit);
    expect(applyRoutingResult(doc, { ...result, status: 'partial' }, 0)).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '只有完整成功的佈線結果才能套用' });
  });
});

describe('checklist', () => {
  it('ticks and clears items only when a routing exists', () => {
    const doc = docWith(acceptanceCircuit());
    expect(setChecklistItem(doc, 'w_GND_1', true)).toBe(doc);
    const routed = ok(applyRoutingResult(doc, autoRoute(doc.circuit), 0));
    const ticked = setChecklistItem(routed, 'w_GND_1', true);
    expect(ticked.routing!.checklist).toEqual({ w_GND_1: true });
    expect(clearChecklist(ticked).routing!.checklist).toEqual({});
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 實作**

`src/app/commands/commands.ts`：

```ts
import { getComponentDefinition } from '../../core/circuit/library';
import { applyRouting } from '../../core/routing/apply';
import type { Circuit, Wire } from '../../types/circuit';
import type { ComponentDefinition, ComponentInstance, ComponentProps, LedColor, Rotation } from '../../types/component';
import type { GridPoint } from '../../types/geometry';
import type { RoutingResult } from '../../types/routing';
import { checkPlacement, isFreeHole } from '../geometry/placement';
import { rotateClockwise } from '../geometry/rotate';
import { snapAnchor } from '../geometry/snap';
import { flippedPath, lPath } from '../geometry/wirePath';
import type { Selection } from '../model/editorTypes';
import { nextComponentId, nextWireId } from '../model/ids';
import { BOARD_ID, circuitSignature, type ProjectFile } from '../model/projectFile';

export type CommandResult = { ok: true; doc: ProjectFile; select?: Selection | null } | { ok: false; reason: string };

const fail = (reason: string): CommandResult => ({ ok: false, reason });
const withCircuit = (doc: ProjectFile, circuit: Circuit): ProjectFile => ({ ...doc, circuit });
const samePoint = (a: GridPoint, b: GridPoint) => a.boardId === b.boardId && a.x === b.x && a.y === b.y;

export function placeComponent(
  doc: ProjectFile,
  defId: string,
  anchor: { x: number; y: number },
  props: ComponentProps = {},
  rotation: Rotation = 0,
): CommandResult {
  const def = getComponentDefinition(defId);
  if (!def) return fail(`未知的元件 ${defId}`);
  const instance: ComponentInstance = {
    id: nextComponentId(doc.circuit, def.category),
    defId,
    boardId: BOARD_ID,
    anchor: snapAnchor(def, rotation, anchor),
    rotation,
    props: { ...props },
  };
  const check = checkPlacement(doc.circuit, instance);
  if (!check.ok) return fail(check.reason);
  return {
    ok: true,
    doc: withCircuit(doc, { ...doc.circuit, components: [...doc.circuit.components, instance] }),
    select: { kind: 'component', id: instance.id },
  };
}

function updateComponent(
  doc: ProjectFile,
  id: string,
  update: (current: ComponentInstance, def: ComponentDefinition) => ComponentInstance | string,
): CommandResult {
  const current = doc.circuit.components.find((c) => c.id === id);
  if (!current) return fail(`找不到元件 ${id}`);
  const def = getComponentDefinition(current.defId);
  if (!def) return fail(`未知的元件 ${current.defId}`);
  const next = update(current, def);
  if (typeof next === 'string') return fail(next);
  const check = checkPlacement(doc.circuit, next, id);
  if (!check.ok) return fail(check.reason);
  return {
    ok: true,
    doc: withCircuit(doc, { ...doc.circuit, components: doc.circuit.components.map((c) => (c.id === id ? next : c)) }),
  };
}

export function moveComponent(doc: ProjectFile, id: string, anchor: { x: number; y: number }): CommandResult {
  return updateComponent(doc, id, (c, def) => ({ ...c, anchor: snapAnchor(def, c.rotation, anchor) }));
}

export function rotateComponent(doc: ProjectFile, id: string): CommandResult {
  return updateComponent(doc, id, (c, def) => rotateClockwise(c, def));
}

export function deleteComponent(doc: ProjectFile, id: string): CommandResult {
  if (!doc.circuit.components.some((c) => c.id === id)) return fail(`找不到元件 ${id}`);
  return {
    ok: true,
    doc: withCircuit(doc, { ...doc.circuit, components: doc.circuit.components.filter((c) => c.id !== id) }),
    select: null,
  };
}

export function deleteWire(doc: ProjectFile, id: string): CommandResult {
  if (!doc.circuit.wires.some((w) => w.id === id)) return fail(`找不到導線 ${id}`);
  return { ok: true, doc: withCircuit(doc, { ...doc.circuit, wires: doc.circuit.wires.filter((w) => w.id !== id) }), select: null };
}

export function addWire(doc: ProjectFile, start: GridPoint, end: GridPoint, horizontalFirst: boolean, color: string): CommandResult {
  if (samePoint(start, end)) return fail('起點和終點不能是同一個孔');
  if (!isFreeHole(doc.circuit, start)) return fail('起點要接在空孔上');
  if (!isFreeHole(doc.circuit, end)) return fail('終點要接在空孔上');
  const wire: Wire = {
    id: nextWireId(doc.circuit),
    color,
    source: 'manual',
    start,
    end,
    path: lPath(start, end, horizontalFirst),
  };
  return {
    ok: true,
    doc: withCircuit(doc, { ...doc.circuit, wires: [...doc.circuit.wires, wire] }),
    select: { kind: 'wire', id: wire.id },
  };
}

export function flipWire(doc: ProjectFile, id: string): CommandResult {
  const wire = doc.circuit.wires.find((w) => w.id === id);
  if (!wire) return fail(`找不到導線 ${id}`);
  const path = flippedPath(wire);
  if (!path) return fail('只有一個轉角的導線才能翻轉');
  return { ok: true, doc: withCircuit(doc, { ...doc.circuit, wires: doc.circuit.wires.map((w) => (w.id === id ? { ...w, path } : w)) }) };
}

export interface PropsPatch {
  resistance?: number;
  color?: LedColor;
  voltage?: number;
  span?: number;
}

export function updateComponentProps(doc: ProjectFile, id: string, patch: PropsPatch): CommandResult {
  return updateComponent(doc, id, (c, def) => {
    if (patch.span !== undefined && (def.package.type !== 'two-lead' || !Number.isInteger(patch.span))) {
      return '只有兩腳元件可以調整兩腳距離';
    }
    if (patch.voltage !== undefined && !(patch.voltage > 0)) return '電壓必須大於 0';
    const next: ComponentInstance = { ...c, props: { ...c.props } };
    if (patch.resistance !== undefined) next.props.resistance = patch.resistance;
    if (patch.color !== undefined) next.props.color = patch.color;
    if (patch.voltage !== undefined) next.props.voltage = patch.voltage;
    if (patch.span !== undefined) next.span = patch.span;
    return next;
  });
}

export function applyRoutingResult(doc: ProjectFile, result: RoutingResult, beforeScore: number): CommandResult {
  if (result.status !== 'success') return fail('只有完整成功的佈線結果才能套用');
  const circuit = applyRouting(doc.circuit, result);
  return {
    ok: true,
    doc: {
      ...doc,
      circuit,
      routing: {
        before: doc.circuit.wires,
        beforeScore,
        metrics: result.metrics,
        score: result.score,
        signature: circuitSignature(circuit),
        checklist: {},
      },
    },
    select: null,
  };
}

export function setChecklistItem(doc: ProjectFile, wireId: string, done: boolean): ProjectFile {
  if (!doc.routing) return doc;
  return { ...doc, routing: { ...doc.routing, checklist: { ...doc.routing.checklist, [wireId]: done } } };
}

export function clearChecklist(doc: ProjectFile): ProjectFile {
  if (!doc.routing) return doc;
  return { ...doc, routing: { ...doc.routing, checklist: {} } };
}
```

### Task 6: Store 與 Undo/Redo

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/store/editorStore.ts`
- Test: `tests/app/editorStore.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 的型別與 `createProject`、`DEFAULT_WIRE_COLOR`；Task 5 的 `CommandResult`、`setChecklistItem`、`clearChecklist`；核心的 `indexCircuit`、`extractNetsWith`、`terminalClusters`、`validateCircuit`、`scoreRouting`
- Produces:
  - `HISTORY_LIMIT = 100`、`Snapshot { circuit; routing }`
  - `EditorState`（欄位見下方程式碼）、`EditorActions`、`EditorStore = EditorState & EditorActions`
  - `analyzeCircuit(circuit): Analysis`
  - `initialEditorState(doc?, projectId?): EditorState`
  - `createEditorStore(): StoreApi<EditorStore>`（測試用，彼此獨立）
  - `useEditor`：全域 store 的 React hook，同時有 `getState`、`setState`、`subscribe`
  - `resetEditor(doc?, projectId?): void`（把全域 store 重設成初始狀態，測試與「開啟專案」共用）
  - 行為：`run`、`undo`、`redo` 只在 `mode === 'edit'` 時生效；`setChecklistItem`、`clearChecklist` 不進歷史；`replaceDocument` 可選擇是否推進歷史；`runCheck` 會切到「檢查結果」頁，沒有任何錯誤與警告時記錄 `checkPassedAt`

- [ ] **Step 1: 寫失敗的測試**

`tests/app/editorStore.test.ts`：

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { StoreApi } from 'zustand/vanilla';
import { applyRoutingResult, placeComponent } from '../../src/app/commands/commands';
import { createProject } from '../../src/app/model/projectFile';
import { analyzeCircuit, createEditorStore, HISTORY_LIMIT, type EditorStore } from '../../src/app/store/editorStore';
import { autoRoute } from '../../src/core/routing/autoRoute';
import { acceptanceCircuit } from '../fixtures/circuits';

let store: StoreApi<EditorStore>;
beforeEach(() => {
  store = createEditorStore();
});

const place = (defId: string, x: number, y: number) => store.getState().run((doc) => placeComponent(doc, defId, { x, y }));

describe('editor store', () => {
  it('runs commands, records history and selects the result', () => {
    expect(place('74LS00', 10, 11)).toBe(true);
    const s = store.getState();
    expect(s.doc.circuit.components.map((c) => c.id)).toEqual(['U1']);
    expect(s.history.past).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(s.dirty).toBe(true);
    expect(s.selection).toEqual({ kind: 'component', id: 'U1' });
  });

  it('reports a failing command as a notice and keeps the history', () => {
    place('74LS00', 10, 11);
    expect(place('74LS04', 10, 11)).toBe(false);
    expect(store.getState().notice).toMatchObject({ text: '第 1 腳會壓到 U1', tone: 'error' });
    expect(store.getState().history.past).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('undoes and redoes, dropping selections that no longer exist', () => {
    place('74LS00', 10, 11);
    store.getState().undo();
    expect(store.getState().doc.circuit.components).toEqual([]);
    expect(store.getState().selection).toBeNull();
    store.getState().redo();
    expect(store.getState().doc.circuit.components.map((c) => c.id)).toEqual(['U1']);
    expect(store.getState().history.future).toEqual([]);
  });

  it(`keeps at most ${HISTORY_LIMIT} undo steps`, () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < HISTORY_LIMIT + 5; i++) store.getState().run((doc) => ({ ok: true, doc }));
    expect(store.getState().history.past).toHaveLength(HISTORY_LIMIT);
  });

  it('ignores commands and undo while routing', () => {
    place('74LS00', 10, 11);
    store.getState().setMode('routing');
    expect(place('74LS04', 30, 11)).toBe(false);
    store.getState().undo();
    expect(store.getState().doc.circuit.components.map((c) => c.id)).toEqual(['U1']);
  });

  it('ticks checklist items without touching the history', () => {
    store.getState().replaceDocument({ ...createProject(), circuit: acceptanceCircuit() }, { projectId: null, undoable: false, dirty: false });
    store.getState().run((d) => applyRoutingResult(d, autoRoute(d.circuit), 0));
    const steps = store.getState().history.past.length;
    const wireId = store.getState().doc.circuit.wires[0].id;
    store.getState().setChecklistItem(wireId, true);
    expect(store.getState().doc.routing!.checklist).toEqual({ [wireId]: true });
    expect(store.getState().history.past).toHaveLength(steps);
    store.getState().clearChecklist();
    expect(store.getState().doc.routing!.checklist).toEqual({});
  });

  it('replaces the document with or without an undo step', () => {
    place('74LS00', 10, 11);
    store.getState().replaceDocument(createProject('新的'), { projectId: 'p_1', undoable: true, dirty: true });
    expect(store.getState()).toMatchObject({ projectId: 'p_1', dirty: true, selection: null });
    expect(store.getState().doc.name).toBe('新的');
    store.getState().undo();
    expect(store.getState().doc.circuit.components.map((c) => c.id)).toEqual(['U1']);
    store.getState().replaceDocument(createProject(), { projectId: null, undoable: false, dirty: false });
    expect(store.getState().history).toEqual({ past: [], future: [] });
  });

  it('runs Check Circuit and remembers when it passed', () => {
    store.getState().runCheck();
    expect(store.getState().rightTab).toBe('check');
    expect(store.getState().checkPassedAt).not.toBeNull();
    place('74LS00', 10, 11);
    store.getState().runCheck();
    expect(store.getState().checkPassedAt).toBeNull();
  });

  it('analyses wires, routable nets and the score', () => {
    const analysis = analyzeCircuit(acceptanceCircuit());
    expect(analysis.wireCount).toBe(7);
    expect(analysis.netCount).toBe(4);
    expect(analysis.score).toBe(0);
    expect(analysis.liveCheck.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('gives each notice a new id', () => {
    store.getState().notify('a');
    const first = store.getState().notice!.id;
    store.getState().notify('b', 'success');
    expect(store.getState().notice).toMatchObject({ text: 'b', tone: 'success' });
    expect(store.getState().notice!.id).toBeGreaterThan(first);
    store.getState().dismissNotice();
    expect(store.getState().notice).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 實作**

`src/app/store/editorStore.ts`：

```ts
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { createStore, type StateCreator } from 'zustand/vanilla';
import { indexCircuit } from '../../core/circuit/circuitIndex';
import { extractNetsWith, terminalClusters } from '../../core/circuit/nets';
import { validateCircuit } from '../../core/circuit/validate';
import { scoreRouting } from '../../core/routing/score';
import type { Circuit, Wire } from '../../types/circuit';
import { clearChecklist as clearChecklistDoc, setChecklistItem as setChecklistItemDoc, type CommandResult } from '../commands/commands';
import { DEFAULT_WIRE_COLOR } from '../model/colors';
import type {
  Analysis,
  CompareView,
  Ghost,
  Mode,
  Notice,
  PartTemplate,
  RightTab,
  RoutingJob,
  Selection,
  Viewport,
  WirePreview,
} from '../model/editorTypes';
import { createProject, type ProjectFile, type RoutingRecord } from '../model/projectFile';

export const HISTORY_LIMIT = 100;

export interface Snapshot {
  circuit: Circuit;
  routing: RoutingRecord | null;
}

export interface EditorState {
  doc: ProjectFile;
  history: { past: Snapshot[]; future: Snapshot[] };
  dirty: boolean;
  projectId: string | null;
  mode: Mode;
  selection: Selection | null;
  wireColor: string;
  viewport: Viewport;
  analysis: Analysis;
  rightTab: RightTab;
  routingJob: RoutingJob;
  compareView: CompareView;
  notice: Notice | null;
  checkPassedAt: number | null;
  ghost: Ghost | null;
  wirePreview: WirePreview | null;
  armedPart: PartTemplate | null;
  focusWireId: string | null;
  previewWires: Wire[];
}

export interface EditorActions {
  run(command: (doc: ProjectFile) => CommandResult): boolean;
  undo(): void;
  redo(): void;
  replaceDocument(doc: ProjectFile, options: { projectId: string | null; undoable: boolean; dirty: boolean }): void;
  markSaved(projectId: string, doc: ProjectFile): void;
  setChecklistItem(wireId: string, done: boolean): void;
  clearChecklist(): void;
  select(selection: Selection | null): void;
  setMode(mode: Mode): void;
  setWireColor(hex: string): void;
  setViewport(viewport: Viewport): void;
  setRightTab(tab: RightTab): void;
  setCompareView(view: CompareView): void;
  setRoutingJob(job: RoutingJob): void;
  notify(text: string, tone?: Notice['tone']): void;
  dismissNotice(): void;
  refreshAnalysis(): void;
  runCheck(): void;
  setGhost(ghost: Ghost | null): void;
  setWirePreview(preview: WirePreview | null): void;
  setArmedPart(part: PartTemplate | null): void;
  setFocusWire(id: string | null): void;
  setPreviewWires(wires: Wire[]): void;
}

export type EditorStore = EditorState & EditorActions;

/** 狀態列與即時檢查用的分析結果。 */
export function analyzeCircuit(circuit: Circuit): Analysis {
  const index = indexCircuit(circuit);
  const nets = extractNetsWith(index, circuit.wires);
  return {
    liveCheck: validateCircuit(circuit),
    wireCount: circuit.wires.length,
    netCount: nets.filter((n) => terminalClusters(n, index).length >= 2).length,
    score: scoreRouting(circuit).score,
  };
}

export function initialEditorState(doc: ProjectFile = createProject(), projectId: string | null = null): EditorState {
  return {
    doc,
    history: { past: [], future: [] },
    dirty: false,
    projectId,
    mode: 'edit',
    selection: null,
    wireColor: DEFAULT_WIRE_COLOR,
    viewport: { scale: 1, tx: 0, ty: 0 },
    analysis: analyzeCircuit(doc.circuit),
    rightTab: 'selection',
    routingJob: { status: 'idle' },
    compareView: 'ai',
    notice: null,
    checkPassedAt: null,
    ghost: null,
    wirePreview: null,
    armedPart: null,
    focusWireId: null,
    previewWires: [],
  };
}

function sanitizeSelection(selection: Selection | null, circuit: Circuit): Selection | null {
  if (!selection) return null;
  switch (selection.kind) {
    case 'component':
      return circuit.components.some((c) => c.id === selection.id) ? selection : null;
    case 'pin':
      return circuit.components.some((c) => c.id === selection.componentId) ? selection : null;
    case 'wire':
      return circuit.wires.some((w) => w.id === selection.id) ? selection : null;
    case 'hole':
      return selection;
  }
}

let noticeCounter = 0;

const editorStateCreator: StateCreator<EditorStore> = (set, get) => ({
  ...initialEditorState(),

  run(command) {
    const state = get();
    if (state.mode !== 'edit') return false;
    const result = command(state.doc);
    if (!result.ok) {
      get().notify(result.reason, 'error');
      return false;
    }
    const snapshot: Snapshot = { circuit: state.doc.circuit, routing: state.doc.routing };
    set({
      doc: result.doc,
      history: { past: [...state.history.past, snapshot].slice(-HISTORY_LIMIT), future: [] },
      dirty: true,
      selection: result.select === undefined ? sanitizeSelection(state.selection, result.doc.circuit) : result.select,
    });
    return true;
  },

  undo() {
    const { mode, history, doc, selection } = get();
    if (mode !== 'edit' || history.past.length === 0) return;
    const previous = history.past[history.past.length - 1];
    const nextDoc: ProjectFile = { ...doc, circuit: previous.circuit, routing: previous.routing };
    set({
      doc: nextDoc,
      history: { past: history.past.slice(0, -1), future: [{ circuit: doc.circuit, routing: doc.routing }, ...history.future] },
      dirty: true,
      selection: sanitizeSelection(selection, nextDoc.circuit),
      routingJob: { status: 'idle' },
      previewWires: [],
    });
  },

  redo() {
    const { mode, history, doc, selection } = get();
    if (mode !== 'edit' || history.future.length === 0) return;
    const [next, ...rest] = history.future;
    const nextDoc: ProjectFile = { ...doc, circuit: next.circuit, routing: next.routing };
    set({
      doc: nextDoc,
      history: { past: [...history.past, { circuit: doc.circuit, routing: doc.routing }].slice(-HISTORY_LIMIT), future: rest },
      dirty: true,
      selection: sanitizeSelection(selection, nextDoc.circuit),
      routingJob: { status: 'idle' },
      previewWires: [],
    });
  },

  replaceDocument(doc, { projectId, undoable, dirty }) {
    const state = get();
    const history = undoable
      ? { past: [...state.history.past, { circuit: state.doc.circuit, routing: state.doc.routing }].slice(-HISTORY_LIMIT), future: [] }
      : { past: [], future: [] };
    set({
      doc,
      projectId,
      dirty,
      history,
      selection: null,
      mode: 'edit',
      routingJob: { status: 'idle' },
      previewWires: [],
      ghost: null,
      wirePreview: null,
      armedPart: null,
      focusWireId: null,
      analysis: analyzeCircuit(doc.circuit),
    });
  },

  markSaved(projectId, doc) {
    set({ projectId, doc, dirty: false });
  },

  setChecklistItem(wireId, done) {
    const doc = setChecklistItemDoc(get().doc, wireId, done);
    if (doc !== get().doc) set({ doc, dirty: true });
  },

  clearChecklist() {
    const doc = clearChecklistDoc(get().doc);
    if (doc !== get().doc) set({ doc, dirty: true });
  },

  select: (selection) => set({ selection }),
  setMode: (mode) => set({ mode }),
  setWireColor: (wireColor) => set({ wireColor }),
  setViewport: (viewport) => set({ viewport }),
  setRightTab: (rightTab) => set({ rightTab }),
  setCompareView: (compareView) => set({ compareView }),
  setRoutingJob: (routingJob) => set({ routingJob }),

  notify(text, tone = 'info') {
    noticeCounter += 1;
    set({ notice: { id: noticeCounter, text, tone } });
  },

  dismissNotice: () => set({ notice: null }),

  refreshAnalysis() {
    set({ analysis: analyzeCircuit(get().doc.circuit) });
  },

  runCheck() {
    const analysis = analyzeCircuit(get().doc.circuit);
    const passed = analysis.liveCheck.every((e) => e.severity === 'info');
    set({ analysis, rightTab: 'check', checkPassedAt: passed ? Date.now() : null });
  },

  setGhost: (ghost) => set({ ghost }),
  setWirePreview: (wirePreview) => set({ wirePreview }),
  setArmedPart: (armedPart) => set({ armedPart }),
  setFocusWire: (focusWireId) => set({ focusWireId }),
  setPreviewWires: (previewWires) => set({ previewWires }),
});

export const createEditorStore = () => createStore<EditorStore>()(editorStateCreator);

export const useEditor = create<EditorStore>()(editorStateCreator);

export function resetEditor(doc: ProjectFile = createProject(), projectId: string | null = null): void {
  useEditor.setState(initialEditorState(doc, projectId));
}
```

### Task 7: 存檔：解析檔案與瀏覽器儲存

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/persistence/projectIO.ts`、`src/app/persistence/browserStorage.ts`
- Test: `tests/app/persistence.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 的 `ProjectFile`、`RoutingRecord`、`PROJECT_FORMAT`、`DEFAULT_PROJECT_NAME`、`createProject`；`getComponentDefinition`
- Produces:
  - `ParseResult = { ok: true; doc } | { ok: false; reason }`、`circuitProblem(value): string | null`
  - `parseProjectFile(text, fallbackName?): ParseResult`（接受 `ProjectFile` 或只有 `Circuit` 的 JSON；格式錯的 `routing` 會被丟掉，不算失敗）
  - `serializeProject(doc): string`、`safeFileName(name): string`、`projectFileName(doc, 'breadboard.json' | 'svg' | 'png'): string`
  - `KeyValueStore`、`STORAGE_KEYS`、`STORAGE_FULL`、`STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE`、`StorageResult<T>`、`ProjectEntry { id; name; savedAt }`
  - `getBrowserStore(): KeyValueStore | null`
  - `writeAutosave(store, doc, projectId)`、`readAutosave(store): { doc; projectId } | null`、`restoreAutosave(store): { doc; projectId }`
  - `listProjects(store): ProjectEntry[]`（依 `savedAt` 新到舊）、`saveProject(store, doc, projectId, now?, makeId?)`、`openProject(store, id)`、`deleteProject(store, id)`、`newProjectId(now?, random?)`
  - 所有函式在 `store` 為 `null` 或丟例外時回傳中文錯誤，不丟例外

- [ ] **Step 1: 寫失敗的測試**

`tests/app/persistence.test.ts`：

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createProject } from '../../src/app/model/projectFile';
import {
  deleteProject,
  listProjects,
  newProjectId,
  openProject,
  readAutosave,
  restoreAutosave,
  saveProject,
  STORAGE_FULL,
  STORAGE_KEYS,
  STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE,
  writeAutosave,
  type KeyValueStore,
} from '../../src/app/persistence/browserStorage';
import { parseProjectFile, projectFileName, safeFileName, serializeProject } from '../../src/app/persistence/projectIO';
import { acceptanceCircuit } from '../fixtures/circuits';

class MemoryStore implements KeyValueStore {
  readonly data = new Map<string, string>();
  getItem(key: string): string | null {
    return this.data.get(key) ?? null;
  }
  setItem(key: string, value: string): void {
    this.data.set(key, value);
  }
  removeItem(key: string): void {
    this.data.delete(key);
  }
}

class ThrowingStore implements KeyValueStore {
  getItem(): string | null {
    throw new Error('SecurityError');
  }
  setItem(): void {
    throw new Error('SecurityError');
  }
  removeItem(): void {
    throw new Error('SecurityError');
  }
}

class FullStore extends MemoryStore {
  setItem(): void {
    throw Object.assign(new Error('full'), { name: 'QuotaExceededError' });
  }
}

const doc = () => ({ ...createProject('我的電路', new Date('2026-10-06T00:00:00Z')), circuit: acceptanceCircuit() });

describe('parseProjectFile', () => {
  it('round-trips a project file', () => {
    const original = doc();
    expect(parseProjectFile(serializeProject(original))).toEqual({ ok: true, doc: original });
  });

  it('wraps a bare circuit and names it after the file', () => {
    const parsed = parseProjectFile(JSON.stringify(acceptanceCircuit()), '驗收');
    expect(parsed.ok && parsed.doc.name).toBe('驗收');
    expect(parsed.ok && parsed.doc.circuit).toEqual(acceptanceCircuit());
  });

  it('explains the first problem in Chinese', () => {
    const c = acceptanceCircuit();
    const first = c.components[0];
    expect(parseProjectFile('{oops')).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '檔案不是有效的 JSON' });
    expect(parseProjectFile('[]')).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '檔案內容不是 breadboard-lab 的電路' });
    expect(parseProjectFile(JSON.stringify({ ...doc(), version: 2 }))).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '不支援的檔案版本' });
    expect(parseProjectFile(JSON.stringify({ ...c, boards: [...c.boards, { ...c.boards[0], id: 'b2' }] }))).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      reason: '目前只支援一塊 MB-102 麵包板',
    });
    expect(parseProjectFile(JSON.stringify({ ...c, components: [{ ...first, defId: '74LS999' }] }))).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      reason: '找不到元件「74LS999」',
    });
    expect(parseProjectFile(JSON.stringify({ ...c, components: [{ ...first, rotation: 45 }] }))).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      reason: `元件 ${first.id} 的旋轉角度不正確`,
    });
    expect(parseProjectFile(JSON.stringify({ ...c, components: [first, first] }))).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: `元件 ID 重複：${first.id}` });
    expect(parseProjectFile(JSON.stringify({ ...c, wires: [{ ...c.wires[0], path: [{ x: 1.5, y: 2 }] }] }))).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      reason: `導線 ${c.wires[0].id} 的座標不正確`,
    });
  });

  it('drops a malformed routing record instead of failing', () => {
    const parsed = parseProjectFile(JSON.stringify({ ...doc(), routing: { before: 'x' } }));
    expect(parsed.ok && parsed.doc.routing).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('file names', () => {
  it('replaces characters that are not allowed in file names', () => {
    expect(safeFileName('a/b\\c:d*e?f"g<h>i|j')).toBe('a_b_c_d_e_f_g_h_i_j');
    expect(safeFileName('   ')).toBe('未命名電路');
    expect(projectFileName(createProject('實驗 1/2'), 'breadboard.json')).toBe('實驗 1_2.breadboard.json');
    expect(projectFileName(createProject('板子'), 'png')).toBe('板子.png');
  });
});

describe('browser storage', () => {
  it('writes and restores the autosave', () => {
    const store = new MemoryStore();
    expect(writeAutosave(store, doc(), 'p_1')).toEqual({ ok: true, value: undefined });
    expect(readAutosave(store)).toEqual({ doc: doc(), projectId: 'p_1' });
  });

  it('falls back to a blank circuit when the autosave is corrupted', () => {
    const store = new MemoryStore();
    store.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.autosave, '{"project":{"format":"breadboard-lab","version":7}}');
    expect(readAutosave(store)).toBeNull();
    expect(restoreAutosave(store).doc.circuit.components).toEqual([]);
    store.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.autosave, 'not json');
    expect(readAutosave(store)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('keeps working when storage is unavailable or full', () => {
    expect(writeAutosave(new ThrowingStore(), doc(), null)).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE });
    expect(writeAutosave(null, doc(), null)).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE });
    expect(readAutosave(new ThrowingStore())).toBeNull();
    expect(listProjects(new ThrowingStore())).toEqual([]);
    expect(saveProject(new FullStore(), doc(), null)).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: STORAGE_FULL });
  });

  it('saves, lists, opens and deletes projects', () => {
    const store = new MemoryStore();
    const first = saveProject(store, doc(), null, new Date('2026-10-06T01:00:00Z'), () => 'p_a');
    expect(first.ok && first.value.projectId).toBe('p_a');
    saveProject(store, { ...doc(), name: '第二個' }, null, new Date('2026-10-06T02:00:00Z'), () => 'p_b');
    saveProject(store, { ...doc(), name: '改名了' }, 'p_a', new Date('2026-10-06T03:00:00Z'));
    expect(listProjects(store)).toEqual([
      { id: 'p_a', name: '改名了', savedAt: '2026-10-06T03:00:00.000Z' },
      { id: 'p_b', name: '第二個', savedAt: '2026-10-06T02:00:00.000Z' },
    ]);
    const opened = openProject(store, 'p_b');
    expect(opened.ok && opened.value.name).toBe('第二個');
    expect(deleteProject(store, 'p_b')).toEqual({ ok: true, value: undefined });
    expect(listProjects(store).map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(['p_a']);
    expect(openProject(store, 'p_b')).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: '找不到這個專案' });
  });

  it('makes readable project ids', () => {
    expect(newProjectId(new Date(0), () => 0)).toBe('p_0_0000');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 實作**

`src/app/persistence/projectIO.ts`：

```ts
import { getComponentDefinition } from '../../core/circuit/library';
import type { Circuit } from '../../types/circuit';
import { DEFAULT_PROJECT_NAME, PROJECT_FORMAT, type ProjectFile, type RoutingRecord } from '../model/projectFile';

export type ParseResult = { ok: true; doc: ProjectFile } | { ok: false; reason: string };

type Obj = Record<string, unknown>;
const isObject = (v: unknown): v is Obj => typeof v === 'object' && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v);
const isInt = (v: unknown): v is number => typeof v === 'number' && Number.isInteger(v);
const isPoint = (v: unknown): boolean => isObject(v) && typeof v.boardId === 'string' && isInt(v.x) && isInt(v.y);
const ROTATIONS = new Set<unknown>([0, 90, 180, 270]);
const METRIC_KEYS = ['wireCount', 'totalLength', 'bends', 'crossings', 'overlaps', 'shortJogs'];

function wireProblem(w: unknown, index: number): string | null {
  if (!isObject(w) || typeof w.id !== 'string' || typeof w.color !== 'string') return `第 ${index + 1} 條導線的資料不完整`;
  if (w.source !== 'manual' && w.source !== 'auto') return `導線 ${w.id} 的來源不正確`;
  if (!isPoint(w.start) || !isPoint(w.end) || !Array.isArray(w.path) || !w.path.every(isPoint)) return `導線 ${w.id} 的座標不正確`;
  return null;
}

/** 回傳電路資料的第一個問題（中文），沒有問題時回傳 null。 */
export function circuitProblem(value: unknown): string | null {
  if (!isObject(value)) return '檔案裡沒有電路資料';
  if (value.version !== 1) return '不支援的電路版本';
  const boards = value.boards;
  if (!Array.isArray(boards) || boards.length !== 1 || !isObject(boards[0]) || boards[0].layoutId !== 'MB102' || typeof boards[0].id !== 'string') {
    return '目前只支援一塊 MB-102 麵包板';
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(value.components)) return '元件資料不正確';
  const componentIds = new Set<string>();
  for (const [i, c] of value.components.entries()) {
    if (!isObject(c) || typeof c.id !== 'string' || typeof c.defId !== 'string' || typeof c.boardId !== 'string') {
      return `第 ${i + 1} 個元件的資料不完整`;
    }
    if (!getComponentDefinition(c.defId)) return `找不到元件「${c.defId}」`;
    if (!isObject(c.anchor) || !isInt(c.anchor.x) || !isInt(c.anchor.y)) return `元件 ${c.id} 的位置不正確`;
    if (!ROTATIONS.has(c.rotation)) return `元件 ${c.id} 的旋轉角度不正確`;
    if (c.span !== undefined && !isInt(c.span)) return `元件 ${c.id} 的兩腳距離不正確`;
    if (!isObject(c.props)) return `元件 ${c.id} 的屬性不正確`;
    if (componentIds.has(c.id)) return `元件 ID 重複：${c.id}`;
    componentIds.add(c.id);
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(value.wires)) return '導線資料不正確';
  const wireIds = new Set<string>();
  for (const [i, w] of value.wires.entries()) {
    const problem = wireProblem(w, i);
    if (problem) return problem;
    const id = (w as Obj).id as string;
    if (wireIds.has(id)) return `導線 ID 重複：${id}`;
    wireIds.add(id);
  }
  return null;
}

function parseRouting(value: unknown): RoutingRecord | null {
  if (!isObject(value)) return null;
  const { before, beforeScore, metrics, score, signature, checklist } = value;
  if (!Array.isArray(before) || before.some((w, i) => wireProblem(w, i) !== null)) return null;
  if (typeof beforeScore !== 'number' || typeof score !== 'number' || typeof signature !== 'string') return null;
  if (!isObject(metrics) || !METRIC_KEYS.every((k) => typeof metrics[k] === 'number')) return null;
  if (!isObject(checklist) || !Object.values(checklist).every((v) => typeof v === 'boolean')) return null;
  return value as unknown as RoutingRecord;
}

export function parseProjectFile(text: string, fallbackName: string = DEFAULT_PROJECT_NAME): ParseResult {
  let data: unknown;
  try {
    data = JSON.parse(text);
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, reason: '檔案不是有效的 JSON' };
  }
  if (!isObject(data)) return { ok: false, reason: '檔案內容不是 breadboard-lab 的電路' };
  if (data.format === PROJECT_FORMAT) {
    if (data.version !== 1) return { ok: false, reason: '不支援的檔案版本' };
    const problem = circuitProblem(data.circuit);
    if (problem) return { ok: false, reason: problem };
    const name = typeof data.name === 'string' && data.name.trim() !== '' ? data.name : fallbackName;
    return {
      ok: true,
      doc: {
        format: PROJECT_FORMAT,
        version: 1,
        name,
        circuit: data.circuit as Circuit,
        routing: parseRouting(data.routing),
        savedAt: typeof data.savedAt === 'string' ? data.savedAt : new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    };
  }
  if ('components' in data && 'wires' in data) {
    const problem = circuitProblem(data);
    if (problem) return { ok: false, reason: problem };
    return {
      ok: true,
      doc: { format: PROJECT_FORMAT, version: 1, name: fallbackName, circuit: data as unknown as Circuit, routing: null, savedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
    };
  }
  return { ok: false, reason: '檔案內容不是 breadboard-lab 的電路' };
}

export function serializeProject(doc: ProjectFile): string {
  return `${JSON.stringify(doc, null, 2)}\n`;
}

/** 把檔名不允許的字元換成 _；空白名稱改用預設名稱。 */
export function safeFileName(name: string): string {
  const cleaned = name.replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g, '_').trim();
  return cleaned === '' ? DEFAULT_PROJECT_NAME : cleaned;
}

export function projectFileName(doc: ProjectFile, extension: 'breadboard.json' | 'svg' | 'png'): string {
  return `${safeFileName(doc.name)}.${extension}`;
}
```

`src/app/persistence/browserStorage.ts`：

```ts
import { createProject, type ProjectFile } from '../model/projectFile';
import { parseProjectFile } from './projectIO';

export interface KeyValueStore {
  getItem(key: string): string | null;
  setItem(key: string, value: string): void;
  removeItem(key: string): void;
}

export const STORAGE_KEYS = {
  autosave: 'breadboard-lab:autosave',
  projects: 'breadboard-lab:projects',
  project: (id: string) => `breadboard-lab:project:${id}`,
};

export const STORAGE_FULL = '瀏覽器儲存空間不足，請匯出 JSON 保存';
export const STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE = '無法使用瀏覽器儲存（可能是隱私模式），請匯出 JSON 保存';

export type StorageResult<T> = { ok: true; value: T } | { ok: false; reason: string };

export interface ProjectEntry {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  savedAt: string;
}

/** 取得瀏覽器的 localStorage；被封鎖或不存在時回傳 null。 */
export function getBrowserStore(): KeyValueStore | null {
  try {
    const store = globalThis.localStorage;
    if (!store) return null;
    store.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.autosave);
    return store;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function isQuotaError(error: unknown): boolean {
  return error instanceof Error && (error.name === 'QuotaExceededError' || error.name === 'NS_ERROR_DOM_QUOTA_REACHED');
}

function write(store: KeyValueStore | null, key: string, value: string): StorageResult<void> {
  if (!store) return { ok: false, reason: STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE };
  try {
    store.setItem(key, value);
    return { ok: true, value: undefined };
  } catch (error) {
    return { ok: false, reason: isQuotaError(error) ? STORAGE_FULL : STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE };
  }
}

function read(store: KeyValueStore | null, key: string): string | null {
  if (!store) return null;
  try {
    return store.getItem(key);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function writeAutosave(store: KeyValueStore | null, doc: ProjectFile, projectId: string | null): StorageResult<void> {
  return write(store, STORAGE_KEYS.autosave, JSON.stringify({ projectId, project: doc }));
}

export function readAutosave(store: KeyValueStore | null): { doc: ProjectFile; projectId: string | null } | null {
  const text = read(store, STORAGE_KEYS.autosave);
  if (text === null) return null;
  try {
    const data = JSON.parse(text) as { projectId?: unknown; project?: unknown };
    const parsed = parseProjectFile(JSON.stringify(data.project));
    if (!parsed.ok) return null;
    return { doc: parsed.doc, projectId: typeof data.projectId === 'string' ? data.projectId : null };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/** 啟動時使用：有可用的自動存檔就載入，否則給一個空白電路。 */
export function restoreAutosave(store: KeyValueStore | null): { doc: ProjectFile; projectId: string | null } {
  return readAutosave(store) ?? { doc: createProject(), projectId: null };
}

export function listProjects(store: KeyValueStore | null): ProjectEntry[] {
  const text = read(store, STORAGE_KEYS.projects);
  if (!text) return [];
  try {
    const data: unknown = JSON.parse(text);
    if (!Array.isArray(data)) return [];
    return data.filter(
      (e): e is ProjectEntry =>
        typeof e === 'object' && e !== null && typeof e.id === 'string' && typeof e.name === 'string' && typeof e.savedAt === 'string',
    );
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export function newProjectId(now: Date = new Date(), random: () => number = Math.random): string {
  return `p_${now.getTime().toString(36)}_${Math.floor(random() * 36 ** 4).toString(36).padStart(4, '0')}`;
}

export function saveProject(
  store: KeyValueStore | null,
  doc: ProjectFile,
  projectId: string | null,
  now: Date = new Date(),
  makeId: () => string = () => newProjectId(now),
): StorageResult<{ projectId: string; doc: ProjectFile }> {
  const id = projectId ?? makeId();
  const saved: ProjectFile = { ...doc, savedAt: now.toISOString() };
  const written = write(store, STORAGE_KEYS.project(id), JSON.stringify(saved));
  if (!written.ok) return written;
  const entries = listProjects(store).filter((e) => e.id !== id);
  entries.push({ id, name: saved.name, savedAt: saved.savedAt });
  entries.sort((a, b) => (a.savedAt < b.savedAt ? 1 : a.savedAt > b.savedAt ? -1 : 0));
  const indexed = write(store, STORAGE_KEYS.projects, JSON.stringify(entries));
  if (!indexed.ok) return indexed;
  return { ok: true, value: { projectId: id, doc: saved } };
}

export function openProject(store: KeyValueStore | null, id: string): StorageResult<ProjectFile> {
  const text = read(store, STORAGE_KEYS.project(id));
  if (text === null) return { ok: false, reason: store ? '找不到這個專案' : STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE };
  const parsed = parseProjectFile(text);
  return parsed.ok ? { ok: true, value: parsed.doc } : { ok: false, reason: `專案已損毀：${parsed.reason}` };
}

export function deleteProject(store: KeyValueStore | null, id: string): StorageResult<void> {
  if (!store) return { ok: false, reason: STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE };
  try {
    store.removeItem(STORAGE_KEYS.project(id));
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, reason: STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE };
  }
  return write(store, STORAGE_KEYS.projects, JSON.stringify(listProjects(store).filter((e) => e.id !== id)));
}
```
