```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createProject } from '../../src/app/model/projectFile';
import { animationStepMs, checklistOrder } from '../../src/app/routing/checklist';
import { RoutingCancelled, RoutingClient, type WorkerLike } from '../../src/app/routing/routingClient';
import { runAutoRoute, type Router } from '../../src/app/routing/runAutoRoute';
import { createEditorStore } from '../../src/app/store/editorStore';
import { applyRouting } from '../../src/core/routing/apply';
import { autoRoute } from '../../src/core/routing/autoRoute';
import type { RoutingResult } from '../../src/types/routing';
import { handleRouteRequest } from '../../src/workers/routingProtocol';
import { circuit, wire } from '../fixtures/build';
import { acceptanceCircuit } from '../fixtures/circuits';

class FakeWorker implements WorkerLike {
  onmessage: ((event: { data: unknown }) => void) | null = null;
  readonly sent: unknown[] = [];
  terminated = false;
  postMessage(message: unknown): void {
    this.sent.push(message);
  }
  terminate(): void {
    this.terminated = true;
  }
  reply(data: unknown): void {
    this.onmessage?.({ data });
  }
}

const jobIdOf = (worker: FakeWorker) => (worker.sent[worker.sent.length - 1] as { jobId: number }).jobId;
const HOLE = /^([A-J]|[TB][+-])\d+$/;

describe('handleRouteRequest', () => {
  it('returns the routing result for the job', () => {
    const response = handleRouteRequest({ type: 'route', jobId: 7, circuit: acceptanceCircuit() });
    expect(response.jobId).toBe(7);
    expect(response.type === 'result' && response.result.status).toBe('success');
  });

  it('turns exceptions into error messages', () => {
    const c = acceptanceCircuit();
    const response = handleRouteRequest({ type: 'route', jobId: 8, circuit: { ...c, boards: [...c.boards, { ...c.boards[0], id: 'b2' }] } });
    expect(response).toEqual({ type: 'error', jobId: 8, message: 'autoRoute currently supports exactly one breadboard' });
  });
});

describe('RoutingClient', () => {
  it('resolves the matching job and ignores stale replies', async () => {
    const worker = new FakeWorker();
    const client = new RoutingClient(() => worker);
    const promise = client.route(acceptanceCircuit());
    const result = autoRoute(acceptanceCircuit());
    worker.reply({ type: 'result', jobId: jobIdOf(worker) + 100, result });
    worker.reply({ type: 'result', jobId: jobIdOf(worker), result });
    await expect(promise).resolves.toBe(result);
  });

  it('rejects with the worker error message', async () => {
    const worker = new FakeWorker();
    const client = new RoutingClient(() => worker);
    const promise = client.route(acceptanceCircuit());
    worker.reply({ type: 'error', jobId: jobIdOf(worker), message: 'boom' });
    await expect(promise).rejects.toThrow('boom');
  });

  it('cancels by terminating the worker and starts a fresh one next time', async () => {
    const workers: FakeWorker[] = [];
    const client = new RoutingClient(() => {
      const w = new FakeWorker();
      workers.push(w);
      return w;
    });
    const promise = client.route(acceptanceCircuit());
    client.cancel();
    await expect(promise).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(RoutingCancelled);
    expect(workers[0].terminated).toBe(true);
    client.route(acceptanceCircuit()).catch(() => undefined);
    expect(workers).toHaveLength(2);
  });
});

describe('checklistOrder', () => {
  it('lists power wires first, then ground, then signals in natural order', () => {
    const routed = applyRouting(acceptanceCircuit(), autoRoute(acceptanceCircuit()));
    const rows = checklistOrder(routed);
    expect(rows.map((r) => r.kind)).toEqual(['power', 'ground', 'ground', 'ground', 'ground', 'signal', 'signal']);
    expect(rows[0]).toMatchObject({ colorName: '紅', netId: 'VCC' });
    expect(rows[1]).toMatchObject({ colorName: '黑', netId: 'GND' });
    expect(rows.slice(5).map((r) => r.netId)).toEqual(['NET_1', 'NET_2']);
    expect(rows.every((r) => HOLE.test(r.from) && HOLE.test(r.to) && r.length >= 1)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('ignores manual wires', () => {
    expect(checklistOrder(circuit([], [wire('m1', 'A10', 'A20')]))).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('speeds up the animation for many wires', () => {
    expect(animationStepMs(7)).toBe(250);
    expect(animationStepMs(40)).toBe(100);
    expect(animationStepMs(0)).toBe(250);
  });
});

describe('runAutoRoute', () => {
  const setup = () => {
    const store = createEditorStore();
    store.getState().replaceDocument({ ...createProject(), circuit: acceptanceCircuit() }, { projectId: null, undoable: false, dirty: false });
    return store;
  };

  it('applies a successful result, starts the animation and shows the checklist', async () => {
    const store = setup();
    await runAutoRoute(store, { route: async (c) => autoRoute(c) });
    const s = store.getState();
    expect(s.mode).toBe('edit');
    expect(s.doc.routing).not.toBeNull();
    expect(s.routingJob).toMatchObject({ status: 'animating', stepMs: 250 });
    expect(s.routingJob.status === 'animating' && s.routingJob.wireIds).toHaveLength(7);
    expect(s.rightTab).toBe('checklist');
    s.undo();
    expect(store.getState().doc.circuit.wires.map((w) => w.id)).toEqual(acceptanceCircuit().wires.map((w) => w.id));
  });

  it('keeps the document and shows a preview when routing fails', async () => {
    const store = setup();
    const full = autoRoute(acceptanceCircuit());
    const failedNets = [{ netId: 'NET_1', reason: 'UNROUTABLE' as const, detail: 'x' }];
    const partial: RoutingResult = { ...full, status: 'partial', message: 'No valid routing solution.', failedNets, wires: full.wires.slice(0, 2) };
    await runAutoRoute(store, { route: async () => partial });
    const s = store.getState();
    expect(s.doc.circuit.wires.every((w) => w.source === 'manual')).toBe(true);
    expect(s.previewWires).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(s.routingJob).toEqual({ status: 'failed', message: 'No valid routing solution.', failedNets });
  });

  it('tells the user when there is nothing to route', async () => {
    const store = createEditorStore();
    await runAutoRoute(store, { route: async (c) => autoRoute(c) });
    expect(store.getState().notice?.text).toBe('目前沒有需要接線的 Net');
    expect(store.getState().routingJob).toEqual({ status: 'idle' });
  });

  it('ignores undo and edits while the router is still working', async () => {
    const store = setup();
    let finish: (result: RoutingResult) => void = () => undefined;
    const router: Router = { route: () => new Promise<RoutingResult>((resolve) => (finish = resolve)) };
    const pending = runAutoRoute(store, router);
    expect(store.getState().mode).toBe('routing');
    store.getState().undo();
    expect(store.getState().run((doc) => ({ ok: true, doc: { ...doc, name: 'x' } }))).toBe(false);
    finish(autoRoute(acceptanceCircuit()));
    await pending;
    expect(store.getState().doc.name).toBe('未命名電路');
    expect(store.getState().doc.routing).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it('returns to editing quietly when cancelled, and reports worker errors', async () => {
    const store = setup();
    await runAutoRoute(store, {
      route: async () => {
        throw new RoutingCancelled();
      },
    });
    expect(store.getState()).toMatchObject({ mode: 'edit', routingJob: { status: 'idle' } });
    await runAutoRoute(store, {
      route: async () => {
        throw new Error('boom');
      },
    });
    expect(store.getState().routingJob).toEqual({ status: 'error', message: '佈線時發生錯誤：boom' });
  });
});
```

`src/workers/routingProtocol.ts`：

```ts
import { autoRoute } from '../core/routing/autoRoute';
import type { Circuit } from '../types/circuit';
import type { RoutingResult } from '../types/routing';

export interface RouteRequest {
  type: 'route';
  jobId: number;
  circuit: Circuit;
}

export type RouteResponse =
  | { type: 'result'; jobId: number; result: RoutingResult }
  | { type: 'error'; jobId: number; message: string };

export function handleRouteRequest(request: RouteRequest): RouteResponse {
  try {
    return { type: 'result', jobId: request.jobId, result: autoRoute(request.circuit) };
  } catch (error) {
    return { type: 'error', jobId: request.jobId, message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) };
  }
}
```

```ts
  onmessage: ((event: MessageEvent<RouteRequest>) => void) | null;
  postMessage(message: unknown): void;
};

scope.onmessage = (event) => {

};
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 實作主執行緒端**

`src/app/routing/routingClient.ts`：

```ts
import type { Circuit } from '../../types/circuit';
import type { RoutingResult } from '../../types/routing';
import type { RouteRequest, RouteResponse } from '../../workers/routingProtocol';

export interface WorkerLike {
  postMessage(message: unknown): void;
  terminate(): void;
  onmessage: ((event: { data: unknown }) => void) | null;
}

export class RoutingCancelled extends Error {
  constructor() {
    super('cancelled');
    this.name = 'RoutingCancelled';
  }
}

interface PendingJob {
  jobId: number;
  resolve: (result: RoutingResult) => void;
  reject: (error: Error) => void;
}

/** 一次只跑一個佈線工作；取消時終止 Worker，下次再建立新的。 */
export class RoutingClient {
  private worker: WorkerLike | null = null;
  private nextJobId = 1;
  private pending: PendingJob | null = null;

  constructor(private readonly createWorker: () => WorkerLike) {}

  route(circuit: Circuit): Promise<RoutingResult> {
    this.cancel();
    const worker = this.ensureWorker();
    const jobId = this.nextJobId++;
    return new Promise<RoutingResult>((resolve, reject) => {
      this.pending = { jobId, resolve, reject };
      const request: RouteRequest = { type: 'route', jobId, circuit };
      worker.postMessage(request);
    });
  }

  cancel(): void {
    const pending = this.pending;
    if (!pending) return;
    this.pending = null;
    this.worker?.terminate();
    this.worker = null;
    pending.reject(new RoutingCancelled());
  }

  private ensureWorker(): WorkerLike {
    if (!this.worker) {
      const worker = this.createWorker();
      worker.onmessage = (event) => this.receive(event.data as RouteResponse);
      this.worker = worker;
    }
    return this.worker;
  }

  private receive(response: RouteResponse): void {
    const pending = this.pending;
    if (!pending || response.jobId !== pending.jobId) return;
    this.pending = null;
    if (response.type === 'result') pending.resolve(response.result);
    else pending.reject(new Error(response.message));
  }
}
```

`src/app/routing/checklist.ts`：

```ts
import { holeName } from '../../core/breadboard/naming';
import { getBoard } from '../../core/breadboard/registry';
import { extractNets } from '../../core/circuit/nets';
import type { Circuit, Net } from '../../types/circuit';
import type { GridPoint } from '../../types/geometry';
import { colorName } from '../model/colors';
import { naturalCompare } from '../model/naturalCompare';

export interface ChecklistRow {
  wireId: string;
  color: string;
  colorName: string;
  from: string;
  to: string;
  length: number;
  netId: string;
  kind: Net['kind'];
}

const KIND_RANK: Record<Net['kind'], number> = { power: 0, ground: 1, signal: 2 };

/** 規格第 9 節：只列 AI 佈線產生的導線；VCC → GND → 訊號，同類依 Net 名稱、導線 ID 的自然順序。 */
export function checklistOrder(circuit: Circuit): ChecklistRow[] {
  const boardInstance = circuit.boards[0];
  const board = boardInstance ? getBoard(boardInstance.layoutId, boardInstance.id) : null;
  const name = (p: GridPoint) => (board ? holeName(board, p) : null) ?? `(${p.x}, ${p.y})`;
  const kindOf = new Map(extractNets(circuit).map((n) => [n.id, n.kind] as const));
  return circuit.wires
    .filter((w) => w.source === 'auto' && w.netId !== undefined)
    .map((w) => ({
      wireId: w.id,
      color: w.color,
      colorName: colorName(w.color),
      from: name(w.start),
      to: name(w.end),
      length: w.path.length - 1,
      netId: w.netId!,
      kind: kindOf.get(w.netId!) ?? 'signal',
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => KIND_RANK[a.kind] - KIND_RANK[b.kind] || naturalCompare(a.netId, b.netId) || naturalCompare(a.wireId, b.wireId));
}

/** 每條導線的動畫時間：最多 250 ms，整段最多約 4 秒。 */
export function animationStepMs(count: number): number {
  return Math.min(250, 4000 / Math.max(1, count));
}
```

`src/app/routing/runAutoRoute.ts`：

```ts
import type { StoreApi } from 'zustand/vanilla';
import { scoreRouting } from '../../core/routing/score';
import type { Circuit } from '../../types/circuit';
import type { RoutingResult } from '../../types/routing';
import { applyRoutingResult } from '../commands/commands';
import type { EditorStore } from '../store/editorStore';
import { animationStepMs, checklistOrder } from './checklist';
import { RoutingCancelled } from './routingClient';

export interface Router {
  route(circuit: Circuit): Promise<RoutingResult>;
}

/** 規格第 8.2 節：鎖住編輯 → 背景佈線 → 成功就套用並播放動畫，失敗就顯示預覽與原因。 */
export async function runAutoRoute(store: Pick<StoreApi<EditorStore>, 'getState'>, router: Router): Promise<void> {
  const start = store.getState();
  if (start.mode !== 'edit') return;
  const circuit = start.doc.circuit;
  const beforeScore = scoreRouting(circuit).score;
  start.setPreviewWires([]);
  start.select(null);
  start.setMode('routing');
  start.setRoutingJob({ status: 'running' });

  let result: RoutingResult;
  try {
    result = await router.route(circuit);
  } catch (error) {
    const s = store.getState();
    s.setMode('edit');
    if (error instanceof RoutingCancelled) s.setRoutingJob({ status: 'idle' });
    else s.setRoutingJob({ status: 'error', message: `佈線時發生錯誤：${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}` });
    return;
  }

  const s = store.getState();
  s.setMode('edit');
  if (result.status === 'success' && result.wires.length === 0) {
    s.setRoutingJob({ status: 'idle' });
    s.notify('目前沒有需要接線的 Net', 'info');
    return;
  }
  if (result.status !== 'success') {
    s.setPreviewWires(result.wires);
    s.setRoutingJob({ status: 'failed', message: result.message, failedNets: result.failedNets });
    return;
  }
  if (!s.run((doc) => applyRoutingResult(doc, result, beforeScore))) {
    s.setRoutingJob({ status: 'idle' });
    return;
  }
  const order = checklistOrder(store.getState().doc.circuit).map((row) => row.wireId);
  s.setRoutingJob({ status: 'animating', wireIds: order, stepMs: animationStepMs(order.length) });
  s.setRightTab('checklist');
}
```

```ts
import type { GridPoint } from '../../types/geometry';
import type { Viewport } from '../model/editorTypes';

export const UNIT = 10;
export const BOARD_WIDTH = 640;
export const BOARD_HEIGHT = 190;
export const MIN_SCALE = 0.5;
export const MAX_SCALE = 8;
export const WHEEL_FACTOR = 1.15;

export interface ScreenPoint {
  x: number;
  y: number;
}

export function clampScale(scale: number): number {
  return Math.min(MAX_SCALE, Math.max(MIN_SCALE, scale));
}

/** 讓整塊板子放進 width × height（留 5% 邊）並置中。 */
export function fitViewport(width: number, height: number): Viewport {
  const scale = clampScale(Math.min(width / BOARD_WIDTH, height / BOARD_HEIGHT) * 0.95);
  return { scale, tx: (width - BOARD_WIDTH * scale) / 2, ty: (height - BOARD_HEIGHT * scale) / 2 };
}

/** 以螢幕點 (sx, sy) 為中心縮放 factor 倍。 */
export function zoomAt(vp: Viewport, factor: number, sx: number, sy: number): Viewport {
  const scale = clampScale(vp.scale * factor);
  const k = scale / vp.scale;
  return { scale, tx: sx - (sx - vp.tx) * k, ty: sy - (sy - vp.ty) * k };
}

export function panBy(vp: Viewport, dx: number, dy: number): Viewport {
  return { ...vp, tx: vp.tx + dx, ty: vp.ty + dy };
}

/** 螢幕座標（相對於 SVG 左上角）→ 格子座標（小數）。 */
export function screenToLattice(vp: Viewport, sx: number, sy: number): { x: number; y: number } {
  return { x: (sx - vp.tx) / vp.scale / UNIT, y: (sy - vp.ty) / vp.scale / UNIT };
}

export function screenToGridPoint(vp: Viewport, sx: number, sy: number, boardId: string): GridPoint {
  const p = screenToLattice(vp, sx, sy);
  return { boardId, x: Math.round(p.x), y: Math.round(p.y) };
}

export function latticeToScreen(vp: Viewport, x: number, y: number): ScreenPoint {
  return { x: x * UNIT * vp.scale + vp.tx, y: y * UNIT * vp.scale + vp.ty };
}

/** 兩指手勢：依兩指距離的比例縮放，並跟著兩指中點平移。 */
export function pinchViewport(startVp: Viewport, start: [ScreenPoint, ScreenPoint], now: [ScreenPoint, ScreenPoint]): Viewport {
  const distance = (a: ScreenPoint, b: ScreenPoint) => Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y) || 1;
  const middle = (a: ScreenPoint, b: ScreenPoint) => ({ x: (a.x + b.x) / 2, y: (a.y + b.y) / 2 });
  const m0 = middle(start[0], start[1]);
  const m1 = middle(now[0], now[1]);
  const zoomed = zoomAt(startVp, distance(now[0], now[1]) / distance(start[0], start[1]), m0.x, m0.y);
  return panBy(zoomed, m1.x - m0.x, m1.y - m0.y);
}
```

`src/app/interaction/pressPlan.ts`：

```ts
import type { Hit } from '../geometry/hitTest';
import type { Selection } from '../model/editorTypes';

export const CLICK_TOLERANCE_PX = 5;
export const LONG_PRESS_MS = 500;

export type DragAction = 'move' | 'wire' | 'pan';

export interface PressPlan {
  click: Selection | null;
  drag: DragAction;
  componentId: string | null;
}

/** 規格第 6.2 節：按下的位置決定「點一下」與「拖曳」各做什麼。 */
export function planPress(hit: Hit, freeHole: boolean): PressPlan {
  switch (hit.kind) {
    case 'pin':
      return { click: { kind: 'pin', componentId: hit.componentId, pinNumber: hit.pinNumber }, drag: 'move', componentId: hit.componentId };
    case 'component':
      return { click: { kind: 'component', id: hit.componentId }, drag: 'move', componentId: hit.componentId };
    case 'wire':
      return { click: { kind: 'wire', id: hit.wireId }, drag: 'pan', componentId: null };
    case 'hole':
      return freeHole
        ? { click: { kind: 'hole', point: hit.point }, drag: 'wire', componentId: null }
        : { click: null, drag: 'pan', componentId: null };
    case 'empty':
      return { click: null, drag: 'pan', componentId: null };
  }
}

/** 唯讀時（佈線中、比較模式）拖曳一律變成平移；不可選取時點擊不做事。 */
export function restrictPlan(plan: PressPlan, editable: boolean, selectable: boolean): PressPlan {
  if (editable && selectable) return plan;
  return {
    click: selectable ? plan.click : null,
    drag: editable ? plan.drag : 'pan',
    componentId: editable ? plan.componentId : null,
  };
}

/** 指標第一次在某個軸向移動到半格以上時決定轉角方向：true = 先水平再垂直。 */
export function wireOrientation(dx: number, dy: number): boolean | null {
  const ax = Math.abs(dx);
  const ay = Math.abs(dy);
  if (ax < 0.5 && ay < 0.5) return null;
  return ax >= ay;
}
```

`src/app/interaction/shortcuts.ts`：

```ts
export type ShortcutAction = 'rotate' | 'delete' | 'undo' | 'redo' | 'save' | 'escape';

export interface KeyInput {
  key: string;
  ctrlKey: boolean;
  metaKey: boolean;
  shiftKey: boolean;
  altKey: boolean;
}

/** 規格第 6.5 節。輸入框中只有 Ctrl+S（⌘S）有效。 */
export function shortcutFor(input: KeyInput, inTextField: boolean): ShortcutAction | null {
  const mod = input.ctrlKey || input.metaKey;
  const key = input.key.toLowerCase();
  if (mod && key === 's') return 'save';
  if (inTextField) return null;
  if (mod && key === 'z') return input.shiftKey ? 'redo' : 'undo';
  if (mod && key === 'y') return 'redo';
  if (mod || input.altKey) return null;
  if (key === 'r') return 'rotate';
  if (key === 'delete' || key === 'backspace') return 'delete';
  if (key === 'escape') return 'escape';
  return null;
}
```

`src/app/board/resistorBands.ts`：

```ts
/** 色碼：黑 0、棕 1、紅 2、橙 3、黃 4、綠 5、藍 6、紫 7、灰 8、白 9。 */
export const BAND_COLORS = ['#000000', '#8b4513', '#d32f2f', '#f57c00', '#fbc02d', '#388e3c', '#1976d2', '#7b1fa2', '#757575', '#ffffff'];
export const GOLD = '#c9a227';

/** 4 色環電阻：第一位數、第二位數、倍率、金色（±5%）。 */
export function resistorBands(ohms: number): [string, string, string, string] {
  const digits = String(Math.max(0, Math.round(ohms)));
  if (digits.length === 1) return [BAND_COLORS[0], BAND_COLORS[Number(digits)], BAND_COLORS[0], GOLD];
  return [BAND_COLORS[Number(digits[0])], BAND_COLORS[Number(digits[1])], BAND_COLORS[Math.min(9, digits.length - 2)], GOLD];
}
```

`src/app/board/ComponentGlyph.tsx`：

```tsx
import { resolveFootprint } from '../../core/circuit/footprint';
import { getComponentDefinition } from '../../core/circuit/library';
import type { ComponentInstance, Footprint, LedColor } from '../../types/component';
import { boundsOf } from '../geometry/componentShape';
import { UNIT } from '../interaction/viewport';
import { resistorBands } from './resistorBands';

const LED_FILL: Record<LedColor, string> = { red: '#ef4444', green: '#22c55e', yellow: '#facc15', blue: '#3b82f6' };
const LEAD = '#9aa0a6';
const u = (v: number) => v * UNIT;

function Pins({ footprint }: { footprint: Footprint }) {
  return (
    <>
      {footprint.pins.map((p) => (
        <rect
          key={p.pinNumber}
          data-pin={p.pinNumber}
          x={u(p.point.x) - 2}
          y={u(p.point.y) - 2}
          width={4}
          height={4}
          rx={0.6}
          fill="#cfd4d8"
          stroke="#8a9096"
          strokeWidth={0.6}
        />
      ))}
    </>
  );
}

interface DipProps {
  footprint: Footprint;
  horizontal: boolean;
  fill: string;
  label: string;
  switchStates?: boolean[];
}

function DipGlyph({ footprint, horizontal, fill, label, switchStates }: DipProps) {
  const pins = footprint.pins;
  const b = boundsOf(pins.map((p) => p.point));
  const rect = horizontal
    ? { x: u(b.minX - 0.45), y: u(b.minY + 0.3), w: u(b.maxX - b.minX + 0.9), h: u(b.maxY - b.minY - 0.6) }
    : { x: u(b.minX + 0.3), y: u(b.minY - 0.45), w: u(b.maxX - b.minX - 0.6), h: u(b.maxY - b.minY + 0.9) };
  const pin1 = pins.find((p) => p.pinNumber === 1)!.point;
  const notch = horizontal
    ? { cx: pin1.x === b.minX ? rect.x : rect.x + rect.w, cy: rect.y + rect.h / 2 }
    : { cx: rect.x + rect.w / 2, cy: pin1.y === b.minY ? rect.y : rect.y + rect.h };
  const dot = horizontal
    ? { cx: u(pin1.x), cy: pin1.y === b.maxY ? rect.y + rect.h - 3 : rect.y + 3 }
    : { cx: pin1.x === b.minX ? rect.x + 3 : rect.x + rect.w - 3, cy: u(pin1.y) };
  const cx = rect.x + rect.w / 2;
  const cy = rect.y + rect.h / 2;
  const half = pins.length / 2;
  return (
    <g>
      <rect x={rect.x} y={rect.y} width={rect.w} height={rect.h} rx={1.5} fill={fill} stroke="#111827" strokeWidth={0.5} />
      <circle cx={notch.cx} cy={notch.cy} r={2.5} fill="#f7f3e8" />
      <circle cx={dot.cx} cy={dot.cy} r={1} fill="#e5e7eb" />
      {label && (
        <text
          x={cx}
          y={cy}
          fontSize={5}
          fill="#ffffff"
          textAnchor="middle"
          dominantBaseline="central"
          transform={horizontal ? undefined : `rotate(-90 ${cx} ${cy})`}
        >
          {label}
        </text>
      )}
      {switchStates &&
        Array.from({ length: half }, (_, i) => {
          const k = i + 1;
          const p = pins.find((pin) => pin.pinNumber === k)!.point;
          const q = pins.find((pin) => pin.pinNumber === pins.length + 1 - k)!.point;
          const mx = (u(p.x) + u(q.x)) / 2;
          const my = (u(p.y) + u(q.y)) / 2;
          const on = switchStates[i] === true;
          const shift = on ? -2.5 : 2.5;
          return (
            <g key={k} data-switch={k}>
              <rect
                x={horizontal ? mx - 2 : mx - 4.5}
                y={horizontal ? my - 4.5 : my - 2}
                width={horizontal ? 4 : 9}
                height={horizontal ? 9 : 4}
                fill="#ffffff"
              />
              <rect
                x={horizontal ? mx - 2 : mx + shift - 2}
                y={horizontal ? my + shift - 2 : my - 2}
                width={4}
                height={4}
                fill="#374151"
              />
              <text
                x={horizontal ? u(p.x) : rect.x + 2.2}
                y={horizontal ? rect.y + 2.2 : u(p.y)}
                fontSize={3}
                fill="#ffffff"
                textAnchor="middle"
                dominantBaseline="central"
              >
                {k}
              </text>
            </g>
          );
        })}
      <Pins footprint={footprint} />
    </g>
  );
}

function ResistorGlyph({ footprint, ohms }: { footprint: Footprint; ohms: number }) {
  const [a, b] = footprint.pins.map((p) => p.point);
  const ax = u(a.x);
  const ay = u(a.y);
  const bx = u(b.x);
  const by = u(b.y);
  const length = Math.hypot(bx - ax, by - ay);
  const angle = (Math.atan2(by - ay, bx - ax) * 180) / Math.PI;
  const body = Math.max(4, Math.min(length - 6, 26));
  const fractions = [0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.8];
  return (
    <g>
      <line x1={ax} y1={ay} x2={bx} y2={by} stroke={LEAD} strokeWidth={1.2} />
      <g transform={`translate(${(ax + bx) / 2} ${(ay + by) / 2}) rotate(${angle})`}>
        <rect x={-body / 2} y={-3} width={body} height={6} rx={2.5} fill="#e9d5a7" stroke="#b08d57" strokeWidth={0.5} />
        {resistorBands(ohms).map((color, i) => (
          <rect key={i} data-band={i} x={-body / 2 + fractions[i] * body - 0.8} y={-3} width={1.6} height={6} fill={color} />
        ))}
      </g>
      <Pins footprint={footprint} />
    </g>
  );
}

function LedGlyph({ footprint, color }: { footprint: Footprint; color: LedColor }) {
  const [a, k] = footprint.pins.map((p) => p.point);
  const ax = u(a.x);
  const ay = u(a.y);
  const kx = u(k.x);
  const ky = u(k.y);
  const length = Math.hypot(kx - ax, ky - ay) || 1;
  const ux = (kx - ax) / length;
  const uy = (ky - ay) / length;
  const mx = (ax + kx) / 2;
  const my = (ay + ky) / 2;
  const r = 4.5;
  const flat = 3.2;
  const chord = Math.sqrt(r * r - flat * flat);
  const fx = mx + ux * flat;
  const fy = my + uy * flat;
  const px = uy;
  const py = -ux;
  return (
    <g>
      <line x1={ax} y1={ay} x2={kx} y2={ky} stroke={LEAD} strokeWidth={1.2} />
      <Pins footprint={footprint} />
      <circle cx={mx} cy={my} r={r} fill={LED_FILL[color]} stroke="#374151" strokeWidth={0.6} opacity={0.92} />
      <line x1={fx - uy * chord} y1={fy + ux * chord} x2={fx + uy * chord} y2={fy - ux * chord} stroke="#374151" strokeWidth={1} />
      <text x={ax + px * 6.5} y={ay + py * 6.5} fontSize={4} fill="#374151" textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="central">
        A
      </text>
      <text x={kx + px * 6.5} y={ky + py * 6.5} fontSize={4} fill="#374151" textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="central">
        K
      </text>
    </g>
  );
}

function TerminalGlyph({ footprint, label, fill }: { footprint: Footprint; label: string; fill: string }) {
  const p = footprint.pins[0].point;
  const x = u(p.x);
  const y = u(p.y);
  const width = Math.max(14, label.length * 3.2 + 4);
  const below = p.y <= 3;
  const tagY = below ? y + 5 : y - 11;
  return (
    <g>
      <line x1={x} y1={y} x2={x} y2={below ? y + 5 : y - 5} stroke={fill} strokeWidth={1} />
      <rect x={x - width / 2} y={tagY} width={width} height={6} rx={1.5} fill={fill} />
      <text x={x} y={tagY + 3} fontSize={4} fill="#ffffff" textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="central">
        {label}
      </text>
      <Pins footprint={footprint} />
    </g>
  );
}

function DcPowerGlyph({ footprint, voltage }: { footprint: Footprint; voltage: number }) {
  const b = boundsOf(footprint.pins.map((p) => p.point));
  const x = u(b.minX) - 4;
  const y = u(b.minY) - 4;
  return (
    <g>
      <rect x={x} y={y} width={u(b.maxX - b.minX) + 8} height={u(b.maxY - b.minY) + 8} rx={2} fill="#374151" />
      {footprint.pins.map((p) => (
        <g key={p.pinNumber}>
          <rect
            data-pin={p.pinNumber}
            x={u(p.point.x) - 2.5}
            y={u(p.point.y) - 2.5}
            width={5}
            height={5}
            rx={0.8}
            fill={p.pinNumber === 1 ? '#d32f2f' : '#212121'}
          />
          <text x={u(p.point.x)} y={u(p.point.y)} fontSize={4} fill="#ffffff" textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="central">
            {p.pinNumber === 1 ? '+' : '−'}
          </text>
        </g>
      ))}
      <text x={x - 1} y={u(b.minY)} fontSize={4} fill="#374151" textAnchor="end" dominantBaseline="central">
        {`${voltage}V`}
      </text>
    </g>
  );
}

function ButtonGlyph({ footprint }: { footprint: Footprint }) {
  const b = boundsOf(footprint.blocked);
  const x = u(b.minX) - 3;
  const y = u(b.minY) - 3;
  const w = u(b.maxX - b.minX) + 6;
  const h = u(b.maxY - b.minY) + 6;
  return (
    <g>
      <rect x={x} y={y} width={w} height={h} rx={2} fill="#1f2937" />
      <circle cx={x + w / 2} cy={y + h / 2} r={7} fill="#4b5563" stroke="#111827" strokeWidth={0.6} />
      <Pins footprint={footprint} />
    </g>
  );
}

export function ComponentGlyph({ instance }: { instance: ComponentInstance }) {
  const def = getComponentDefinition(instance.defId);
  if (!def) return null;
  const footprint = resolveFootprint(instance, def);
  const props = { ...def.defaultProps, ...instance.props };
  const horizontal = instance.rotation === 0 || instance.rotation === 180;
  switch (def.category) {
    case 'ic':
      return <DipGlyph footprint={footprint} horizontal={horizontal} fill="#2b2b2b" label={def.name} />;
    case 'dip-switch':
      return <DipGlyph footprint={footprint} horizontal={horizontal} fill="#c62828" label="" switchStates={props.switchStates ?? []} />;
    case 'resistor':
      return <ResistorGlyph footprint={footprint} ohms={props.resistance ?? 1000} />;
    case 'led':
      return <LedGlyph footprint={footprint} color={props.color ?? 'red'} />;
    case 'vcc':
      return <TerminalGlyph footprint={footprint} label={`VCC ${props.voltage ?? 5}V`} fill="#d32f2f" />;
    case 'gnd':
      return <TerminalGlyph footprint={footprint} label="GND" fill="#212121" />;
    case 'dc-power':
      return <DcPowerGlyph footprint={footprint} voltage={props.voltage ?? 5} />;
    case 'pushbutton':
      return <ButtonGlyph footprint={footprint} />;
  }
}
```

```tsx
    const m1Paths = [...container.querySelectorAll('[data-wire-id="m1"] path')];

    expect(container.querySelector('[data-wire-id="b"]')!.getAttribute('data-delay')).toBe('0');

  it('draws the overlay: selection, markers, ghost and previews', () => {

    const layer = container.querySelector('[data-layer="overlay"]')!;

    expect(layer.querySelectorAll('[data-marker="error"]')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(layer.querySelectorAll('[data-marker="warning"]')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(layer.querySelector('[data-ghost="invalid"]')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(layer.querySelector('[data-wire-preview]')).not.toBeNull();

    const c = circuit([comp('U1', '74LS00', 'F10')]);

      { ...base, code: 'IC_POWER_MISSING', severity: 'error', points: [hole('F11')] },

    ];
    expect(markersFromErrors(errors, c)).toEqual([
      { point: hole('F11'), severity: 'error' },
      { point: hole('F10'), severity: 'error' },
    ]);
  });

  it('collects the wires that should be drawn as invalid', () => {
    const errors: CircuitError[] = [
      { ...base, code: 'WIRE_OVERLAP', severity: 'error', points: [], wireIds: ['m1', 'm2'] },
      { ...base, code: 'SHORT_CIRCUIT', severity: 'error', points: [], wireIds: ['m3'] },
    ];
    expect([...invalidWireIds(errors)]).toEqual(['m1', 'm2']);
  });
});

describe('netHighlightFor', () => {
  it('finds every wire and pin on the selected net', () => {
    const c = circuit([comp('D1', 'led', 'C10'), comp('D2', 'led', 'C30')], [wire('m1', 'A10', 'A30'), wire('m2', 'B11', 'B20')]);
    const fromPin = netHighlightFor({ kind: 'pin', componentId: 'D1', pinNumber: 1 }, c)!;
    expect([...fromPin.wireIds]).toEqual(['m1']);
    expect(fromPin.pinPoints).toEqual([hole('C10'), hole('C30')]);
    expect(netHighlightFor({ kind: 'wire', id: 'm1' }, c)!.netId).toBe(fromPin.netId);
    expect(netHighlightFor({ kind: 'component', id: 'D1' }, c)).toBeNull();
    expect(netHighlightFor(null, c)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 實作分析工具**

`src/app/board/markers.ts`：

```ts
import { resolveFootprint } from '../../core/circuit/footprint';
import { getComponentDefinition } from '../../core/circuit/library';
import type { Circuit, CircuitError } from '../../types/circuit';
import type { GridPoint } from '../../types/geometry';

export interface Marker {
  point: GridPoint;
  severity: 'error' | 'warning';
}

/** 規格第 5.6 節：錯誤畫在它的點位上；沒有點位時畫在相關元件的第 1 腳。同一點只留最嚴重的。 */
export function markersFromErrors(errors: CircuitError[], circuit: Circuit): Marker[] {
  const byKey = new Map<string, Marker>();
  const add = (point: GridPoint, severity: Marker['severity']) => {
    const key = `${point.boardId}:${point.x},${point.y}`;
    const existing = byKey.get(key);
    if (!existing || (existing.severity === 'warning' && severity === 'error')) byKey.set(key, { point, severity });
  };
  for (const e of errors) {
    if (e.severity === 'info') continue;
    const severity = e.severity;
    if (e.points.length > 0) {
      for (const p of e.points) add(p, severity);
      continue;
    }
    for (const id of e.componentIds) {
      const instance = circuit.components.find((c) => c.id === id);
      const def = instance ? getComponentDefinition(instance.defId) : undefined;
      if (instance && def) add(resolveFootprint(instance, def).pins[0].point, severity);
    }
  }
  return [...byKey.values()];
}

/** 規格第 5.4 節：有這些錯誤的導線畫成紅色虛線。 */
export const WIRE_ERROR_CODES = new Set<string>(['WIRE_PATH_INVALID', 'WIRE_ENDPOINT_INVALID', 'WIRE_THROUGH_OBSTACLE', 'WIRE_OVERLAP']);

export function invalidWireIds(errors: CircuitError[]): Set<string> {
  return new Set(errors.filter((e) => WIRE_ERROR_CODES.has(e.code)).flatMap((e) => e.wireIds));
}
```

`src/app/board/netHighlight.ts`：

```ts
import { groupOfPoint, indexCircuit, pinPoint } from '../../core/circuit/circuitIndex';
import { extractNetsWith } from '../../core/circuit/nets';
import type { Circuit } from '../../types/circuit';
import type { GridPoint } from '../../types/geometry';
import type { Selection } from '../model/editorTypes';

export interface NetHighlight {
  netId: string;
  wireIds: Set<string>;
  pinPoints: GridPoint[];
}

/** 選取腳或導線時，找出同一個 Net 的所有導線與腳。 */
export function netHighlightFor(selection: Selection | null, circuit: Circuit): NetHighlight | null {
  if (!selection || (selection.kind !== 'pin' && selection.kind !== 'wire')) return null;
  const index = indexCircuit(circuit);
  let groupId: string | null = null;
  if (selection.kind === 'pin') {
    const p = pinPoint(index, { componentId: selection.componentId, pinNumber: selection.pinNumber });
    groupId = p ? groupOfPoint(index, p) : null;
  } else {
    const w = circuit.wires.find((x) => x.id === selection.id);
    groupId = w ? groupOfPoint(index, w.start) : null;
  }
  if (!groupId) return null;
  const net = extractNetsWith(index, circuit.wires).find((n) => n.groupIds.includes(groupId!));
  if (!net) return null;
  const groups = new Set(net.groupIds);
  const wireIds = new Set(
    circuit.wires
      .filter((w) => {
        const g = groupOfPoint(index, w.start);
        return g !== null && groups.has(g);
      })
      .map((w) => w.id),
  );
  const pinPoints = net.pins.map((ref) => pinPoint(index, ref)).filter((p): p is GridPoint => p !== undefined);
  return { netId: net.id, wireIds, pinPoints };
}
```

`src/app/board/BoardBase.tsx`：

```tsx
import { memo } from 'react';
import { getBoard } from '../../core/breadboard/registry';
import { UNIT } from '../interaction/viewport';

const board = getBoard('MB102', 'b1');
const holes = board.nodes.filter((n) => n.kind === 'hole');
const COLUMN_LABELS = [1, 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30, 35, 40, 45, 50, 55, 60];
const ROW_LABELS: Array<{ y: number; text: string; color: string }> = [
  { y: 1, text: '−', color: '#1565c0' },
  { y: 2, text: '+', color: '#d32f2f' },
  ...['A', 'B', 'C', 'D', 'E'].map((text, i) => ({ y: 4 + i, text, color: '#6b7280' })),
  ...['F', 'G', 'H', 'I', 'J'].map((text, i) => ({ y: 11 + i, text, color: '#6b7280' })),
  { y: 17, text: '+', color: '#d32f2f' },
  { y: 18, text: '−', color: '#1565c0' },
];

/** 靜態底板：板面、電源軌色線、凹槽、830 個孔、欄號與列號。只畫一次。 */
export const BoardBase = memo(function BoardBase() {
  return (
    <g>
      <rect x={0} y={0} width={640} height={190} rx={8} fill="#f7f3e8" stroke="#d6cfbd" strokeWidth={1} />
      <rect x={10} y={86} width={620} height={18} fill="#e9e2d0" />
      <line x1={25} y1={5} x2={615} y2={5} stroke="#1565c0" strokeWidth={0.8} />
      <line x1={25} y1={25} x2={615} y2={25} stroke="#d32f2f" strokeWidth={0.8} />
      <line x1={25} y1={165} x2={615} y2={165} stroke="#d32f2f" strokeWidth={0.8} />
      <line x1={25} y1={185} x2={615} y2={185} stroke="#1565c0" strokeWidth={0.8} />
      {holes.map((n) => (
        <rect key={n.id} data-hole={n.name ?? undefined} x={n.x * UNIT - 2.5} y={n.y * UNIT - 2.5} width={5} height={5} rx={1} fill="#4a4a4a" />
      ))}
      {COLUMN_LABELS.flatMap((x) => [
        <text key={`t${x}`} x={x * UNIT} y={30} fontSize={4} fill="#6b7280" textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="central">
          {x}
        </text>,
        <text key={`b${x}`} x={x * UNIT} y={160} fontSize={4} fill="#6b7280" textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="central">
          {x}
        </text>,
      ])}
      {ROW_LABELS.flatMap((r) => [
        <text key={`l${r.y}`} x={4} y={r.y * UNIT} fontSize={5} fill={r.color} textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="central">
          {r.text}
        </text>,
        <text key={`r${r.y}`} x={636} y={r.y * UNIT} fontSize={5} fill={r.color} textAnchor="middle" dominantBaseline="central">
          {r.text}
        </text>,
      ])}
    </g>
  );
});
```

`src/app/board/WireLayer.tsx`：

```tsx
import { memo, useMemo, type CSSProperties } from 'react';
import { nodeIdOf } from '../../core/breadboard/naming';
import { analyzeWireInteractions } from '../../core/circuit/wireGeometry';
import type { Wire } from '../../types/circuit';
import type { GridPoint } from '../../types/geometry';
import { UNIT } from '../interaction/viewport';

export function wirePathD(path: GridPoint[]): string {
  return path.map((p, i) => `${i === 0 ? 'M' : 'L'}${p.x * UNIT} ${p.y * UNIT}`).join(' ');
}

/** 把 #rrggbb 調暗 amount（0–1）。 */
export function darken(hex: string, amount = 0.3): string {
  const m = /^#([0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(hex);
  if (!m) return '#333333';
  const n = parseInt(m[1], 16);
  const channel = (shift: number) => Math.round(((n >> shift) & 255) * (1 - amount)).toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
  return `#${channel(16)}${channel(8)}${channel(0)}`;
}

interface Crossing {
  key: string;
  d: string;
  color: string;
}

/** 交叉點上把後畫的那條線加一段淺色外框，看起來疊在上面。 */
function crossingSegments(wires: Wire[]): Crossing[] {
  const order = new Map(wires.map((w, i) => [w.id, i]));
  return analyzeWireInteractions(wires).crossings.flatMap(({ nodeId, wireIds }) => {
    const top = wireIds
      .map((id) => wires[order.get(id)!])
      .sort((a, b) => order.get(b.id)! - order.get(a.id)!)[0];
    const k = top.path.findIndex((p) => nodeIdOf(p) === nodeId);
    if (k <= 0 || k >= top.path.length - 1) return [];
    const p = top.path[k];
    const mid = (q: GridPoint) => `${((q.x + p.x) / 2) * UNIT} ${((q.y + p.y) / 2) * UNIT}`;
    return [{ key: `${nodeId}-${top.id}`, d: `M${mid(top.path[k - 1])} L${p.x * UNIT} ${p.y * UNIT} L${mid(top.path[k + 1])}`, color: top.color }];
  });
}

const EMPTY = new Set<string>();
const LINE = { fill: 'none', strokeLinecap: 'round' as const, strokeLinejoin: 'round' as const };

export interface WireLayerProps {
  wires: Wire[];
  invalidIds?: ReadonlySet<string>;
  dimmedIds?: ReadonlySet<string>;
  animation?: { order: string[]; stepMs: number } | null;
  fading?: Wire[];
}

export const WireLayer = memo(function WireLayer({ wires, invalidIds = EMPTY, dimmedIds = EMPTY, animation = null, fading = [] }: WireLayerProps) {
  const crossings = useMemo(() => crossingSegments(wires), [wires]);
  const orderIndex = useMemo(() => new Map((animation?.order ?? []).map((id, i) => [id, i])), [animation]);
  return (
    <>
      {fading.map((w) => (
        <path key={`fade-${w.id}`} d={wirePathD(w.path)} stroke={w.color} strokeWidth={5.6} {...LINE} style={{ animation: 'bbl-fade 300ms linear forwards' }} />
      ))}
      {wires.map((w) => {
        const d = wirePathD(w.path);
        const index = animation ? orderIndex.get(w.id) : undefined;
        const length = (w.path.length - 1) * UNIT;
        const style: CSSProperties | undefined =
          animation && index !== undefined
            ? {
                strokeDasharray: length,
                strokeDashoffset: length,
                animation: `bbl-draw ${animation.stepMs}ms linear ${index * animation.stepMs}ms forwards`,
              }
            : undefined;
        const invalid = invalidIds.has(w.id);
        return (
          <g key={w.id} data-wire-id={w.id} data-delay={index !== undefined && animation ? index * animation.stepMs : undefined} opacity={dimmedIds.has(w.id) ? 0.35 : undefined}>
            <path d={d} stroke={darken(w.color)} strokeWidth={7} {...LINE} style={style} />
            <path d={d} stroke={invalid ? '#d32f2f' : w.color} strokeWidth={5.6} {...LINE} strokeDasharray={invalid && !style ? '4 3' : undefined} style={style} />
            {[w.start, w.end].map((p, i) => (
              <rect key={i} x={p.x * UNIT - 2} y={p.y * UNIT - 2} width={4} height={4} rx={0.6} fill="#c0c4c8" stroke="#80868b" strokeWidth={0.5} />
            ))}
          </g>
        );
      })}
      {crossings.map((c) => (
        <g key={c.key} data-crossing="">
          <path d={c.d} stroke="#ffffff" strokeWidth={9} {...LINE} />
          <path d={c.d} stroke={c.color} strokeWidth={5.6} {...LINE} />
        </g>
      ))}
    </>
  );
});
```

`src/app/board/OverlayLayer.tsx`：

```tsx
import { resolveFootprint } from '../../core/circuit/footprint';
import { getComponentDefinition } from '../../core/circuit/library';
import type { Circuit, Wire } from '../../types/circuit';
import { boundsOf, componentCells } from '../geometry/componentShape';
import { UNIT } from '../interaction/viewport';
import type { Ghost, Selection, WirePreview } from '../model/editorTypes';
import { ComponentGlyph } from './ComponentGlyph';
import type { Marker } from './markers';
import type { NetHighlight } from './netHighlight';
import { wirePathD } from './WireLayer';

export interface OverlayData {
  selection: Selection | null;
  net: NetHighlight | null;
  markers: Marker[];
  ghost: Ghost | null;
  wirePreview: WirePreview | null;
  wireColor: string;
  previewWires: Wire[];
  focusWireId: string | null;
}

const SELECT = '#2196f3';
const NET = '#00bcd4';
const LINE = { fill: 'none', strokeLinecap: 'round' as const, strokeLinejoin: 'round' as const };

function SelectionMark({ circuit, selection }: { circuit: Circuit; selection: Selection }) {
  switch (selection.kind) {
    case 'component': {
      const c = circuit.components.find((x) => x.id === selection.id);
      const def = c ? getComponentDefinition(c.defId) : undefined;
      if (!c || !def) return null;
      const b = boundsOf(componentCells(c, def));
      return (
        <rect
          data-selection="component"
          x={(b.minX - 0.6) * UNIT}
          y={(b.minY - 0.6) * UNIT}
          width={(b.maxX - b.minX + 1.2) * UNIT}
          height={(b.maxY - b.minY + 1.2) * UNIT}
          rx={2}
          fill="none"
          stroke={SELECT}
          strokeWidth={1.5}
        />
      );
    }
    case 'pin': {
      const c = circuit.components.find((x) => x.id === selection.componentId);
      const def = c ? getComponentDefinition(c.defId) : undefined;
      const pin = c && def ? resolveFootprint(c, def).pins.find((p) => p.pinNumber === selection.pinNumber) : undefined;
      if (!pin) return null;
      return <circle data-selection="pin" cx={pin.point.x * UNIT} cy={pin.point.y * UNIT} r={4.5} fill="none" stroke={SELECT} strokeWidth={1.5} />;
    }
    case 'wire': {
      const w = circuit.wires.find((x) => x.id === selection.id);
      if (!w) return null;
      return <path data-selection="wire" d={wirePathD(w.path)} stroke={SELECT} strokeWidth={11} opacity={0.35} {...LINE} />;
    }
    case 'hole':
      return (
        <rect
          data-selection="hole"
          x={selection.point.x * UNIT - 4}
          y={selection.point.y * UNIT - 4}
          width={8}
          height={8}
          fill="none"
          stroke={SELECT}
          strokeWidth={1.2}
        />
      );
  }
}

function GhostMark({ ghost }: { ghost: Ghost }) {
  const def = getComponentDefinition(ghost.instance.defId);
  if (!def) return null;
  const b = boundsOf(componentCells(ghost.instance, def));
  return (
    <g opacity={0.6} data-ghost={ghost.valid ? 'valid' : 'invalid'}>
      <ComponentGlyph instance={ghost.instance} />
      <rect
        x={(b.minX - 0.6) * UNIT}
        y={(b.minY - 0.6) * UNIT}
        width={(b.maxX - b.minX + 1.2) * UNIT}
        height={(b.maxY - b.minY + 1.2) * UNIT}
        rx={2}
        fill="none"
        stroke={ghost.valid ? '#16a34a' : '#dc2626'}
        strokeWidth={1.5}
        strokeDasharray="3 2"
      />
    </g>
  );
}

export function OverlayLayer({ circuit, selection, net, markers, ghost, wirePreview, wireColor, previewWires, focusWireId }: OverlayData & { circuit: Circuit }) {
  const focus = focusWireId ? circuit.wires.find((w) => w.id === focusWireId) : undefined;
  return (
    <g pointerEvents="none">
      {previewWires.map((w) => (
        <path key={w.id} data-preview-wire={w.id} d={wirePathD(w.path)} stroke={w.color} strokeWidth={5.6} opacity={0.4} {...LINE} />
      ))}
      {net &&
        circuit.wires
          .filter((w) => net.wireIds.has(w.id))
          .map((w) => <path key={`net-${w.id}`} d={wirePathD(w.path)} stroke={NET} strokeWidth={11} opacity={0.35} {...LINE} />)}
      {net &&
        net.pinPoints.map((p) => <circle key={`pin-${p.x},${p.y}`} cx={p.x * UNIT} cy={p.y * UNIT} r={4} fill="none" stroke={NET} strokeWidth={1.5} />)}
      {focus && <path d={wirePathD(focus.path)} stroke={SELECT} strokeWidth={11} opacity={0.35} {...LINE} />}
      {selection && <SelectionMark circuit={circuit} selection={selection} />}
      {markers.map((m) => (
        <g key={`${m.point.x},${m.point.y}`} data-marker={m.severity}>
          {m.severity === 'error' && <circle cx={m.point.x * UNIT} cy={m.point.y * UNIT} r={5} fill="none" stroke="#d32f2f" strokeWidth={1.2} />}
          <text
            x={m.point.x * UNIT + 4}
            y={m.point.y * UNIT - 4}
            fontSize={6}
            fill={m.severity === 'error' ? '#d32f2f' : '#f59e0b'}
            textAnchor="middle"
            dominantBaseline="central"
          >
            ⚠
          </text>
        </g>
      ))}
      {ghost && <GhostMark ghost={ghost} />}
      {wirePreview && (
        <path
          data-wire-preview=""
          d={wirePathD(wirePreview.path)}
          stroke={wireColor}
          strokeWidth={5.6}
          opacity={0.7}
          strokeDasharray={wirePreview.valid ? undefined : '4 3'}
          {...LINE}
        />
      )}
    </g>
  );
}
```

`src/app/board/BoardSvg.tsx`：

```tsx
import { memo, type MouseEvent, type PointerEvent, type Ref } from 'react';
import type { Circuit, Wire } from '../../types/circuit';
import type { ComponentInstance } from '../../types/component';
import type { Viewport } from '../model/editorTypes';
import { BoardBase } from './BoardBase';
import { ComponentGlyph } from './ComponentGlyph';
import { OverlayLayer, type OverlayData } from './OverlayLayer';
import { WireLayer } from './WireLayer';

export type { OverlayData } from './OverlayLayer';

export interface BoardHandlers {
  onPointerDown?: (e: PointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => void;
  onPointerMove?: (e: PointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => void;
  onPointerUp?: (e: PointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => void;
  onPointerCancel?: (e: PointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => void;
  onContextMenu?: (e: MouseEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => void;
}

export interface BoardSvgProps {
  circuit: Circuit;
  viewport: Viewport;
  invalidWireIds?: ReadonlySet<string>;
  dimmedWireIds?: ReadonlySet<string>;
  animation?: { order: string[]; stepMs: number } | null;
  fadingWires?: Wire[];
  overlay?: OverlayData | null;
  svgRef?: Ref<SVGSVGElement>;
  handlers?: BoardHandlers;
  label?: string;
}

const ComponentLayer = memo(function ComponentLayer({ components }: { components: ComponentInstance[] }) {
  return (
    <>
      {components.map((c) => (
        <g key={c.id} data-component-id={c.id}>
          <ComponentGlyph instance={c} />
        </g>
      ))}
    </>
  );
});

export function BoardSvg({
  circuit,
  viewport,
  invalidWireIds,
  dimmedWireIds,
  animation = null,
  fadingWires,
  overlay = null,
  svgRef,
  handlers,
  label = '麵包板',
}: BoardSvgProps) {
  return (
    <svg
      ref={svgRef}
      data-testid="board-svg"
      aria-label={label}
      width="100%"
      height="100%"
      style={{ display: 'block', touchAction: 'none', userSelect: 'none' }}
      {...handlers}
    >
      <g transform={`translate(${viewport.tx} ${viewport.ty}) scale(${viewport.scale})`}>
        <g data-layer="board">
          <BoardBase />
        </g>
        <g data-layer="components">
          <ComponentLayer components={circuit.components} />
        </g>
        <g data-layer="wires">
          <WireLayer wires={circuit.wires} invalidIds={invalidWireIds} dimmedIds={dimmedWireIds} animation={animation} fading={fadingWires} />
        </g>
        {overlay && (
          <g data-layer="overlay">
            <OverlayLayer circuit={circuit} {...overlay} />
          </g>
        )}
      </g>
    </svg>
  );
}
```

`src/app/persistence/download.ts`：

```ts
export function downloadBlob(filename: string, blob: Blob): void {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const link = document.createElement('a');
  link.href = url;
  link.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  link.remove();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
}

export function downloadText(filename: string, text: string, type = 'application/json'): void {
  downloadBlob(filename, new Blob([text], { type: `${type};charset=utf-8` }));
}

/** 開啟選檔視窗，讀出文字內容；使用者取消時回傳 null。 */
export function pickTextFile(accept = '.json,application/json'): Promise<{ name: string; text: string } | null> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = accept;
    input.style.display = 'none';
    input.addEventListener(
      'change',
      async () => {
        const file = input.files?.[0];
        input.remove();
        if (!file) {
          resolve(null);
          return;
        }
        resolve({ name: file.name, text: await file.text() });
      },
      { once: true },
    );
    input.addEventListener(
      'cancel',
      () => {
        input.remove();
        resolve(null);
      },
      { once: true },
    );
    document.body.appendChild(input);
    input.click();
  });
}
```

`src/app/persistence/exportImage.ts`：

```ts
import { BOARD_HEIGHT, BOARD_WIDTH } from '../interaction/viewport';

const SVG_NS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
export const EXPORT_LAYERS = ['board', 'components', 'wires'] as const;

/** 規格第 11.3 節：複製板子、元件、導線三層，去掉動畫、淡出中的舊導線與變淡，變成獨立的 SVG。 */
export function boardSvgMarkup(svg: SVGSVGElement): string {
  const out = svg.ownerDocument.createElementNS(SVG_NS, 'svg');
  out.setAttribute('viewBox', `0 0 ${BOARD_WIDTH} ${BOARD_HEIGHT}`);
  out.setAttribute('width', String(BOARD_WIDTH * 2));
  out.setAttribute('height', String(BOARD_HEIGHT * 2));
  for (const name of EXPORT_LAYERS) {
    const layer = svg.querySelector(`[data-layer="${name}"]`);
    if (layer) out.appendChild(layer.cloneNode(true));
  }
  out.querySelectorAll('[data-fading]').forEach((el) => el.remove());
  out.querySelectorAll('[style]').forEach((el) => el.removeAttribute('style'));
  out.querySelectorAll('[data-wire-id]').forEach((el) => el.removeAttribute('opacity'));
  return new XMLSerializer().serializeToString(out);
}

/** 把 SVG 文字畫到 scale 倍大的白底 canvas，輸出 PNG。 */
export async function svgMarkupToPng(markup: string, width: number = BOARD_WIDTH * 2, height: number = BOARD_HEIGHT * 2, scale = 2): Promise<Blob> {
  const image = new Image();
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    image.onload = () => resolve();
    image.onerror = () => reject(new Error('無法把電路圖轉成圖片'));
    image.src = `data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,${encodeURIComponent(markup)}`;
  });
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = width * scale;
  canvas.height = height * scale;
  const context = canvas.getContext('2d');
  if (!context) throw new Error('瀏覽器不支援畫布');
  context.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
  context.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  context.drawImage(image, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  return new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) =>
    canvas.toBlob((blob) => (blob ? resolve(blob) : reject(new Error('無法產生 PNG'))), 'image/png'),
  );
}
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5 的指令；Task 6 的 `useEditor`、`resetEditor`；Task 3 的 `hitTest`、`snapAnchor`、`anchorCenteredAt`、`lPath`；Task 4 的 `checkPlacement`、`isFreeHole`；Task 9 的視窗數學、`planPress`、`restrictPlan`、`wireOrientation`、`shortcutFor`；Task 11 的 `BoardSvg`、`markersFromErrors`、`invalidWireIds`、`netHighlightFor`
- Produces:

```tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { BoardCanvas } from '../../src/app/board/BoardCanvas';
import { addWire, placeComponent } from '../../src/app/commands/commands';
import { ContextMenu } from '../../src/app/interaction/ContextMenu';
import { runShortcut, selectedComponentId, useKeyboardShortcuts } from '../../src/app/interaction/useKeyboardShortcuts';
import { resetEditor, useEditor } from '../../src/app/store/editorStore';
import { hole } from '../fixtures/build';

afterEach(cleanup);
beforeEach(() => resetEditor());

const place = (defId: string, x: number, y: number) => useEditor.getState().run((doc) => placeComponent(doc, defId, { x, y }));
const ids = () => useEditor.getState().doc.circuit.components.map((c) => c.id);
const noSave = () => undefined;

describe('runShortcut', () => {
  it('rotates and deletes the selected part, and deletes a selected wire', () => {
    place('resistor', 20, 6);
    runShortcut('rotate', noSave);
    expect(useEditor.getState().doc.circuit.components[0].rotation).toBe(90);
    runShortcut('delete', noSave);
    expect(ids()).toEqual([]);
    useEditor.getState().run((doc) => addWire(doc, hole('A10'), hole('A20'), true, '#212121'));
    runShortcut('delete', noSave);
    expect(useEditor.getState().doc.circuit.wires).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('undoes, redoes and saves', () => {
    place('led', 40, 12);
    runShortcut('undo', noSave);
    expect(ids()).toEqual([]);
    runShortcut('redo', noSave);
    expect(ids()).toEqual(['D1']);
    const save = vi.fn();
    runShortcut('save', save);
    expect(save).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('escape cancels a pending action first, then leaves compare mode, then clears the selection', () => {
    place('led', 40, 12);
    useEditor.getState().setArmedPart({ key: 'led-red', defId: 'led', label: '紅色 LED', props: { color: 'red' } });
    runShortcut('escape', noSave);
    expect(useEditor.getState().armedPart).toBeNull();
    expect(useEditor.getState().selection).not.toBeNull();
    useEditor.getState().setMode('compare');
    runShortcut('escape', noSave);
    expect(useEditor.getState().mode).toBe('edit');
    runShortcut('escape', noSave);
    expect(useEditor.getState().selection).toBeNull();
  });

  it('treats a selected pin as its part', () => {
    expect(selectedComponentId({ kind: 'pin', componentId: 'U1', pinNumber: 3 })).toBe('U1');
    expect(selectedComponentId({ kind: 'wire', id: 'm1' })).toBeNull();
    expect(selectedComponentId(null)).toBeNull();
  });
});

function ShortcutHost({ onSave }: { onSave: () => void }) {
  useKeyboardShortcuts(onSave);
  return <input aria-label="name" />;
}

describe('useKeyboardShortcuts', () => {
  it('listens on the window but ignores typing in inputs, except Ctrl+S', () => {
    place('led', 40, 12);
    const save = vi.fn();
    render(<ShortcutHost onSave={save} />);
    fireEvent.keyDown(screen.getByLabelText('name'), { key: 'Delete' });
    expect(ids()).toEqual(['D1']);
    fireEvent.keyDown(window, { key: 'z', ctrlKey: true });
    expect(ids()).toEqual([]);
    fireEvent.keyDown(screen.getByLabelText('name'), { key: 's', ctrlKey: true });
    expect(save).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });
});

describe('ContextMenu', () => {
  it('offers rotate and delete for parts, and flip for wires', () => {
    place('resistor', 20, 6);
    const onClose = vi.fn();
    render(<ContextMenu menu={{ x: 10, y: 10, hit: { kind: 'component', componentId: 'R1' } }} onClose={onClose} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: '旋轉 90°' })).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: '刪除' }));
    expect(ids()).toEqual([]);
    expect(onClose).toHaveBeenCalled();
    cleanup();
    useEditor.getState().run((doc) => addWire(doc, hole('A10'), hole('C12'), true, '#212121'));
    render(<ContextMenu menu={{ x: 10, y: 10, hit: { kind: 'wire', wireId: 'm1' } }} onClose={noSave} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: '翻轉轉角' }));
    expect(useEditor.getState().doc.circuit.wires[0].path[1]).toMatchObject({ x: 10, y: 5 });
  });
});

describe('BoardCanvas', () => {
  it('renders the board and zooms with the buttons', () => {
    render(<BoardCanvas />);
    expect(screen.getByTestId('board-svg')).toBeTruthy();
    const before = useEditor.getState().viewport.scale;
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '放大' }));
    expect(useEditor.getState().viewport.scale).toBeGreaterThan(before);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '縮小' }));
    expect(useEditor.getState().viewport.scale).toBeCloseTo(before, 5);
  });
});
```
