- [ ] **Step 3: 實作鍵盤、登記與右鍵選單**

`src/app/interaction/boardRegistry.ts`：

```ts
import type { Viewport } from '../model/editorTypes';
import { screenToLattice } from './viewport';

let element: SVGSVGElement | null = null;

/** 主畫面的板子 SVG（元件庫拖放時用來換算座標）。 */
export function registerBoardElement(el: SVGSVGElement | null): void {
  element = el;
}

export function getBoardElement(): SVGSVGElement | null {
  return element;
}

/** 螢幕座標 → 主畫面板子上的格子座標（小數）；不在板子範圍內時回傳 null。 */
export function clientToBoardLattice(clientX: number, clientY: number, viewport: Viewport): { x: number; y: number } | null {
  if (!element) return null;
  const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (clientX < rect.left || clientX > rect.right || clientY < rect.top || clientY > rect.bottom) return null;
  return screenToLattice(viewport, clientX - rect.left, clientY - rect.top);
}
```

`src/app/interaction/useKeyboardShortcuts.ts`：

```ts
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { deleteComponent, deleteWire, rotateComponent } from '../commands/commands';
import type { Selection } from '../model/editorTypes';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';
import { shortcutFor, type ShortcutAction } from './shortcuts';

export function isTextTarget(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  const el = target as HTMLElement | null;
  if (!el || typeof el.tagName !== 'string') return false;
  return el.tagName === 'INPUT' || el.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || el.tagName === 'SELECT' || el.isContentEditable === true;
}

export function selectedComponentId(selection: Selection | null): string | null {
  if (!selection) return null;
  if (selection.kind === 'component') return selection.id;
  if (selection.kind === 'pin') return selection.componentId;
  return null;
}

export function runShortcut(action: ShortcutAction, onSave: () => void): void {
  const s = useEditor.getState();
  switch (action) {
    case 'undo':
      s.undo();
      return;
    case 'redo':
      s.redo();
      return;
    case 'save':
      onSave();
      return;
    case 'rotate': {
      const id = selectedComponentId(s.selection);
      if (id) s.run((doc) => rotateComponent(doc, id));
      return;
    }
    case 'delete': {
      const selection = s.selection;
      const id = selectedComponentId(selection);
      if (id) s.run((doc) => deleteComponent(doc, id));
      else if (selection?.kind === 'wire') s.run((doc) => deleteWire(doc, selection.id));
      return;
    }
    case 'escape':
      if (s.ghost || s.wirePreview || s.armedPart) {
        s.setGhost(null);
        s.setWirePreview(null);
        s.setArmedPart(null);
        return;
      }
      if (s.mode === 'compare') {
        s.setMode('edit');
        return;
      }
      s.select(null);
  }
}

export function useKeyboardShortcuts(onSave: () => void): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const action = shortcutFor(e, isTextTarget(e.target));
      if (!action) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      runShortcut(action, onSave);
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [onSave]);
}
```

`src/app/interaction/ContextMenu.tsx`：

```tsx
import { deleteComponent, deleteWire, flipWire, rotateComponent, type CommandResult } from '../commands/commands';
import type { ProjectFile } from '../model/projectFile';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';
import type { ContextMenuState } from './useBoardInteractions';

function Item({ label, onClick }: { label: string; onClick: () => void }) {
  return (
    <button type="button" role="menuitem" className="block min-h-11 w-full px-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-100 lg:min-h-0 lg:py-1.5" onClick={onClick}>
      {label}
    </button>
  );
}

export function ContextMenu({ menu, onClose }: { menu: ContextMenuState; onClose: () => void }) {
  const componentId = menu.hit.kind === 'pin' || menu.hit.kind === 'component' ? menu.hit.componentId : null;
  const wireId = menu.hit.kind === 'wire' ? menu.hit.wireId : null;
  const act = (command: (doc: ProjectFile) => CommandResult) => () => {
    useEditor.getState().run(command);
    onClose();
  };
  return (
    <>
      <div
        className="fixed inset-0 z-40"
        onPointerDown={onClose}
        onContextMenu={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          onClose();
        }}
      />
      <div role="menu" className="fixed z-50 min-w-36 rounded-md border border-gray-200 bg-white py-1 text-sm shadow-lg" style={{ left: menu.x, top: menu.y }}>
        {componentId && <Item label="旋轉 90°" onClick={act((doc) => rotateComponent(doc, componentId))} />}
        {componentId && <Item label="刪除" onClick={act((doc) => deleteComponent(doc, componentId))} />}
        {wireId && <Item label="翻轉轉角" onClick={act((doc) => flipWire(doc, wireId))} />}
        {wireId && <Item label="刪除" onClick={act((doc) => deleteWire(doc, wireId))} />}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 實作板子上的指標互動**

`src/app/interaction/useBoardInteractions.ts`：

```ts
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState, type MouseEvent as ReactMouseEvent, type PointerEvent as ReactPointerEvent } from 'react';
import { getComponentDefinition } from '../../core/circuit/library';
import type { Circuit } from '../../types/circuit';
import type { GridPoint } from '../../types/geometry';
import { addWire, moveComponent, placeComponent } from '../commands/commands';
import { hitTest, type Hit } from '../geometry/hitTest';
import { checkPlacement, isFreeHole } from '../geometry/placement';
import { anchorCenteredAt, snapAnchor } from '../geometry/snap';
import { lPath } from '../geometry/wirePath';
import type { Viewport } from '../model/editorTypes';
import { BOARD_ID } from '../model/projectFile';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';
import { CLICK_TOLERANCE_PX, LONG_PRESS_MS, planPress, restrictPlan, wireOrientation, type PressPlan } from './pressPlan';
import { isTextTarget } from './useKeyboardShortcuts';
import { panBy, pinchViewport, screenToGridPoint, screenToLattice, WHEEL_FACTOR, zoomAt, type ScreenPoint } from './viewport';

export interface ContextMenuState {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  hit: Hit;
}

export interface BoardInteractionOptions {
  circuit: Circuit;
  editable: boolean;
  selectable: boolean;
}

interface Session {
  pointerId: number;
  start: ScreenPoint;
  plan: PressPlan;
  startPoint: GridPoint;
  dragging: boolean;
  grab: { dx: number; dy: number } | null;
  horizontalFirst: boolean | null;
  startViewport: Viewport;
  longPress: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null;
}

interface Pinch {
  ids: [number, number];
  start: [ScreenPoint, ScreenPoint];
  startViewport: Viewport;
}

const PAN_ONLY: PressPlan = { click: null, drag: 'pan', componentId: null };

/** 規格第 6.2 節的指標狀態機：點擊選取、拖曳移動元件／接線／平移、滾輪縮放、兩指縮放、長按與右鍵選單。 */
export function useBoardInteractions({ circuit, editable, selectable }: BoardInteractionOptions) {
  const svg = useRef<SVGSVGElement | null>(null);
  const [svgElement, setSvgElement] = useState<SVGSVGElement | null>(null);
  const session = useRef<Session | null>(null);
  const pinch = useRef<Pinch | null>(null);
  const pointers = useRef(new Map<number, ScreenPoint>());
  const spaceHeld = useRef(false);
  const [contextMenu, setContextMenu] = useState<ContextMenuState | null>(null);

  const ref = useCallback((el: SVGSVGElement | null) => {
    svg.current = el;
    setSvgElement(el);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const down = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.code === 'Space' && !isTextTarget(e.target)) spaceHeld.current = true;
    };
    const up = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.code === 'Space') spaceHeld.current = false;
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', down);
    window.addEventListener('keyup', up);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', down);
      window.removeEventListener('keyup', up);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!svgElement) return;
    const onWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const rect = svgElement.getBoundingClientRect();
      const s = useEditor.getState();
      s.setViewport(zoomAt(s.viewport, e.deltaY < 0 ? WHEEL_FACTOR : 1 / WHEEL_FACTOR, e.clientX - rect.left, e.clientY - rect.top));
    };
    svgElement.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: false });
    return () => svgElement.removeEventListener('wheel', onWheel);
  }, [svgElement]);

  const local = (e: { clientX: number; clientY: number }): ScreenPoint => {
    const rect = svg.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
    return { x: e.clientX - (rect?.left ?? 0), y: e.clientY - (rect?.top ?? 0) };
  };

  const clearTransient = () => {
    const s = useEditor.getState();
    s.setGhost(null);
    s.setWirePreview(null);
  };

  const endSession = () => {
    const current = session.current;
    if (current?.longPress) clearTimeout(current.longPress);
    session.current = null;
  };

  const openMenu = (clientX: number, clientY: number, point: GridPoint) => {
    if (!editable) return;
    const hit = hitTest(circuit, point);
    if (hit.kind !== 'pin' && hit.kind !== 'component' && hit.kind !== 'wire') return;
    useEditor.getState().select(hit.kind === 'wire' ? { kind: 'wire', id: hit.wireId } : { kind: 'component', id: hit.componentId });
    setContextMenu({ x: clientX, y: clientY, hit });
  };

  const placeArmedPart = (at: ScreenPoint) => {
    const s = useEditor.getState();
    const part = s.armedPart;
    const def = part ? getComponentDefinition(part.defId) : undefined;
    if (!part || !def) return;
    const anchor = anchorCenteredAt(def, 0, undefined, screenToLattice(s.viewport, at.x, at.y));
    if (s.run((doc) => placeComponent(doc, part.defId, anchor, part.props))) s.setArmedPart(null);
  };

  const onPointerDown = (e: ReactPointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
    if (e.button === 2) return;
    const p = local(e);
    pointers.current.set(e.pointerId, p);
    try {
      e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    } catch {
      // 合成事件或已結束的指標
    }
    setContextMenu(null);
    if (pointers.current.size >= 2) {
      endSession();
      clearTransient();
      const [[idA, a], [idB, b]] = [...pointers.current.entries()];
      pinch.current = { ids: [idA, idB], start: [a, b], startViewport: useEditor.getState().viewport };
      return;
    }
    const s = useEditor.getState();
    const point = screenToGridPoint(s.viewport, p.x, p.y, BOARD_ID);
    const panOnly = e.button === 1 || spaceHeld.current;
    let plan = PAN_ONLY;
    if (!panOnly) {
      const hit = hitTest(circuit, point);
      plan = restrictPlan(planPress(hit, hit.kind === 'hole' && isFreeHole(circuit, point)), editable, selectable);
    }
    let grab: Session['grab'] = null;
    if (plan.drag === 'move' && plan.componentId) {
      const c = circuit.components.find((x) => x.id === plan.componentId);
      const lattice = screenToLattice(s.viewport, p.x, p.y);
      if (c) grab = { dx: lattice.x - c.anchor.x, dy: lattice.y - c.anchor.y };
    }
    const { clientX, clientY } = e;
    const longPress =
      e.pointerType === 'touch' && !panOnly
        ? setTimeout(() => {
            if (session.current && !session.current.dragging) {
              endSession();
              openMenu(clientX, clientY, point);
            }
          }, LONG_PRESS_MS)
        : null;
    session.current = { pointerId: e.pointerId, start: p, plan, startPoint: point, dragging: false, grab, horizontalFirst: null, startViewport: s.viewport, longPress };
  };

  const onPointerMove = (e: ReactPointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
    if (!pointers.current.has(e.pointerId)) return;
    const p = local(e);
    pointers.current.set(e.pointerId, p);
    const pz = pinch.current;
    if (pz) {
      const a = pointers.current.get(pz.ids[0]);
      const b = pointers.current.get(pz.ids[1]);
      if (a && b) useEditor.getState().setViewport(pinchViewport(pz.startViewport, pz.start, [a, b]));
      return;
    }
    const ses = session.current;
    if (!ses || ses.pointerId !== e.pointerId) return;
    if (!ses.dragging) {
      if (Math.hypot(p.x - ses.start.x, p.y - ses.start.y) < CLICK_TOLERANCE_PX) return;
      ses.dragging = true;
      if (ses.longPress) clearTimeout(ses.longPress);
      ses.longPress = null;
    }
    const s = useEditor.getState();
    if (ses.plan.drag === 'pan') {
      s.setViewport(panBy(ses.startViewport, p.x - ses.start.x, p.y - ses.start.y));
    } else if (ses.plan.drag === 'move') {
      const c = circuit.components.find((x) => x.id === ses.plan.componentId);
      const def = c ? getComponentDefinition(c.defId) : undefined;
      if (!c || !def || !ses.grab) return;
      const lattice = screenToLattice(s.viewport, p.x, p.y);
      const instance = { ...c, anchor: snapAnchor(def, c.rotation, { x: lattice.x - ses.grab.dx, y: lattice.y - ses.grab.dy }) };
      const check = checkPlacement(circuit, instance, c.id);
      s.setGhost({ instance, valid: check.ok, reason: check.ok ? null : check.reason });
    } else {
      const lattice = screenToLattice(s.viewport, p.x, p.y);
      if (ses.horizontalFirst === null) ses.horizontalFirst = wireOrientation(lattice.x - ses.startPoint.x, lattice.y - ses.startPoint.y);
      const end = screenToGridPoint(s.viewport, p.x, p.y, BOARD_ID);
      const same = end.x === ses.startPoint.x && end.y === ses.startPoint.y;
      s.setWirePreview({
        start: ses.startPoint,
        end,
        path: lPath(ses.startPoint, end, ses.horizontalFirst ?? true),
        valid: !same && isFreeHole(circuit, end),
      });
    }
  };

  const onPointerUp = (e: ReactPointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
    pointers.current.delete(e.pointerId);
    try {
      e.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    } catch {
      // 已經釋放
    }
    if (pinch.current) {
      if (pointers.current.size < 2) pinch.current = null;
      return;
    }
    const ses = session.current;
    if (!ses || ses.pointerId !== e.pointerId) return;
    endSession();
    const s = useEditor.getState();
    if (!ses.dragging) {
      if (s.armedPart && editable) {
        placeArmedPart(ses.start);
        return;
      }
      s.select(ses.plan.click);
      if (ses.plan.click) s.setRightTab('selection');
      return;
    }
    if (ses.plan.drag === 'move') {
      const ghost = s.ghost;
      const id = ses.plan.componentId;
      s.setGhost(null);
      if (!ghost || !id) return;
      if (ghost.valid) s.run((doc) => moveComponent(doc, id, ghost.instance.anchor));
      else if (ghost.reason) s.notify(`這裡放不下：${ghost.reason}`, 'error');
    } else if (ses.plan.drag === 'wire') {
      const preview = s.wirePreview;
      s.setWirePreview(null);
      if (preview?.valid) s.run((doc) => addWire(doc, preview.start, preview.end, ses.horizontalFirst ?? true, s.wireColor));
    }
  };

  const onPointerCancel = (e: ReactPointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
    pointers.current.delete(e.pointerId);
    if (pointers.current.size < 2) pinch.current = null;
    if (session.current?.pointerId === e.pointerId) {
      endSession();
      clearTransient();
    }
  };

  const onContextMenu = (e: ReactMouseEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const p = local(e);
    openMenu(e.clientX, e.clientY, screenToGridPoint(useEditor.getState().viewport, p.x, p.y, BOARD_ID));
  };

  return {
    ref,
    handlers: { onPointerDown, onPointerMove, onPointerUp, onPointerCancel, onContextMenu },
    contextMenu,
    closeContextMenu: () => setContextMenu(null),
  };
}
```

`src/app/interaction/usePartDrag.ts`：

```ts
import { useCallback, type PointerEvent as ReactPointerEvent } from 'react';
import { getComponentDefinition } from '../../core/circuit/library';
import type { ComponentInstance } from '../../types/component';
import { placeComponent } from '../commands/commands';
import { checkPlacement } from '../geometry/placement';
import { anchorCenteredAt } from '../geometry/snap';
import type { Ghost, PartTemplate } from '../model/editorTypes';
import { BOARD_ID } from '../model/projectFile';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';
import { clientToBoardLattice } from './boardRegistry';
import { CLICK_TOLERANCE_PX } from './pressPlan';

/** 元件庫方塊：拖到板子上放置；原地點一下則拿起元件，之後點板子放置。 */
export function usePartDrag(part: PartTemplate) {
  return useCallback(
    (e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLElement>) => {
      if (e.button !== 0 || useEditor.getState().mode !== 'edit') return;
      const def = getComponentDefinition(part.defId);
      if (!def) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      const pointerId = e.pointerId;
      const start = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
      let dragging = false;

      const ghostAt = (clientX: number, clientY: number): Ghost | null => {
        const s = useEditor.getState();
        const lattice = clientToBoardLattice(clientX, clientY, s.viewport);
        if (!lattice) return null;
        const instance: ComponentInstance = {
          id: '__ghost__',
          defId: part.defId,
          boardId: BOARD_ID,
          anchor: anchorCenteredAt(def, 0, undefined, lattice),
          rotation: 0,
          props: { ...part.props },
        };
        const check = checkPlacement(s.doc.circuit, instance);
        return { instance, valid: check.ok, reason: check.ok ? null : check.reason };
      };

      const detach = () => {
        window.removeEventListener('pointermove', onMove);
        window.removeEventListener('pointerup', onUp);
        window.removeEventListener('pointercancel', onCancel);
      };

      const onMove = (ev: PointerEvent) => {
        if (ev.pointerId !== pointerId) return;
        if (!dragging && Math.hypot(ev.clientX - start.x, ev.clientY - start.y) < CLICK_TOLERANCE_PX) return;
        dragging = true;
        useEditor.getState().setGhost(ghostAt(ev.clientX, ev.clientY));
      };

      const onUp = (ev: PointerEvent) => {
        if (ev.pointerId !== pointerId) return;
        detach();
        const s = useEditor.getState();
        if (!dragging) {
          s.setArmedPart(part);
          s.notify(`點一下麵包板放置 ${part.label}`, 'info');
          return;
        }
        const ghost = ghostAt(ev.clientX, ev.clientY);
        s.setGhost(null);
        if (!ghost) return;
        if (!ghost.valid) {
          s.notify(`這裡放不下：${ghost.reason}`, 'error');
          return;
        }
        s.run((doc) => placeComponent(doc, part.defId, ghost.instance.anchor, part.props));
      };

      const onCancel = (ev: PointerEvent) => {
        if (ev.pointerId !== pointerId) return;
        detach();
        useEditor.getState().setGhost(null);
      };

      window.addEventListener('pointermove', onMove);
      window.addEventListener('pointerup', onUp);
      window.addEventListener('pointercancel', onCancel);
    },
    [part],
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 實作畫布**

`src/app/board/BoardCanvas.tsx`：

```tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type RefObject } from 'react';
import type { Circuit } from '../../types/circuit';
import { registerBoardElement } from '../interaction/boardRegistry';
import { ContextMenu } from '../interaction/ContextMenu';
import { useBoardInteractions } from '../interaction/useBoardInteractions';
import { fitViewport, zoomAt } from '../interaction/viewport';
import { isRoutingStale } from '../model/projectFile';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';
import { BoardSvg, type OverlayData } from './BoardSvg';
import { invalidWireIds, markersFromErrors } from './markers';
import { netHighlightFor } from './netHighlight';

function useElementSize(ref: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>): { width: number; height: number } {
  const [size, setSize] = useState({ width: 0, height: 0 });
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const update = () => setSize({ width: el.clientWidth, height: el.clientHeight });
    update();
    if (typeof ResizeObserver === 'undefined') return;
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(update);
    observer.observe(el);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [ref]);
  return size;
}

function ZoomButton({ label, text, onClick }: { label: string; text: string; onClick: () => void }) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      aria-label={label}
      title={label}
      onClick={onClick}
      className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-gray-300 bg-white text-lg shadow-sm hover:bg-gray-50 lg:h-8 lg:w-8 lg:text-base"
    >
      {text}
    </button>
  );
}

export interface BoardCanvasProps {
  /** 要顯示的電路；省略時顯示目前文件，並在編輯模式下可以編輯。 */
  circuit?: Circuit;
  label?: string;
  selectable?: boolean;
  registerAsMain?: boolean;
}

export function BoardCanvas({ circuit, label, selectable = true, registerAsMain = false }: BoardCanvasProps) {
  const doc = useEditor((s) => s.doc);
  const mode = useEditor((s) => s.mode);
  const viewport = useEditor((s) => s.viewport);
  const selection = useEditor((s) => s.selection);
  const ghost = useEditor((s) => s.ghost);
  const wirePreview = useEditor((s) => s.wirePreview);
  const wireColor = useEditor((s) => s.wireColor);
  const previewWires = useEditor((s) => s.previewWires);
  const focusWireId = useEditor((s) => s.focusWireId);
  const liveCheck = useEditor((s) => s.analysis.liveCheck);
  const routingJob = useEditor((s) => s.routingJob);
  const armed = useEditor((s) => s.armedPart !== null);

  const showsDocument = circuit === undefined;
  const shown = circuit ?? doc.circuit;
  const editable = showsDocument && mode === 'edit';
  const { ref, handlers, contextMenu, closeContextMenu } = useBoardInteractions({ circuit: shown, editable, selectable });

  const setSvg = useCallback(
    (el: SVGSVGElement | null) => {
      ref(el);
      if (registerAsMain) registerBoardElement(el);
    },
    [ref, registerAsMain],
  );

  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const size = useElementSize(containerRef);
  useEffect(() => {
    const v = useEditor.getState().viewport;
    const isDefault = v.scale === 1 && v.tx === 0 && v.ty === 0;
    if (size.width > 0 && size.height > 0 && isDefault) useEditor.getState().setViewport(fitViewport(size.width, size.height));
  }, [size.width, size.height]);

  const net = useMemo(() => (showsDocument ? netHighlightFor(selection, shown) : null), [showsDocument, selection, shown]);
  const markers = useMemo(() => (showsDocument ? markersFromErrors(liveCheck, shown) : []), [showsDocument, liveCheck, shown]);
  const invalid = useMemo(() => (showsDocument ? invalidWireIds(liveCheck) : undefined), [showsDocument, liveCheck]);
  const dimmed = useMemo(() => {
    if (!showsDocument) return undefined;
    if (focusWireId) return new Set(shown.wires.filter((w) => w.id !== focusWireId).map((w) => w.id));
    if (!doc.routing || isRoutingStale(doc)) return undefined;
    return new Set(
      Object.entries(doc.routing.checklist)
        .filter(([, done]) => done)
        .map(([id]) => id),
    );
  }, [showsDocument, focusWireId, shown, doc]);
  const animation = useMemo(
    () => (showsDocument && routingJob.status === 'animating' ? { order: routingJob.wireIds, stepMs: routingJob.stepMs } : null),
    [showsDocument, routingJob],
  );
  const fading = animation && doc.routing ? doc.routing.before : undefined;
  const overlay: OverlayData | null = showsDocument ? { selection, net, markers, ghost, wirePreview, wireColor, previewWires, focusWireId } : null;

  const zoomBy = (factor: number) => {
    const s = useEditor.getState();
    s.setViewport(zoomAt(s.viewport, factor, size.width / 2, size.height / 2));
  };
  const fit = () => useEditor.getState().setViewport(fitViewport(size.width, size.height));

  return (
    <div ref={containerRef} className={`relative h-full w-full overflow-hidden ${armed ? 'cursor-copy' : ''}`}>
      <BoardSvg
        svgRef={setSvg}
        circuit={shown}
        viewport={viewport}
        invalidWireIds={invalid}
        dimmedWireIds={dimmed}
        animation={animation}
        fadingWires={fading}
        overlay={overlay}
        handlers={handlers}
        label={label}
      />
      {label && <div className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-3 rounded bg-white/90 px-2 py-1 text-sm font-semibold shadow">{label}</div>}
      <div className="absolute bottom-3 right-3 flex gap-1">
        <ZoomButton label="放大" text="＋" onClick={() => zoomBy(1.25)} />
        <ZoomButton label="縮小" text="－" onClick={() => zoomBy(1 / 1.25)} />
        <ZoomButton label="符合畫面" text="⤢" onClick={fit} />
        <ZoomButton label="重設" text="⟲" onClick={fit} />
      </div>
      {contextMenu && <ContextMenu menu={contextMenu} onClose={closeContextMenu} />}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';

describe('InfoPanel — check results', () => {

describe('InfoPanel — checklist', () => {

    state().run((doc) => applyRoutingResult(doc, autoRoute(doc.circuit), 0));
    state().setRightTab('checklist');

  it('explains what appears here before routing', () => {

  it('ticks wires off and tracks progress', () => {
    routed();
    render(<InfoPanel />);
    expect(screen.getByText('已完成 0 / 7')).toBeTruthy();
    const boxes = screen.getAllByRole('checkbox');
    expect(boxes).toHaveLength(7);
    fireEvent.click(boxes[0]);
    expect(screen.getByText('已完成 1 / 7')).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '全部清除' }));
    expect(screen.getByText('已完成 0 / 7')).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('warns and disables ticking when the circuit changed after routing', () => {
    routed();
    state().run((doc) => placeComponent(doc, 'led', { x: 50, y: 4 }));
    state().setRightTab('checklist');
    render(<InfoPanel />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert').textContent).toContain('接線清單已失效');
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('checkbox')[0]).toHaveProperty('disabled', true);
  });

  it('switches tabs', () => {
    render(<InfoPanel />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: '檢查結果' }));
    expect(state().rightTab).toBe('check');
  });
});
```

```ts
import type { ComponentCategory, LedColor, PinRole } from '../../types/component';

const trim = (v: number) => String(Math.round(v * 100) / 100);

export function formatOhms(ohms: number): string {
  if (ohms >= 1_000_000) return `${trim(ohms / 1_000_000)}MΩ`;
  if (ohms >= 1000) return `${trim(ohms / 1000)}kΩ`;
  return `${trim(ohms)}Ω`;
}

export const ROLE_NAMES: Record<PinRole, string> = {
  input: '輸入',
  output: '輸出',
  bidir: '雙向',
  power: '電源',
  ground: '接地',
  nc: '未使用',
  passive: '被動',
  anode: '陽極',
  cathode: '陰極',
  'vcc-source': '電源正極',
  'gnd-source': '電源負極',
};

export const CATEGORY_NAMES: Record<ComponentCategory, string> = {
  ic: 'IC',
  resistor: '電阻',
  led: 'LED',
  vcc: 'VCC 端子',
  gnd: 'GND 端子',
  'dc-power': 'DC 電源',
  'dip-switch': '指撥開關',
  pushbutton: '按鈕',
};

export const LED_COLOR_NAMES: Record<LedColor, string> = { red: '紅色', green: '綠色', yellow: '黃色', blue: '藍色' };
```

`src/app/panels/focus.ts`：

```ts
import { getBoardElement } from '../interaction/boardRegistry';
import { latticeToScreen, panBy } from '../interaction/viewport';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';

/** 把格點移到主畫面中央；畫布還沒有尺寸時不動作。 */
export function centerViewportOn(point: { x: number; y: number }): void {
  const el = getBoardElement();
  if (!el) return;
  const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (rect.width === 0 || rect.height === 0) return;
  const s = useEditor.getState();
  const at = latticeToScreen(s.viewport, point.x, point.y);
  s.setViewport(panBy(s.viewport, rect.width / 2 - at.x, rect.height / 2 - at.y));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 實作三個頁籤與面板**

`src/app/panels/SelectionInfo.tsx`：

```tsx
import { useMemo, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { holeName } from '../../core/breadboard/naming';
import { getBoard } from '../../core/breadboard/registry';
import { effectiveSpan, resolveFootprint } from '../../core/circuit/footprint';
import { getComponentDefinition, RESISTOR_PRESETS } from '../../core/circuit/library';
import { extractNets } from '../../core/circuit/nets';
import { getNodeState } from '../../core/circuit/nodeState';
import { describeGroup } from '../../core/routing/problem';
import type { LedColor } from '../../types/component';
import type { GridPoint } from '../../types/geometry';
import { netHighlightFor } from '../board/netHighlight';
import { deleteComponent, deleteWire, flipWire, rotateComponent, updateComponentProps, type CommandResult, type PropsPatch } from '../commands/commands';
import { flippedPath } from '../geometry/wirePath';
import { colorName } from '../model/colors';
import type { ProjectFile } from '../model/projectFile';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';
import { CATEGORY_NAMES, formatOhms, LED_COLOR_NAMES, ROLE_NAMES } from './format';

const board = getBoard('MB102', 'b1');
const nameOf = (p: GridPoint) => holeName(board, p) ?? `(${p.x}, ${p.y})`;
const run = (command: (doc: ProjectFile) => CommandResult) => useEditor.getState().run(command);

function Row({ label, children }: { label: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex justify-between gap-3 py-1 text-sm">
      <dt className="text-gray-500">{label}</dt>
      <dd className="text-right font-medium">{children}</dd>
    </div>
  );
}

function ActionButton({ children, onClick, disabled, label }: { children: ReactNode; onClick: () => void; disabled?: boolean; label?: string }) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      aria-label={label}
      disabled={disabled}
      onClick={onClick}
      className="min-h-11 min-w-11 rounded-md border border-gray-300 bg-white px-3 text-sm hover:bg-gray-50 disabled:opacity-40 lg:min-h-8 lg:min-w-8"
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

function ComponentInfo({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const circuit = useEditor((s) => s.doc.circuit);
  const editable = useEditor((s) => s.mode === 'edit');
  const instance = circuit.components.find((c) => c.id === id);
  const def = instance ? getComponentDefinition(instance.defId) : undefined;
  if (!instance || !def) return null;
  const props = { ...def.defaultProps, ...instance.props };
  const pin1 = resolveFootprint(instance, def).pins[0].point;
  const update = (patch: PropsPatch) => run((doc) => updateComponentProps(doc, id, patch));
  const span = effectiveSpan(instance, def);
  const pkg = def.package;
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <h3 className="text-base font-semibold">{`${def.name}（${instance.id}）`}</h3>
      <dl>
        <Row label="類型">{CATEGORY_NAMES[def.category]}</Row>
        <Row label="第 1 腳位置">{nameOf(pin1)}</Row>
        <Row label="旋轉">{`${instance.rotation}°`}</Row>
      </dl>
      {def.category === 'resistor' && (
        <label className="block text-sm">
          電阻值
          <select
            aria-label="電阻值"
            disabled={!editable}
            value={props.resistance ?? 1000}
            onChange={(e) => update({ resistance: Number(e.target.value) })}
            className="mt-1 block w-full rounded border border-gray-300 p-2"
          >
            {RESISTOR_PRESETS.map((ohms) => (
              <option key={ohms} value={ohms}>
                {formatOhms(ohms)}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
      )}
      {def.category === 'led' && (
        <label className="block text-sm">
          LED 顏色
          <select
            aria-label="LED 顏色"
            disabled={!editable}
            value={props.color ?? 'red'}
            onChange={(e) => update({ color: e.target.value as LedColor })}
            className="mt-1 block w-full rounded border border-gray-300 p-2"
          >
            {(Object.keys(LED_COLOR_NAMES) as LedColor[]).map((color) => (
              <option key={color} value={color}>
                {LED_COLOR_NAMES[color]}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
      )}
      {pkg.type === 'two-lead' && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
          <span>兩腳距離</span>
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <ActionButton label="縮短兩腳距離" disabled={!editable || span <= pkg.minSpan} onClick={() => update({ span: span - 1 })}>
              －
            </ActionButton>
            <span>{`${span} 格`}</span>
            <ActionButton label="加長兩腳距離" disabled={!editable} onClick={() => update({ span: span + 1 })}>
              ＋
            </ActionButton>
          </span>
        </div>
      )}
      {(def.category === 'vcc' || def.category === 'dc-power') && (
        <label className="block text-sm">
          電壓（V）
          <input
            aria-label="電壓"
            type="number"
            min={0.5}
            step={0.5}
            disabled={!editable}
            value={props.voltage ?? 5}
            onChange={(e) => {
              const v = Number(e.target.value);
              if (v > 0) update({ voltage: v });
            }}
            className="mt-1 block w-full rounded border border-gray-300 p-2"
          />
        </label>
      )}
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <ActionButton disabled={!editable} onClick={() => run((doc) => rotateComponent(doc, id))}>
          旋轉 90°
        </ActionButton>
        <ActionButton disabled={!editable} onClick={() => run((doc) => deleteComponent(doc, id))}>
          刪除
        </ActionButton>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function PinInfo({ componentId, pinNumber }: { componentId: string; pinNumber: number }) {
  const circuit = useEditor((s) => s.doc.circuit);
  const net = useMemo(
    () => extractNets(circuit).find((n) => n.pins.some((p) => p.componentId === componentId && p.pinNumber === pinNumber)),
    [circuit, componentId, pinNumber],
  );
  const instance = circuit.components.find((c) => c.id === componentId);
  const def = instance ? getComponentDefinition(instance.defId) : undefined;
  const pin = def?.pins.find((p) => p.number === pinNumber);
  const point = instance && def ? resolveFootprint(instance, def).pins.find((p) => p.pinNumber === pinNumber)?.point : undefined;
  if (!instance || !def || !pin || !point) return null;
  const pinLabel = (cid: string, n: number) => {
    const other = circuit.components.find((c) => c.id === cid);
    const otherDef = other ? getComponentDefinition(other.defId) : undefined;
    const otherPin = otherDef?.pins.find((p) => p.number === n);
    return `${cid} 第 ${n} 腳（${otherPin?.name ?? '?'}）`;
  };
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <h3 className="text-base font-semibold">{`${def.name}（${instance.id}）`}</h3>
      <dl>
        <Row label="腳號">{`第 ${pinNumber} 腳`}</Row>
        <Row label="腳名">{pin.name}</Row>
        <Row label="角色">{ROLE_NAMES[pin.role]}</Row>
        <Row label="孔">{nameOf(point)}</Row>
        <Row label="Net">{net?.name ?? '（未連接）'}</Row>
      </dl>
      {net && (
        <div>
          <h4 className="mb-1 text-sm font-semibold">這個 Net 的腳</h4>
          <ul className="space-y-0.5 text-sm">
            {net.pins
              .filter((p) => !(p.componentId === componentId && p.pinNumber === pinNumber))
              .map((p) => (
                <li key={`${p.componentId}#${p.pinNumber}`}>{pinLabel(p.componentId, p.pinNumber)}</li>
              ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function WireInfo({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const circuit = useEditor((s) => s.doc.circuit);
  const editable = useEditor((s) => s.mode === 'edit');
  const net = useMemo(() => netHighlightFor({ kind: 'wire', id }, circuit), [circuit, id]);
  const wire = circuit.wires.find((w) => w.id === id);
  if (!wire) return null;
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <h3 className="text-base font-semibold">{`導線 ${wire.id}`}</h3>
      <dl>
        <Row label="起點">{nameOf(wire.start)}</Row>
        <Row label="終點">{nameOf(wire.end)}</Row>
        <Row label="長度">{`${wire.path.length - 1} 格`}</Row>
        <Row label="顏色">{colorName(wire.color)}</Row>
        <Row label="Net">{net?.netId ?? '（沒有接到元件）'}</Row>
        <Row label="來源">{wire.source === 'auto' ? 'AI 標準佈線' : '手動'}</Row>
      </dl>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <ActionButton disabled={!editable || flippedPath(wire) === null} onClick={() => run((doc) => flipWire(doc, id))}>
          翻轉轉角
        </ActionButton>
        <ActionButton disabled={!editable} onClick={() => run((doc) => deleteWire(doc, id))}>
          刪除
        </ActionButton>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function HoleInfo({ point }: { point: GridPoint }) {
  const circuit = useEditor((s) => s.doc.circuit);
  const state = getNodeState(circuit, point);
  if (!state) return null;
  let content = '（空孔）';
  if (state.pin) content = `${state.pin.componentId} 第 ${state.pin.pinNumber} 腳`;
  else if (state.wireEnds.length > 0) content = `導線 ${state.wireEnds.join('、')} 的端點`;
  else if (state.blockedBy.length > 0) content = `${state.blockedBy.join('、')} 的本體下方`;
  return (
    <dl>
      <Row label="孔">{state.name ?? '（板面，沒有孔）'}</Row>
      <Row label="導通群組">{state.groupId ? describeGroup(state.groupId) : '—'}</Row>
      <Row label="插在這裡">{content}</Row>
    </dl>
  );
}

export function SelectionInfo() {
  const selection = useEditor((s) => s.selection);
  if (!selection) return <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">點一下元件、腳位、導線或孔，就會在這裡顯示資訊。</p>;
  switch (selection.kind) {
    case 'component':
      return <ComponentInfo id={selection.id} />;
    case 'pin':
      return <PinInfo componentId={selection.componentId} pinNumber={selection.pinNumber} />;
    case 'wire':
      return <WireInfo id={selection.id} />;
    case 'hole':
      return <HoleInfo point={selection.point} />;
  }
}
```

`src/app/panels/CheckResults.tsx`：

```tsx
import type { CircuitError } from '../../types/circuit';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';
import { centerViewportOn } from './focus';

function focusError(error: CircuitError): void {
  const s = useEditor.getState();
  if (error.componentIds[0]) s.select({ kind: 'component', id: error.componentIds[0] });
  else if (error.wireIds[0]) s.select({ kind: 'wire', id: error.wireIds[0] });
  if (error.points[0]) centerViewportOn(error.points[0]);
}

function Group({ title, items }: { title: string; items: CircuitError[] }) {
  if (items.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <section>
      <h3 className="mb-1 text-sm font-semibold">{title}</h3>
      <ul className="space-y-1">
        {items.map((e, i) => (
          <li key={`${e.code}-${i}`}>
            <button type="button" className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-md px-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-gray-100 lg:min-h-0 lg:py-1.5" onClick={() => focusError(e)}>
              {e.message}
            </button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}

export function CheckResults() {
  const liveCheck = useEditor((s) => s.analysis.liveCheck);
  const errors = liveCheck.filter((e) => e.severity === 'error');
  const warnings = liveCheck.filter((e) => e.severity === 'warning');
  if (errors.length === 0 && warnings.length === 0) {
    return <p className="rounded-md bg-green-50 p-3 text-sm font-medium text-green-700">✓ Circuit Valid：電路檢查通過</p>;
  }
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <Group title={`🔴 錯誤（${errors.length}）`} items={errors} />
      <Group title={`🟡 警告（${warnings.length}）`} items={warnings} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/app/panels/Checklist.tsx`：

```tsx
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { isRoutingStale } from '../model/projectFile';
import { checklistOrder, type ChecklistRow } from '../routing/checklist';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';
import { centerViewportOn } from './focus';

export function Checklist() {
  const doc = useEditor((s) => s.doc);
  const focusWireId = useEditor((s) => s.focusWireId);
  const rows = useMemo(() => checklistOrder(doc.circuit), [doc.circuit]);
  const routing = doc.routing;
  if (!routing) return <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">執行 AI 標準佈線後，這裡會列出照著插的接線順序。</p>;
  const stale = isRoutingStale(doc);
  const done = rows.filter((r) => routing.checklist[r.wireId]).length;

  const toggleFocus = (row: ChecklistRow) => {
    const s = useEditor.getState();
    const next = s.focusWireId === row.wireId ? null : row.wireId;
    s.setFocusWire(next);
    const wire = doc.circuit.wires.find((w) => w.id === row.wireId);
    if (next && wire) centerViewportOn(wire.path[Math.floor(wire.path.length / 2)]);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      {stale && (
        <p role="alert" className="rounded-md bg-amber-50 p-2 text-sm text-amber-800">
          電路已修改，接線清單已失效，請重新執行 AI 標準佈線
        </p>
      )}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
        <span>{`已完成 ${done} / ${rows.length}`}</span>
        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={stale}
          onClick={() => useEditor.getState().clearChecklist()}
          className="min-h-11 rounded px-2 text-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-50 disabled:opacity-40 lg:min-h-0 lg:py-1"
        >
          全部清除
        </button>
      </div>
      <ol className="space-y-1">
        {rows.map((row) => {
          const checked = routing.checklist[row.wireId] === true;
          return (
            <li
              key={row.wireId}
              className={`flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 ${focusWireId === row.wireId ? 'border-blue-400 bg-blue-50' : 'border-transparent'}`}
            >
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                className="h-5 w-5"
                aria-label={`${row.from} → ${row.to} 已插好`}
                checked={checked}
                disabled={stale}
                onChange={(e) => useEditor.getState().setChecklistItem(row.wireId, e.target.checked)}
              />
              <button type="button" onClick={() => toggleFocus(row)} className={`flex min-h-11 flex-1 items-center gap-2 text-left text-sm lg:min-h-0 ${checked ? 'opacity-50' : ''}`}>
                <span className="inline-block h-3 w-3 rounded-sm border border-gray-400" style={{ background: row.color }} />
                <span className="w-6">{row.colorName}</span>
                <span className="flex-1 font-mono">{`${row.from} → ${row.to}`}</span>
                <span className="text-gray-500">{`${row.length} 格`}</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">{row.netId}</span>
              </button>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ol>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/app/panels/InfoPanel.tsx`：

```tsx
import type { RightTab } from '../model/editorTypes';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';
import { CheckResults } from './CheckResults';
import { Checklist } from './Checklist';
import { SelectionInfo } from './SelectionInfo';

const TABS: Array<{ id: RightTab; label: string }> = [
  { id: 'selection', label: '選取' },
  { id: 'check', label: '檢查結果' },
  { id: 'checklist', label: '接線清單' },
];

export function InfoPanel() {
  const tab = useEditor((s) => s.rightTab);
  const setTab = useEditor((s) => s.setRightTab);
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col bg-white">
      <div role="tablist" className="flex border-b border-gray-200">
        {TABS.map((t) => (
          <button
            key={t.id}
            type="button"
            role="tab"
            aria-selected={tab === t.id}
            onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}
            className={`min-h-11 flex-1 px-2 text-sm lg:min-h-9 ${tab === t.id ? 'border-b-2 border-blue-600 font-semibold text-blue-700' : 'text-gray-600'}`}
          >
            {t.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div role="tabpanel" className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-3">
        {tab === 'selection' && <SelectionInfo />}
        {tab === 'check' && <CheckResults />}
        {tab === 'checklist' && <Checklist />}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 15: 佈線畫面：統計卡、失敗說明、橫幅、動畫計時、比較模式

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/routing/useRoutingAnimation.ts`、`src/app/panels/RoutingStatus.tsx`、`src/app/panels/Banners.tsx`、`src/app/board/CompareView.tsx`
- Test: `tests/app/routingUi.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6 的 `useEditor`；Task 13 的 `BoardCanvas`；核心的 `extractNets`；`FailureReason`
- Produces:
  - `useRoutingAnimation()`：`routingJob` 為 `animating` 時，經過 `導線數 × stepMs + 300` ms 後改成 `done`
  - `StatsCard()`：`Standard Routing Complete`、Wire Count、Total Length、Bends、Crossings、Overlaps、Routing Score、`你的接線` 分數；按鈕 `略過動畫`、`關閉`
  - `RoutingProblems()`：失敗時顯示訊息、每個失敗 Net 的中文原因、提示、`關閉預覽`、`關閉`；錯誤時顯示錯誤訊息（`role="alert"`）
  - `CheckBanner()`（檢查通過後顯示 3 秒）、`NoticeToast()`（通知 3 秒後自動消失）
  - `SIDE_BY_SIDE_MIN_WIDTH = 1280`、`compareLayout(width): 'side-by-side' | 'toggle'`、`CompareView()`（按鈕 `你的接線`、`AI 標準佈線`、`離開比較`）

```tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { act, cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { compareLayout, CompareView } from '../../src/app/board/CompareView';
import { applyRoutingResult } from '../../src/app/commands/commands';
import { createProject } from '../../src/app/model/projectFile';
import { CheckBanner, NoticeToast } from '../../src/app/panels/Banners';
import { RoutingProblems, StatsCard } from '../../src/app/panels/RoutingStatus';
import { useRoutingAnimation } from '../../src/app/routing/useRoutingAnimation';
import { resetEditor, useEditor } from '../../src/app/store/editorStore';
import { autoRoute } from '../../src/core/routing/autoRoute';
import { wire } from '../fixtures/build';
import { acceptanceCircuit } from '../fixtures/circuits';

afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  vi.useRealTimers();
});
beforeEach(() => resetEditor());

const state = () => useEditor.getState();
const loadAcceptance = () =>
  state().replaceDocument({ ...createProject(), circuit: acceptanceCircuit() }, { projectId: null, undoable: false, dirty: false });
const routed = () => {
  loadAcceptance();
  state().run((doc) => applyRoutingResult(doc, autoRoute(doc.circuit), 12.5));
};
const setWidth = (width: number) => Object.defineProperty(window, 'innerWidth', { value: width, configurable: true, writable: true });

describe('StatsCard', () => {
  it('shows the statistics and both scores, and can skip or close', () => {
    routed();
    state().setRoutingJob({ status: 'animating', wireIds: ['a'], stepMs: 250 });
    render(<StatsCard />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Standard Routing Complete')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText('Wire Count')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText(String(state().doc.routing!.score))).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText('12.5')).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '略過動畫' }));
    expect(state().routingJob).toEqual({ status: 'done' });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '關閉' }));
    expect(state().routingJob).toEqual({ status: 'idle' });

  it('stays hidden when nothing was routed', () => {
    const { container } = render(<StatsCard />);
    expect(container.textContent).toBe('');
  });
});

describe('RoutingProblems', () => {
  it('explains failures in Chinese and highlights a failed net', () => {
    loadAcceptance();
    state().setPreviewWires([wire('w_NET_1_1', 'C20', 'C30')]);
    state().setRoutingJob({
      status: 'failed',
      message: 'No valid routing solution.',
      failedNets: [{ netId: 'NET_1', reason: 'NO_FREE_HOLE', detail: '第 20 欄上半（A–E）沒有空孔可以接線' }],
    });
    render(<RoutingProblems />);
    expect(screen.getByText('No valid routing solution.')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText('NET_1：沒有空孔（第 20 欄上半（A–E）沒有空孔可以接線）')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText('試著把元件分散一點，或移開擋路的元件')).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText(/^NET_1：/));
    expect(state().selection).toMatchObject({ kind: 'pin', componentId: 'D1' });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '關閉預覽' }));
    expect(state().previewWires).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('shows worker errors', () => {
    state().setRoutingJob({ status: 'error', message: '佈線時發生錯誤：boom' });
    render(<RoutingProblems />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert').textContent).toContain('佈線時發生錯誤：boom');
  });
});

describe('banners and timers', () => {
  it('shows the green check banner for 3 seconds', () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    render(<CheckBanner />);
    act(() => state().runCheck());
    expect(screen.getByText('✓ Circuit Valid：電路檢查通過')).toBeTruthy();
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(3000));
    expect(screen.queryByText('✓ Circuit Valid：電路檢查通過')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('dismisses notices after 3 seconds', () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    render(<NoticeToast />);
    act(() => state().notify('這裡放不下：第 1 腳沒有插在孔上', 'error'));
    expect(screen.getByText('這裡放不下：第 1 腳沒有插在孔上')).toBeTruthy();
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(3000));
    expect(state().notice).toBeNull();
  });

  it('finishes the routing animation after every wire has been drawn', () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    function Host() {
      useRoutingAnimation();
      return null;
    }
    render(<Host />);
    act(() => state().setRoutingJob({ status: 'animating', wireIds: ['a', 'b'], stepMs: 100 }));
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(499));
    expect(state().routingJob.status).toBe('animating');
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(1));
    expect(state().routingJob).toEqual({ status: 'done' });
  });
});

describe('compare mode', () => {
  it('puts the boards side by side from 1280 px', () => {
    expect(compareLayout(1280)).toBe('side-by-side');
    expect(compareLayout(1279)).toBe('toggle');
  });

  it('lets narrow screens switch between the two wirings', () => {
    routed();
    setWidth(800);
    render(<CompareView />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/AI 標準佈線（Routing Score/)).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '你的接線' }));
    expect(state().compareView).toBe('mine');
    expect(screen.getByText('你的接線（Routing Score 12.5）')).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '離開比較' }));
    expect(state().mode).toBe('edit');
  });

  it('shows both boards on wide screens', () => {
    routed();
    setWidth(1400);
    render(<CompareView />);
    expect(screen.getAllByTestId('board-svg')).toHaveLength(2);
  });
});
```

`src/app/routing/useRoutingAnimation.ts`：

```ts
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';

/** 動畫播完（每條導線 stepMs，再加 0.3 秒淡出）後把佈線工作改成 done。 */
export function useRoutingAnimation(): void {
  const job = useEditor((s) => s.routingJob);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (job.status !== 'animating') return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (useEditor.getState().routingJob === job) useEditor.getState().setRoutingJob({ status: 'done' });
    }, job.wireIds.length * job.stepMs + 300);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [job]);
}
```

`src/app/panels/RoutingStatus.tsx`：

```tsx
import { extractNets } from '../../core/circuit/nets';
import type { FailureReason } from '../../types/routing';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';

const REASON_TEXT: Record<FailureReason, string> = {
  NO_FREE_HOLE: '沒有空孔',
  UNROUTABLE: '找不到路徑',
  ITERATION_LIMIT: '板子太擠',
  TIME_LIMIT: '板子太擠',
  LEGALITY_CHECK: '結果沒通過檢查',
};

const CARD = 'absolute left-1/2 top-3 z-30 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-lg border bg-white p-3 shadow-lg';

function CloseButton({ onClick }: { onClick: () => void }) {
  return (
    <button type="button" aria-label="關閉" onClick={onClick} className="min-h-11 min-w-11 rounded text-sm hover:bg-gray-100 lg:min-h-0 lg:min-w-0 lg:px-2 lg:py-1">
      ✕
    </button>
  );
}

export function StatsCard() {
  const job = useEditor((s) => s.routingJob);
  const routing = useEditor((s) => s.doc.routing);
  if ((job.status !== 'animating' && job.status !== 'done') || !routing) return null;
  const m = routing.metrics;
  const rows: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['Wire Count', String(m.wireCount)],
    ['Total Length', `${m.totalLength} holes`],
    ['Bends', String(m.bends)],
    ['Crossings', String(m.crossings)],
    ['Overlaps', String(m.overlaps)],
  ];
  return (
    <div role="status" className={`${CARD} w-[min(92vw,360px)] border-green-200`}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <strong className="text-green-700">Standard Routing Complete</strong>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
          {job.status === 'animating' && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => useEditor.getState().setRoutingJob({ status: 'done' })}
              className="min-h-11 rounded px-2 text-sm text-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-50 lg:min-h-0 lg:py-1"
            >
              略過動畫
            </button>
          )}
          <CloseButton onClick={() => useEditor.getState().setRoutingJob({ status: 'idle' })} />
        </div>
      </div>
      <dl className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-sm">
        {rows.map(([label, value]) => (
          <div key={label} className="contents">
            <dt>{label}</dt>
            <dd className="text-right">{value}</dd>
          </div>
        ))}
        <dt className="font-semibold">Routing Score</dt>
        <dd className="text-right font-semibold">{routing.score}</dd>
        <dt className="text-gray-500">你的接線</dt>
        <dd className="text-right text-gray-500">{routing.beforeScore}</dd>
      </dl>
    </div>
  );
}

export function RoutingProblems() {
  const job = useEditor((s) => s.routingJob);
  const previewCount = useEditor((s) => s.previewWires.length);
  const circuit = useEditor((s) => s.doc.circuit);
  const close = () => {
    const s = useEditor.getState();
    s.setRoutingJob({ status: 'idle' });
    s.setPreviewWires([]);
  };
  if (job.status === 'error') {
    return (
      <div role="alert" className={`${CARD} w-[min(92vw,420px)] border-red-200`}>
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
          <strong className="text-red-700">{job.message}</strong>
          <CloseButton onClick={close} />
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (job.status !== 'failed') return null;
  const focusNet = (netId: string) => {
    const first = extractNets(circuit).find((n) => n.id === netId)?.pins[0];
    if (first) useEditor.getState().select({ kind: 'pin', componentId: first.componentId, pinNumber: first.pinNumber });
  };
  return (
    <div role="alert" className={`${CARD} w-[min(92vw,420px)] border-red-200`}>
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
        <strong className="text-red-700">{job.message}</strong>
        <CloseButton onClick={close} />
      </div>
      <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-sm">
        {job.failedNets.map((f) => (
          <li key={f.netId}>
            <button type="button" className="w-full text-left hover:underline" onClick={() => focusNet(f.netId)}>
              {`${f.netId}：${REASON_TEXT[f.reason]}（${f.detail}）`}
            </button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-600">試著把元件分散一點，或移開擋路的元件</p>
      {previewCount > 0 && (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => useEditor.getState().setPreviewWires([])}
          className="mt-2 min-h-11 rounded border border-gray-300 px-2 text-sm hover:bg-gray-50 lg:min-h-0 lg:py-1"
        >
          關閉預覽
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/app/panels/Banners.tsx`：

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';

/** 電路檢查通過時顯示 3 秒的綠色橫幅。 */
export function CheckBanner() {
  const passedAt = useEditor((s) => s.checkPassedAt);
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (passedAt === null) {
      setVisible(false);
      return;
    }
    setVisible(true);
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setVisible(false), 3000);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [passedAt]);
  if (!visible) return null;
  return (
    <div role="status" className="absolute left-1/2 top-3 z-30 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-md bg-green-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow-lg">
      ✓ Circuit Valid：電路檢查通過
    </div>
  );
}

/** 通知訊息，3 秒後自動消失。 */
export function NoticeToast() {
  const notice = useEditor((s) => s.notice);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!notice) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => useEditor.getState().dismissNotice(), 3000);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [notice]);
  if (!notice) return null;
  const tone = notice.tone === 'error' ? 'bg-red-600' : notice.tone === 'success' ? 'bg-green-600' : 'bg-gray-800';
  return (
    <div role="status" className={`fixed bottom-12 left-1/2 z-50 max-w-[90vw] -translate-x-1/2 rounded-md px-4 py-2 text-sm text-white shadow-lg ${tone}`}>
      {notice.text}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/app/board/CompareView.tsx`：

```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';
import { BoardCanvas } from './BoardCanvas';

export const SIDE_BY_SIDE_MIN_WIDTH = 1280;

export function compareLayout(width: number): 'side-by-side' | 'toggle' {
  return width >= SIDE_BY_SIDE_MIN_WIDTH ? 'side-by-side' : 'toggle';
}

function useWindowWidth(): number {
  const [width, setWidth] = useState(() => window.innerWidth);
  useEffect(() => {
    const onResize = () => setWidth(window.innerWidth);
    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
  }, []);
  return width;
}

const BUTTON = 'min-h-11 rounded-md px-3 text-sm lg:min-h-8';

/** 規格第 10 節：左「你的接線」、右「AI 標準佈線」；窄螢幕改用切換鈕。 */
export function CompareView() {
  const doc = useEditor((s) => s.doc);
  const view = useEditor((s) => s.compareView);
  const width = useWindowWidth();
  const mine = useMemo(() => (doc.routing ? { ...doc.circuit, wires: doc.routing.before } : null), [doc]);
  if (!mine || !doc.routing) return null;
  const mineLabel = `你的接線（Routing Score ${doc.routing.beforeScore}）`;
  const aiLabel = `AI 標準佈線（Routing Score ${doc.routing.score}）`;
  const exit = (
    <button type="button" onClick={() => useEditor.getState().setMode('edit')} className={`${BUTTON} border border-gray-300 bg-white hover:bg-gray-50`}>
      離開比較
    </button>
  );
  if (compareLayout(width) === 'side-by-side') {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
        <div className="flex justify-end border-b border-gray-200 bg-white p-2">{exit}</div>
        <div className="grid min-h-0 flex-1 grid-cols-2 gap-1">
          <BoardCanvas circuit={mine} label={mineLabel} selectable={false} />
          <BoardCanvas label={aiLabel} registerAsMain />
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }
  const toggle = (target: 'mine' | 'ai', text: string) => (
    <button
      type="button"
      aria-pressed={view === target}
      onClick={() => useEditor.getState().setCompareView(target)}
      className={`${BUTTON} ${view === target ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white' : 'bg-white text-gray-700'}`}
    >
      {text}
    </button>
  );
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-gray-200 bg-white p-2">
        <div role="group" aria-label="比較" className="flex gap-1 rounded-md border border-gray-300 p-0.5">
          {toggle('mine', '你的接線')}
          {toggle('ai', 'AI 標準佈線')}
        </div>
        {exit}
      </div>
      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1">
        {view === 'mine' ? (
          <BoardCanvas circuit={mine} label={mineLabel} selectable={false} registerAsMain />
        ) : (
          <BoardCanvas label={aiLabel} registerAsMain />
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
});

    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '開啟「乙」' }));

describe('file actions', () => {

    render(<DialogHost />);
    act(() => {

    });
    let saving: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
    act(() => {
      saving = saveCurrent();
    });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('名稱'), { target: { value: '我的 LED' } });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '確定' }));
    await act(async () => {
      await saving;
    });
    expect(state().dirty).toBe(false);
    expect(state().doc.name).toBe('我的 LED');
    expect(listProjects(getBrowserStore()).map((e) => e.name)).toEqual(['我的 LED']);
  });

  it('confirms before discarding unsaved work, and the new circuit can be undone', async () => {
    render(<DialogHost />);
    act(() => {
      state().run((doc) => placeComponent(doc, 'led', { x: 40, y: 12 }));
    });
    let pending: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
    act(() => {
      pending = newCircuit();
    });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '確定' }));
    await act(async () => {
      await pending;
    });
    expect(state().doc.circuit.components).toEqual([]);
    act(() => state().undo());
    expect(state().doc.circuit.components).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});
```

`src/app/panels/partTemplates.ts`：

```ts
import { getComponentDefinitions, RESISTOR_PRESETS } from '../../core/circuit/library';
import type { LedColor } from '../../types/component';
import type { PartTemplate } from '../model/editorTypes';
import { formatOhms, LED_COLOR_NAMES } from './format';

export interface PartCategory {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  parts: PartTemplate[];
}

const LED_COLORS: LedColor[] = ['red', 'green', 'yellow', 'blue'];

/** 規格第 12 節的元件庫分類。 */
export function partCatalog(): PartCategory[] {
  const ics = getComponentDefinitions()
    .filter((d) => d.category === 'ic')
    .map((d): PartTemplate => ({ key: d.id, defId: d.id, label: d.name, props: {} }));
  return [
    { id: '74ls', title: '74LS', parts: ics },
    {
      id: 'resistor',
      title: '電阻',
      parts: RESISTOR_PRESETS.map((ohms) => ({ key: `resistor-${ohms}`, defId: 'resistor', label: formatOhms(ohms), props: { resistance: ohms } })),
    },
    {
      id: 'led',
      title: 'LED',
      parts: LED_COLORS.map((color) => ({ key: `led-${color}`, defId: 'led', label: `${LED_COLOR_NAMES[color]} LED`, props: { color } })),
    },
    {
      id: 'power',
      title: '電源',
      parts: [
        { key: 'vcc', defId: 'vcc', label: 'VCC', props: {} },
        { key: 'gnd', defId: 'gnd', label: 'GND', props: {} },
        { key: 'dc-power', defId: 'dc-power', label: 'DC Power', props: {} },
      ],
    },
    {
      id: 'switch',
      title: '開關與按鈕',
      parts: [
        { key: 'dipsw-4', defId: 'dipsw-4', label: '指撥開關（4 位元）', props: {} },
        { key: 'dipsw-8', defId: 'dipsw-8', label: '指撥開關（8 位元）', props: {} },
        { key: 'pushbutton', defId: 'pushbutton', label: 'Push Button', props: {} },
      ],
    },
  ];
}

export function filterCatalog(catalog: PartCategory[], query: string): PartCategory[] {
  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!q) return catalog;
  return catalog
    .map((c) => ({
      ...c,
      parts: c.parts.filter((p) => p.label.toLowerCase().includes(q) || p.defId.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.title.toLowerCase().includes(q)),
    }))
    .filter((c) => c.parts.length > 0);
}
```

`src/app/panels/PartsPanel.tsx`：

```tsx
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { getComponentDefinition } from '../../core/circuit/library';
import type { ComponentInstance } from '../../types/component';
import { ComponentGlyph } from '../board/ComponentGlyph';
import { boundsOf, componentCells } from '../geometry/componentShape';
import { usePartDrag } from '../interaction/usePartDrag';
import type { PartTemplate } from '../model/editorTypes';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';
import { filterCatalog, partCatalog } from './partTemplates';

function PartIcon({ part }: { part: PartTemplate }) {
  const def = getComponentDefinition(part.defId);
  if (!def) return null;
  const instance: ComponentInstance = { id: 'icon', defId: part.defId, boardId: 'b1', anchor: { x: 0, y: 0 }, rotation: 0, props: part.props };
  const b = boundsOf(componentCells(instance, def));
  const m = 1.5;
  return (
    <svg aria-hidden="true" width={36} height={24} viewBox={`${(b.minX - m) * 10} ${(b.minY - m) * 10} ${(b.maxX - b.minX + 2 * m) * 10} ${(b.maxY - b.minY + 2 * m) * 10}`}>
      <ComponentGlyph instance={instance} />
    </svg>
  );
}

function PartTile({ part }: { part: PartTemplate }) {
  const onPointerDown = usePartDrag(part);
  const armed = useEditor((s) => s.armedPart?.key === part.key);
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      data-part={part.key}
      onPointerDown={onPointerDown}
      style={{ touchAction: 'none' }}
      className={`flex min-h-11 items-center gap-1 rounded-md border px-1.5 py-1 text-left text-xs hover:bg-gray-50 ${armed ? 'border-blue-500 bg-blue-50' : 'border-gray-200'}`}
    >
      <PartIcon part={part} />
      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{part.label}</span>
    </button>
  );
}

export function PartsPanel() {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const catalog = useMemo(() => partCatalog(), []);
  const visible = filterCatalog(catalog, query);
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col bg-white">
      <div className="border-b border-gray-200 p-2">
        <input
          type="search"
          aria-label="搜尋元件"
          placeholder="搜尋元件（例如 74LS00、220Ω）"
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 px-2 py-2 text-sm"
        />
      </div>
      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-2">
        {visible.map((c) => (
          <section key={c.id} className="mb-3">
            <h3 className="mb-1 text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500">{c.title}</h3>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1">
              {c.parts.map((p) => (
                <PartTile key={p.key} part={p} />
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>
        ))}
        {visible.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">找不到符合的元件</p>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/app/routing/routingService.ts`：

```ts
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';
import { RoutingClient } from './routingClient';
import { runAutoRoute } from './runAutoRoute';

let client: RoutingClient | null = null;

/** 第一次按「AI 標準佈線」時才載入 Worker，讓單元測試與首次載入不受影響。 */
export async function startAutoRoute(): Promise<void> {
  if (useEditor.getState().mode !== 'edit') return;
  if (!client) {
    const { createRoutingWorker } = await import('./workerFactory');
    client = new RoutingClient(createRoutingWorker);
  }
  await runAutoRoute(useEditor, client);
}

export function cancelAutoRoute(): void {
  client?.cancel();
}
```

`src/app/panels/StatusBar.tsx`：

```tsx
import { cancelAutoRoute } from '../routing/routingService';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';

export function StatusBar({ autosaveError }: { autosaveError: string | null }) {
  const mode = useEditor((s) => s.mode);
  const job = useEditor((s) => s.routingJob);
  const analysis = useEditor((s) => s.analysis);
  let status = '就緒';
  if (mode === 'routing') status = '計算中…';
  else if (mode === 'compare') status = '比較中';
  else if (job.status === 'animating' || job.status === 'done') status = '佈線完成';
  return (
    <footer className="flex min-h-9 flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 border-t border-gray-300 bg-white px-3 py-1 text-sm">
      <span data-testid="status">{status}</span>
      {mode === 'routing' && (
        <button type="button" onClick={cancelAutoRoute} className="min-h-11 rounded border border-gray-300 px-3 lg:min-h-0 lg:py-0.5">
          取消
        </button>
      )}
      <span>{`導線 ${analysis.wireCount}`}</span>
      <span>{`Net ${analysis.netCount}`}</span>
      <span>{`Routing Score ${analysis.score}`}</span>
      <span className="ml-auto text-gray-500">{autosaveError ?? '已自動儲存'}</span>
    </footer>
  );
}
```

`src/app/panels/dialogs.tsx`：

```tsx
import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { deleteProject, getBrowserStore, listProjects, type ProjectEntry } from '../persistence/browserStorage';

type DialogRequest = { id: number } & (
  | { kind: 'name'; title: string; initial: string; resolve: (value: string | null) => void }
  | { kind: 'confirm'; message: string; resolve: (ok: boolean) => void }
  | { kind: 'open'; resolve: (id: string | null) => void }
);

type DistributiveOmit<T, K extends keyof never> = T extends unknown ? Omit<T, K> : never;

const useDialogs = create<{ request: DialogRequest | null }>()(() => ({ request: null }));
let counter = 0;

function cancel(request: DialogRequest): void {
  if (request.kind === 'confirm') request.resolve(false);
  else request.resolve(null);
}

function show(request: DistributiveOmit<DialogRequest, 'id'>): void {
  const previous = useDialogs.getState().request;
  if (previous) cancel(previous);
  counter += 1;
  useDialogs.setState({ request: { ...request, id: counter } as DialogRequest });
}

function close(): void {
  useDialogs.setState({ request: null });
}

export const STORAGE_HINT = '學校電腦可能會清除瀏覽器資料，重要的電路請匯出 JSON';

export function askName(title: string, initial: string): Promise<string | null> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => show({ kind: 'name', title, initial, resolve }));
}

export function askConfirm(message: string): Promise<boolean> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => show({ kind: 'confirm', message, resolve }));
}

export function askOpenProject(): Promise<string | null> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => show({ kind: 'open', resolve }));
}

const BUTTON = 'min-h-11 rounded-md px-4 text-sm lg:min-h-9';

function Modal({ title, children }: { title: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/30 p-4">
      <div role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={title} className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-lg bg-white p-4 shadow-xl">
        <h2 className="mb-3 text-base font-semibold">{title}</h2>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function NameDialog({ request }: { request: Extract<DialogRequest, { kind: 'name' }> }) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState(request.initial);
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  return (
    <Modal title={request.title}>
      <form
        onSubmit={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (!trimmed) return;
          close();
          request.resolve(trimmed);
        }}
      >
        <input aria-label="名稱" autoFocus value={value} onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)} className="w-full rounded border border-gray-300 p-2" />
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500">{STORAGE_HINT}</p>
        <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            className={`${BUTTON} border border-gray-300`}
            onClick={() => {
              close();
              request.resolve(null);
            }}
          >
            取消
          </button>
          <button type="submit" disabled={!trimmed} className={`${BUTTON} bg-blue-600 text-white disabled:opacity-40`}>
            確定
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function ConfirmDialog({ request }: { request: Extract<DialogRequest, { kind: 'confirm' }> }) {
  const answer = (ok: boolean) => () => {
    close();
    request.resolve(ok);
  };
  return (
    <Modal title="請確認">
      <p className="text-sm">{request.message}</p>
      <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end gap-2">
        <button type="button" className={`${BUTTON} border border-gray-300`} onClick={answer(false)}>
          取消
        </button>
        <button type="button" className={`${BUTTON} bg-blue-600 text-white`} onClick={answer(true)}>
          確定
        </button>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function OpenDialog({ request }: { request: Extract<DialogRequest, { kind: 'open' }> }) {
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<ProjectEntry[]>(() => listProjects(getBrowserStore()));
  const [pendingDelete, setPendingDelete] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const remove = (entry: ProjectEntry) => {
    deleteProject(getBrowserStore(), entry.id);
    setPendingDelete(null);
    setEntries(listProjects(getBrowserStore()));
  };
  const finish = (id: string | null) => {
    close();
    request.resolve(id);
  };
  return (
    <Modal title="開啟專案">
      {entries.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">還沒有儲存過的專案</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="max-h-72 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {entries.map((entry) => (
            <li key={entry.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded border border-gray-200 p-2">
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <div className="truncate text-sm font-medium">{entry.name}</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">{new Date(entry.savedAt).toLocaleString('zh-TW')}</div>
              </div>
              {pendingDelete === entry.id ? (
                <>
                  <button type="button" aria-label={`確定刪除「${entry.name}」`} className={`${BUTTON} bg-red-600 text-white`} onClick={() => remove(entry)}>
                    刪除
                  </button>
                  <button type="button" className={`${BUTTON} border border-gray-300`} onClick={() => setPendingDelete(null)}>
                    不刪除
                  </button>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <button type="button" aria-label={`開啟「${entry.name}」`} className={`${BUTTON} bg-blue-600 text-white`} onClick={() => finish(entry.id)}>
                    開啟
                  </button>
                  <button type="button" aria-label={`刪除「${entry.name}」`} className={`${BUTTON} border border-gray-300`} onClick={() => setPendingDelete(entry.id)}>
                    刪除
                  </button>
                </>
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500">{STORAGE_HINT}</p>
      <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end">
        <button type="button" className={`${BUTTON} border border-gray-300`} onClick={() => finish(null)}>
          關閉
        </button>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

export function DialogHost() {
  const request = useDialogs((s) => s.request);
  if (!request) return null;
  switch (request.kind) {
    case 'name':
      return <NameDialog key={request.id} request={request} />;
    case 'confirm':
      return <ConfirmDialog key={request.id} request={request} />;
    case 'open':
      return <OpenDialog key={request.id} request={request} />;
  }
}
```

`src/app/fileActions.ts`：

```ts
import { getBoardElement } from './interaction/boardRegistry';
import { createProject } from './model/projectFile';
import { askConfirm, askName, askOpenProject } from './panels/dialogs';
import { getBrowserStore, openProject, saveProject } from './persistence/browserStorage';
import { downloadBlob, downloadText, pickTextFile } from './persistence/download';
import { boardSvgMarkup, svgMarkupToPng } from './persistence/exportImage';
import { parseProjectFile, projectFileName, serializeProject } from './persistence/projectIO';
import { useEditor } from './store/editorStore';

const UNSAVED = '目前的電路還沒有儲存，';

export async function newCircuit(): Promise<void> {
  const s = useEditor.getState();
  if (s.mode !== 'edit') return;
  if (s.dirty && !(await askConfirm(`${UNSAVED}確定要開新電路嗎？`))) return;
  useEditor.getState().replaceDocument(createProject(), { projectId: null, undoable: true, dirty: false });
}

export async function saveAs(): Promise<void> {
  const name = await askName('另存新檔', useEditor.getState().doc.name);
  if (!name) return;
  const result = saveProject(getBrowserStore(), { ...useEditor.getState().doc, name }, null);
  const s = useEditor.getState();
  if (!result.ok) {
    s.notify(result.reason, 'error');
    return;
  }
  s.markSaved(result.value.projectId, result.value.doc);
  s.notify(`已儲存「${name}」`, 'success');
}

export async function saveCurrent(): Promise<void> {
  const s = useEditor.getState();
  if (!s.projectId) return saveAs();
  const result = saveProject(getBrowserStore(), s.doc, s.projectId);
  if (!result.ok) {
    s.notify(result.reason, 'error');
    return;
  }
  s.markSaved(result.value.projectId, result.value.doc);
  s.notify(`已儲存「${result.value.doc.name}」`, 'success');
}

export async function openFromBrowser(): Promise<void> {
  const s = useEditor.getState();
  if (s.mode !== 'edit') return;
  if (s.dirty && !(await askConfirm(`${UNSAVED}確定要開啟其他專案嗎？`))) return;
  const id = await askOpenProject();
  if (!id) return;
  const result = openProject(getBrowserStore(), id);
  if (!result.ok) {
    useEditor.getState().notify(result.reason, 'error');
    return;
  }
  useEditor.getState().replaceDocument(result.value, { projectId: id, undoable: false, dirty: false });
}

export async function importJson(): Promise<void> {
  if (useEditor.getState().mode !== 'edit') return;
  const file = await pickTextFile();
  if (!file) return;
  const parsed = parseProjectFile(file.text, file.name.replace(/(\.breadboard)?\.json$/i, ''));
  const s = useEditor.getState();
  if (!parsed.ok) {
    s.notify(`無法匯入：${parsed.reason}`, 'error');
    return;
  }
  s.replaceDocument(parsed.doc, { projectId: null, undoable: true, dirty: true });
  s.notify(`已匯入「${parsed.doc.name}」`, 'success');
}

export function exportJson(): void {
  const doc = useEditor.getState().doc;
  downloadText(projectFileName(doc, 'breadboard.json'), serializeProject(doc));
}

export function exportSvg(): void {
  const el = getBoardElement();
  const s = useEditor.getState();
  if (!el) {
    s.notify('找不到麵包板畫面', 'error');
    return;
  }
  downloadText(projectFileName(s.doc, 'svg'), boardSvgMarkup(el), 'image/svg+xml');
}

export async function exportPng(): Promise<void> {
  const el = getBoardElement();
  const s = useEditor.getState();
  if (!el) {
    s.notify('找不到麵包板畫面', 'error');
    return;
  }
  try {
    downloadBlob(projectFileName(s.doc, 'png'), await svgMarkupToPng(boardSvgMarkup(el)));
  } catch (error) {
    s.notify(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error), 'error');
  }
}
```

  - `MenuBarProps { partsOpen; infoOpen; onToggleParts(); onToggleInfo(); onShowInfo() }`、`MenuBar(props)`：Logo、`檔案`／`編輯` 下拉選單（`role="menu"`）、`導線顏色` 選盤（`role="radio"`，名稱如 `紅色導線`）、`電路檢查 Check Circuit`、`AI 標準佈線`、`比較`（`aria-pressed`）；寬度 < 1024 px 時另有 `元件庫`、`資訊` 抽屜按鈕
