`tests/app/menuBar.test.tsx`：

```tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { act, cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { applyRoutingResult, placeComponent } from '../../src/app/commands/commands';
import { createProject } from '../../src/app/model/projectFile';
import { MenuBar, type MenuBarProps } from '../../src/app/panels/MenuBar';
import { startAutoRoute } from '../../src/app/routing/routingService';
import { resetEditor, useEditor } from '../../src/app/store/editorStore';
import { autoRoute } from '../../src/core/routing/autoRoute';
import { acceptanceCircuit } from '../fixtures/circuits';

vi.mock('../../src/app/routing/routingService', () => ({
  startAutoRoute: vi.fn(() => Promise.resolve()),
  cancelAutoRoute: vi.fn(),
}));

afterEach(cleanup);
beforeEach(() => {
  resetEditor();
  vi.mocked(startAutoRoute).mockClear();
});

const state = () => useEditor.getState();
const noop = () => {};
const renderBar = (props: Partial<MenuBarProps> = {}) =>
  render(<MenuBar partsOpen={false} infoOpen={false} onToggleParts={noop} onToggleInfo={noop} onShowInfo={noop} {...props} />);

describe('MenuBar', () => {
  it('lists the file menu items from the spec and closes with Esc', () => {
    renderBar();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '檔案' }));
    const menu = screen.getByRole('menu', { name: '檔案' });
    expect(within(menu).getAllByRole('menuitem').map((el) => el.textContent)).toEqual([
      '新電路',
      '儲存',
      '另存新檔',
      '開啟',
      '匯入 JSON',
      '匯出 JSON',
      '匯出 SVG',
      '匯出 PNG',
    ]);
    fireEvent.keyDown(window, { key: 'Escape' });
    expect(screen.queryByRole('menu')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('undoes and redoes from the edit menu', () => {
    renderBar();
    act(() => {
      state().run((doc) => placeComponent(doc, 'led', { x: 40, y: 12 }));
    });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '編輯' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: '重做' }).hasAttribute('disabled')).toBe(true);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: '復原' }));
    expect(state().doc.circuit.components).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('menu')).toBeNull();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '編輯' }));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: '重做' }));
    expect(state().doc.circuit.components).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('rotates and deletes the selected component', () => {
    renderBar();
    act(() => {
      state().run((doc) => placeComponent(doc, 'resistor', { x: 30, y: 6 }));
      state().select({ kind: 'component', id: state().doc.circuit.components[0].id });
    });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '編輯' }));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: '旋轉' }));
    expect(state().doc.circuit.components[0].rotation).toBe(90);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '編輯' }));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: '刪除' }));
    expect(state().doc.circuit.components).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('picks the wire colour', () => {
    renderBar();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: '藍色導線' }));
    expect(state().wireColor).toBe('#1565c0');
    expect(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: '藍色導線' }).getAttribute('aria-checked')).toBe('true');
    expect(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: '紅色導線' }).getAttribute('aria-checked')).toBe('false');
  });

  it('runs the circuit check and asks to show the info panel', () => {
    const onShowInfo = vi.fn();
    renderBar({ onShowInfo });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '電路檢查 Check Circuit' }));
    expect(state().rightTab).toBe('check');
    expect(onShowInfo).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('starts AI routing', () => {
    renderBar();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'AI 標準佈線' }));
    expect(startAutoRoute).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('enables compare only while the routing is up to date', () => {
    renderBar();
    const compare = () => screen.getByRole('button', { name: '比較' });
    expect(compare().hasAttribute('disabled')).toBe(true);
    act(() => {
      state().replaceDocument({ ...createProject(), circuit: acceptanceCircuit() }, { projectId: null, undoable: false, dirty: false });
      state().run((doc) => applyRoutingResult(doc, autoRoute(doc.circuit), 0));
    });
    expect(compare().hasAttribute('disabled')).toBe(false);
    fireEvent.click(compare());
    expect(state().mode).toBe('compare');
    expect(compare().getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    fireEvent.click(compare());
    expect(state().mode).toBe('edit');
    act(() => {
      state().run((doc) => placeComponent(doc, 'led', { x: 55, y: 12 }));
    });
    expect(compare().hasAttribute('disabled')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('toggles the drawers', () => {
    const onToggleParts = vi.fn();
    const onToggleInfo = vi.fn();
    renderBar({ onToggleParts, onToggleInfo });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '元件庫' }));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '資訊' }));
    expect(onToggleParts).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(onToggleInfo).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});
```

`tests/app/App.test.tsx`（整個取代 Task 1 的版本）：

```tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { act, cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { App, bootstrapEditor } from '../../src/app/App';
import { addWire, applyRoutingResult } from '../../src/app/commands/commands';
import { createProject } from '../../src/app/model/projectFile';
import { getBrowserStore, readAutosave, STORAGE_KEYS, writeAutosave } from '../../src/app/persistence/browserStorage';
import { resetEditor, useEditor } from '../../src/app/store/editorStore';
import { autoRoute } from '../../src/core/routing/autoRoute';
import { hole } from '../fixtures/build';
import { acceptanceCircuit } from '../fixtures/circuits';

afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  vi.useRealTimers();
});
beforeEach(() => {
  localStorage.clear();
  resetEditor();
});

const state = () => useEditor.getState();

describe('bootstrapEditor', () => {
  it('restores the autosaved project', () => {
    writeAutosave(getBrowserStore(), { ...createProject('實驗一'), circuit: acceptanceCircuit() }, 'p_1');
    bootstrapEditor();
    expect(state().doc.name).toBe('實驗一');
    expect(state().projectId).toBe('p_1');
    expect(state().analysis.wireCount).toBe(7);
    expect(state().dirty).toBe(false);
  });

  it('starts with an empty board when the autosave is damaged', () => {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.autosave, '{ broken');
    bootstrapEditor();
    expect(state().doc.circuit.components).toEqual([]);
    expect(state().projectId).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('App', () => {
  it('lays out the menu bar, parts library, board, info panel and status bar', () => {
    render(<App />);
    expect(screen.getByText('breadboard-lab')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '電路檢查 Check Circuit' })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('搜尋元件')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('board-svg')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: '選取' })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('status').textContent).toBe('就緒');
  });

  it('refreshes the analysis after 150 ms and autosaves after 1 s', () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    render(<App />);
    act(() => {
      state().run((doc) => addWire(doc, hole('A45'), hole('A50'), true, '#d32f2f'));
    });
    expect(state().analysis.wireCount).toBe(0);
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(150));
    expect(state().analysis.wireCount).toBe(1);
    expect(screen.getByText('導線 1')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(readAutosave(getBrowserStore())).toBeNull();
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(850));
    expect(readAutosave(getBrowserStore())?.doc.circuit.wires).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('saves with Ctrl+S through the name dialog', async () => {
    render(<App />);
    act(() => {
      fireEvent.keyDown(window, { key: 's', ctrlKey: true });
    });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('名稱'), { target: { value: '實驗二' } });
    await act(async () => {
      fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '確定' }));
    });
    expect(state().projectId).not.toBeNull();
    expect(state().doc.name).toBe('實驗二');
    expect(state().dirty).toBe(false);
  });

  it('opens the drawers and closes the parts drawer once a part is picked', () => {
    render(<App />);
    const parts = screen.getByRole('complementary', { name: '元件庫' });
    expect(parts.getAttribute('data-open')).toBe('false');
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '元件庫' }));
    expect(parts.getAttribute('data-open')).toBe('true');
    act(() => state().setArmedPart({ key: 'vcc', defId: 'vcc', label: 'VCC', props: {} }));
    expect(parts.getAttribute('data-open')).toBe('false');
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '資訊' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('complementary', { name: '資訊' }).getAttribute('data-open')).toBe('true');
  });

  it('shows the compare view in compare mode', () => {
    act(() => {
      state().replaceDocument({ ...createProject(), circuit: acceptanceCircuit() }, { projectId: null, undoable: false, dirty: false });
      state().run((doc) => applyRoutingResult(doc, autoRoute(doc.circuit), 0));
    });
    render(<App />);
    act(() => state().setMode('compare'));
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '離開比較' })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('status').textContent).toBe('比較中');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 實作選單列**

`src/app/panels/MenuBar.tsx`：

```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { exportJson, exportPng, exportSvg, importJson, newCircuit, openFromBrowser, saveAs, saveCurrent } from '../fileActions';
import { runShortcut, selectedComponentId } from '../interaction/useKeyboardShortcuts';
import { WIRE_COLORS } from '../model/colors';
import { isRoutingStale } from '../model/projectFile';
import { startAutoRoute } from '../routing/routingService';
import { useEditor } from '../store/editorStore';

const MOD = typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && /Mac|iPhone|iPad/.test(navigator.userAgent) ? '⌘' : 'Ctrl+';
const BUTTON = 'flex min-h-11 min-w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-md px-3 text-sm lg:min-h-8 lg:min-w-0';

interface MenuItem {
  label: string;
  onSelect: () => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
  hint?: string;
}

function DropdownMenu({ label, items }: { label: string; items: MenuItem[] }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const root = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onPointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {
      if (!root.current?.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);
    };
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setOpen(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    };
  }, [open]);
  return (
    <div ref={root} className="relative">
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-haspopup="menu"
        aria-expanded={open}
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
        className={`${BUTTON} hover:bg-gray-100 ${open ? 'bg-gray-100' : ''}`}
      >
        {label}
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div role="menu" aria-label={label} className="absolute left-0 top-full z-40 mt-1 min-w-44 rounded-md border border-gray-200 bg-white py-1 shadow-lg">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <button
              key={item.label}
              type="button"
              role="menuitem"
              title={item.hint}
              disabled={item.disabled}
              onClick={() => {
                setOpen(false);
                item.onSelect();
              }}
              className="block min-h-11 w-full px-4 text-left text-sm hover:bg-gray-100 disabled:text-gray-400 disabled:hover:bg-transparent lg:min-h-0 lg:py-1.5"
            >
              {item.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function WireColorPicker() {
  const wireColor = useEditor((s) => s.wireColor);
  return (
    <div role="radiogroup" aria-label="導線顏色" className="flex flex-wrap items-center">
      {WIRE_COLORS.map((c) => {
        const checked = c.hex.toLowerCase() === wireColor.toLowerCase();
        return (
          <button
            key={c.hex}
            type="button"
            role="radio"
            aria-checked={checked}
            aria-label={`${c.name}色導線`}
            title={`${c.name}色導線`}
            onClick={() => useEditor.getState().setWireColor(c.hex)}
            className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-md hover:bg-gray-100 lg:h-7 lg:w-7"
          >
            <span
              className={`block h-5 w-5 rounded-full border ${checked ? 'border-blue-600 ring-2 ring-blue-500 ring-offset-1' : 'border-gray-400'}`}
              style={{ backgroundColor: c.hex }}
            />
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

export interface MenuBarProps {
  partsOpen: boolean;
  infoOpen: boolean;
  onToggleParts: () => void;
  onToggleInfo: () => void;
  /** 按「電路檢查」後要讓資訊面板看得到（平板上是打開抽屜）。 */
  onShowInfo: () => void;
}

const save = () => void saveCurrent();

/** 規格第 12 節的選單列。 */
export function MenuBar({ partsOpen, infoOpen, onToggleParts, onToggleInfo, onShowInfo }: MenuBarProps) {
  const mode = useEditor((s) => s.mode);
  const doc = useEditor((s) => s.doc);
  const canUndo = useEditor((s) => s.history.past.length > 0);
  const canRedo = useEditor((s) => s.history.future.length > 0);
  const selection = useEditor((s) => s.selection);
  const canCompare = useMemo(() => doc.routing !== null && !isRoutingStale(doc), [doc]);
  const editing = mode === 'edit';
  const componentSelected = selectedComponentId(selection) !== null;

  const fileItems: MenuItem[] = [
    { label: '新電路', onSelect: () => void newCircuit(), disabled: !editing },
    { label: '儲存', onSelect: save, hint: `${MOD}S` },
    { label: '另存新檔', onSelect: () => void saveAs() },
    { label: '開啟', onSelect: () => void openFromBrowser(), disabled: !editing },
    { label: '匯入 JSON', onSelect: () => void importJson(), disabled: !editing },
    { label: '匯出 JSON', onSelect: exportJson },
    { label: '匯出 SVG', onSelect: exportSvg },
    { label: '匯出 PNG', onSelect: () => void exportPng() },
  ];
  const editItems: MenuItem[] = [
    { label: '復原', onSelect: () => runShortcut('undo', save), disabled: !editing || !canUndo, hint: `${MOD}Z` },
    { label: '重做', onSelect: () => runShortcut('redo', save), disabled: !editing || !canRedo, hint: `${MOD}Y` },
    { label: '旋轉', onSelect: () => runShortcut('rotate', save), disabled: !editing || !componentSelected, hint: 'R' },
    {
      label: '刪除',
      onSelect: () => runShortcut('delete', save),
      disabled: !editing || (!componentSelected && selection?.kind !== 'wire'),
      hint: 'Delete',
    },
  ];

  const toggleCompare = () => {
    const s = useEditor.getState();
    if (s.mode === 'compare') {
      s.setMode('edit');
      return;
    }
    if (s.mode !== 'edit' || !canCompare) return;
    s.setArmedPart(null);
    s.setMode('compare');
  };

  return (
    <header className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1 border-b border-gray-300 bg-white px-2 py-1">
      <button type="button" aria-expanded={partsOpen} onClick={onToggleParts} className={`${BUTTON} border border-gray-300 lg:hidden`}>
        元件庫
      </button>
      <span className="px-2 font-semibold">breadboard-lab</span>
      <DropdownMenu label="檔案" items={fileItems} />
      <DropdownMenu label="編輯" items={editItems} />
      <WireColorPicker />
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => {
          useEditor.getState().runCheck();
          onShowInfo();
        }}
        className={`${BUTTON} border border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-50`}
      >
        電路檢查 Check Circuit
      </button>
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={!editing}
        onClick={() => void startAutoRoute()}
        className={`${BUTTON} bg-blue-600 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50`}
      >
        AI 標準佈線
      </button>
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-pressed={mode === 'compare'}
        disabled={mode !== 'compare' && (!editing || !canCompare)}
        onClick={toggleCompare}
        className={`${BUTTON} border ${mode === 'compare' ? 'border-blue-600 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700' : 'border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-50'} disabled:opacity-40`}
      >
        比較
      </button>
      <button type="button" aria-expanded={infoOpen} onClick={onToggleInfo} className={`${BUTTON} ml-auto border border-gray-300 lg:hidden`}>
        資訊
      </button>
    </header>
  );
}
```

`src/app/App.tsx`（整個取代）：

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { BoardCanvas } from './board/BoardCanvas';
import { CompareView } from './board/CompareView';
import { saveCurrent } from './fileActions';
import { useKeyboardShortcuts } from './interaction/useKeyboardShortcuts';
import { CheckBanner, NoticeToast } from './panels/Banners';
import { DialogHost } from './panels/dialogs';
import { InfoPanel } from './panels/InfoPanel';
import { MenuBar } from './panels/MenuBar';
import { PartsPanel } from './panels/PartsPanel';
import { RoutingProblems, StatsCard } from './panels/RoutingStatus';
import { StatusBar } from './panels/StatusBar';
import { getBrowserStore, restoreAutosave, writeAutosave } from './persistence/browserStorage';
import { useRoutingAnimation } from './routing/useRoutingAnimation';
import { resetEditor, useEditor } from './store/editorStore';

export const ANALYSIS_DELAY_MS = 150;
export const AUTOSAVE_DELAY_MS = 1000;

/** 第一次繪製前呼叫：有可用的自動存檔就載入，沒有或損毀就用空白電路。 */
export function bootstrapEditor(): void {
  const { doc, projectId } = restoreAutosave(getBrowserStore());
  resetEditor(doc, projectId);
}

/** 規格第 4.5 節：電路改變後 150 ms 重算即時檢查與狀態列，文件改變後 1 秒自動存檔。回傳自動存檔的錯誤訊息。 */
function useBackgroundWork(): string | null {
  const doc = useEditor((s) => s.doc);
  const projectId = useEditor((s) => s.projectId);
  const [autosaveError, setAutosaveError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => useEditor.getState().refreshAnalysis(), ANALYSIS_DELAY_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [doc.circuit]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      const result = writeAutosave(getBrowserStore(), doc, projectId);
      setAutosaveError(result.ok ? null : result.reason);
    }, AUTOSAVE_DELAY_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [doc, projectId]);
  return autosaveError;
}

const saveShortcut = () => void saveCurrent();

const DRAWER = 'absolute inset-y-0 z-20 flex-col bg-white shadow-xl lg:static lg:z-auto lg:flex lg:shadow-none';

/** 規格第 12 節：≥ 1024 px 三欄；較窄時元件庫與資訊面板改成抽屜。 */
export function App() {
  const autosaveError = useBackgroundWork();
  useKeyboardShortcuts(saveShortcut);
  useRoutingAnimation();
  const mode = useEditor((s) => s.mode);
  const picking = useEditor((s) => s.armedPart !== null || s.ghost !== null);
  const [partsOpen, setPartsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [infoOpen, setInfoOpen] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (picking) setPartsOpen(false);
  }, [picking]);

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <MenuBar
        partsOpen={partsOpen}
        infoOpen={infoOpen}
        onToggleParts={() => setPartsOpen((v) => !v)}
        onToggleInfo={() => setInfoOpen((v) => !v)}
        onShowInfo={() => setInfoOpen(true)}
      />
      <div className="relative flex min-h-0 flex-1">
        <aside aria-label="元件庫" data-open={partsOpen} className={`${partsOpen ? 'flex' : 'hidden'} ${DRAWER} left-0 w-60 border-r border-gray-300`}>
          <PartsPanel />
        </aside>
        <main className="relative min-w-0 flex-1">
          {mode === 'compare' ? <CompareView /> : <BoardCanvas registerAsMain />}
          <StatsCard />
          <RoutingProblems />
          <CheckBanner />
        </main>
        <aside aria-label="資訊" data-open={infoOpen} className={`${infoOpen ? 'flex' : 'hidden'} ${DRAWER} right-0 w-75 border-l border-gray-300`}>
          <InfoPanel />
        </aside>
      </div>
      <StatusBar autosaveError={autosaveError} />
      <DialogHost />
      <NoticeToast />
    </div>
  );
}
```

import { defineConfig } from '@playwright/test';

/** Windows 內建的 Edge 就是 Chromium 核心；其他系統請先執行 npx playwright install chromium，或設定 PW_CHANNEL。 */
const channel = process.env.PW_CHANNEL || (process.platform === 'win32' ? 'msedge' : undefined);

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: 'e2e',
  timeout: 60_000,
  expect: { timeout: 15_000 },
  workers: 1,
  reporter: 'list',
  use: {
    baseURL: 'http://localhost:4173',
    channel,
    viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 },
    trace: 'retain-on-failure',
  },
  webServer: {
    command: 'npm run build && npm run build:single && npx vite preview --port 4173 --strictPort',
    url: 'http://localhost:4173',
    reuseExistingServer: false,
    timeout: 180_000,
  },
});

`scripts/make-e2e-fixtures.ts`：

```ts
import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { createProject } from '../src/app/model/projectFile';
import { serializeProject } from '../src/app/persistence/projectIO';
import type { ComponentInstance } from '../src/types/component';
import { wire } from '../tests/fixtures/build';
import { acceptanceCircuit, syntheticCircuit } from '../tests/fixtures/circuits';

const savedAt = new Date('2026-10-06T00:00:00Z');

/** syntheticCircuit 的元件都是 0° 的兩腳元件：電阻兩腳相距 4 格，LED 相距 1 格。 */
function pinsOf(c: ComponentInstance): [[number, number], [number, number]] {
  const span = c.defId === 'resistor' ? 4 : 1;
  return [
    [c.anchor.x, c.anchor.y],
    [c.anchor.x + span, c.anchor.y],
  ];
}

/** 規格第 13.3 節：50 個元件、100 條導線。 */
const dense = syntheticCircuit();
const parts = dense.components;
dense.wires.push(...parts.map((c, k) => wire(`v${k}`, pinsOf(c)[1], pinsOf(parts[(k + 29) % parts.length])[0])));

mkdirSync('e2e/fixtures', { recursive: true });
writeFileSync('e2e/fixtures/acceptance.breadboard.json', `${serializeProject({ ...createProject('驗收電路', savedAt), circuit: acceptanceCircuit() })}\n`);
writeFileSync('e2e/fixtures/dense.breadboard.json', `${serializeProject({ ...createProject('效能測試', savedAt), circuit: dense })}\n`);
console.log(`dense: ${dense.components.length} components, ${dense.wires.length} wires`);
```

Run: `npx tsx scripts/make-e2e-fixtures.ts`
Expected: 輸出 `dense: 50 components, 100 wires`，產生 `e2e/fixtures/` 下兩個 JSON 檔。

- [ ] **Step 3: 共用的操作函式**

`e2e/helpers.ts`：

```ts
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { expect, type Locator, type Page } from '@playwright/test';

export interface Pt {
  x: number;
  y: number;
}

/** 主畫面的麵包板（比較模式下是第一塊）。 */
export function board(page: Page): Locator {
  return page.getByTestId('board-svg').first();
}

export async function holeCenter(page: Page, name: string): Promise<Pt> {
  const box = await board(page).locator(`[data-layer="board"] [data-hole="${name}"]`).boundingBox();
  if (!box) throw new Error(`hole ${name} is not visible`);
  return { x: box.x + box.width / 2, y: box.y + box.height / 2 };
}

export function midpoint(a: Pt, b: Pt): Pt {
  return { x: (a.x + b.x) / 2, y: (a.y + b.y) / 2 };
}

/** 相鄰兩孔在螢幕上的距離（像素）。 */
export async function cellSize(page: Page): Promise<number> {
  const a = await holeCenter(page, 'A1');
  const b = await holeCenter(page, 'A2');
  return b.x - a.x;
}

/** 打開編輯器，等到第一次「符合畫面」完成、孔的位置不再變動。 */
export async function openEditor(page: Page, url = '/'): Promise<void> {
  await page.goto(url);
  await expect(board(page)).toBeVisible();
  await expect
    .poll(async () => {
      const a = await holeCenter(page, 'A1');
      await page.waitForTimeout(100);
      const b = await holeCenter(page, 'A1');
      return Math.abs(a.x - b.x) + Math.abs(a.y - b.y);
    })
    .toBeLessThan(0.5);
}

/** 從元件庫把元件拖到板子上；to 是元件範圍中心要落下的位置。 */
export async function dragPart(page: Page, key: string, to: Pt): Promise<void> {
  const tile = page.locator(`[data-part="${key}"]`);
  await tile.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
  const box = await tile.boundingBox();
  if (!box) throw new Error(`part ${key} is not visible`);
  await page.mouse.move(box.x + box.width / 2, box.y + box.height / 2);
  await page.mouse.down();
  await page.mouse.move(to.x, to.y, { steps: 12 });
  await page.mouse.up();
}

/** 用滑鼠從一個孔拉線到另一個孔：先往水平方向動，產生「先水平再垂直」的 L 形。 */
export async function drawWire(page: Page, from: string, to: string): Promise<void> {
  const a = await holeCenter(page, from);
  const b = await holeCenter(page, to);
  const cell = await cellSize(page);
  await page.mouse.move(a.x, a.y);
  await page.mouse.down();
  if (Math.abs(b.x - a.x) >= 1) await page.mouse.move(a.x + Math.sign(b.x - a.x) * cell * 0.8, a.y, { steps: 4 });
  await page.mouse.move(b.x, b.y, { steps: 10 });
  await page.mouse.up();
}

/** 檔案 → 匯入 JSON，選 e2e/fixtures 裡的檔案。 */
export async function importFixture(page: Page, file: string): Promise<void> {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '檔案' }).click();
  const chooser = page.waitForEvent('filechooser');
  await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: '匯入 JSON' }).click();
  await (await chooser).setFiles(resolve(process.cwd(), 'e2e/fixtures', file));
}

export function wires(page: Page, prefix = ''): Locator {
  return board(page).locator(prefix ? `[data-layer="wires"] [data-wire-id^="${prefix}"]` : '[data-layer="wires"] [data-wire-id]');
}

export function components(page: Page): Locator {
  return board(page).locator('[data-layer="components"] [data-component-id]');
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 驗收流程（規格第 13.2 節，網站版）**

`e2e/acceptance.spec.ts`：

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { board, components, dragPart, drawWire, holeCenter, midpoint, openEditor, wires } from './helpers';

/** 與子專案 1 的 acceptanceCircuit 相同的接線：VCC、GND、74LS00 第 1 閘 → 電阻 → LED → GND。 */
const MANUAL_WIRES: Array<[string, string]> = [
  ['T+6', 'A10'],
  ['B-6', 'J16'],
  ['J10', 'I16'],
  ['J11', 'H16'],
  ['J12', 'J30'],
  ['J34', 'J40'],
  ['J41', 'B-41'],
];

test('需求文件第三十四章的驗收流程', async ({ page }) => {
  // 1–2：開啟網站，看見 MB-102 麵包板（830 孔）
  await openEditor(page);
  await expect(board(page).locator('[data-layer="board"] [data-hole]')).toHaveCount(830);

  // 3–6：從元件庫拖入 74LS00、LED、電阻、VCC、GND
  await dragPart(page, '74LS00', await holeCenter(page, 'E13'));
  await dragPart(page, 'led-red', midpoint(await holeCenter(page, 'G40'), await holeCenter(page, 'G41')));
  await dragPart(page, 'resistor-220', await holeCenter(page, 'G32'));
  await dragPart(page, 'vcc', await holeCenter(page, 'T+5'));
  await dragPart(page, 'gnd', await holeCenter(page, 'B-5'));
  await expect(components(page)).toHaveCount(5);

  // 7：手動接線
  for (const [from, to] of MANUAL_WIRES) await drawWire(page, from, to);
  await expect(wires(page, 'm')).toHaveCount(7);
  await expect(page.getByText('導線 7', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();

  // 8–9：電路檢查，看到檢查結果
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '電路檢查 Check Circuit' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('tab', { name: '檢查結果' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');
  await expect(page.getByRole('tabpanel')).not.toBeEmpty();

  // 10–12：AI 標準佈線，依 Net 重新計算所有導線
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'AI 標準佈線' }).click();
  const card = page.getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: 'Standard Routing Complete' });
  await expect(card).toBeVisible();
  await expect(wires(page, 'm')).toHaveCount(0);
  await expect(wires(page, 'w_').first()).toBeAttached();

  // 13–17：新導線只有水平垂直、沒有重疊，統計卡顯示 Crossings、Total Length、Bends
  const paths = await wires(page, 'w_').evaluateAll((els) => els.map((el) => el.querySelector('path')?.getAttribute('d') ?? ''));
  expect(paths.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  for (const d of paths) {
    const points = [...d.matchAll(/[ML](-?[\d.]+) (-?[\d.]+)/g)].map((m) => [Number(m[1]), Number(m[2])]);
    expect(points.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
    for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i++) {
      expect(points[i][0] === points[i - 1][0] || points[i][1] === points[i - 1][1]).toBe(true);
    }
  }
  for (const label of ['Wire Count', 'Total Length', 'Bends', 'Crossings', 'Overlaps']) {
    await expect(card.getByText(label, { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  }
  await expect(card.locator('dt', { hasText: 'Overlaps' }).locator('xpath=following-sibling::dd[1]')).toHaveText('0');

  // 18：顯示 Routing Score（統計卡與狀態列）
  await expect(card.getByText('Routing Score', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText(/^Routing Score \d+(\.\d+)?$/)).toBeVisible();

  // 19：比較模式（1440 px 寬，左右並排）
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '比較' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByTestId('board-svg')).toHaveCount(2);
  await expect(page.getByText(/^你的接線（Routing Score/)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText(/^AI 標準佈線（Routing Score/)).toBeVisible();
  await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
  await expect(page.getByTestId('board-svg')).toHaveCount(1);

  // 20：Ctrl+Z 回到佈線前的接線
  await page.keyboard.press('Control+z');
  await expect(wires(page, 'm')).toHaveCount(7);
  await expect(wires(page, 'w_')).toHaveCount(0);
  await expect(page.getByText('導線 7', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 平板觸控、單一 HTML、存檔與匯出、效能參考**

`e2e/tablet.spec.ts`：

```ts
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { cellSize, components, holeCenter, midpoint, openEditor, wires, type Pt } from './helpers';

test.use({ viewport: { width: 820, height: 1180 }, hasTouch: true });

/** 用 Chromium 的觸控事件拖曳（先往水平方向動，決定 L 形的轉角）。 */
async function touchDrag(page: Page, from: Pt, to: Pt, cell: number): Promise<void> {
  const cdp = await page.context().newCDPSession(page);
  const send = (type: 'touchStart' | 'touchMove' | 'touchEnd', p?: Pt) =>
    cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', { type, touchPoints: p ? [{ x: p.x, y: p.y }] : [] });
  await send('touchStart', from);
  const dir = Math.abs(to.x - from.x) < 1 ? 0 : Math.sign(to.x - from.x);
  const first = dir === 0 ? { x: from.x, y: from.y + Math.sign(to.y - from.y) * cell * 0.8 } : { x: from.x + dir * cell * 0.8, y: from.y };
  await send('touchMove', first);
  for (let i = 1; i <= 8; i++) await send('touchMove', { x: first.x + ((to.x - first.x) * i) / 8, y: first.y + ((to.y - first.y) * i) / 8 });
  await send('touchEnd');
  await cdp.detach();
}

test('平板：按鈕夠大，可以用觸控放置元件與接線', async ({ page }) => {
  await openEditor(page);

  for (const button of await page.locator('header button').all()) {
    const box = await button.boundingBox();
    expect(box, (await button.getAttribute('aria-label')) ?? (await button.textContent()) ?? '').not.toBeNull();
    expect(Math.min(box!.width, box!.height)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(44);
  }

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '元件庫' }).tap();
  await page.locator('[data-part="led-red"]').tap();
  await expect(page.getByRole('complementary', { name: '元件庫' })).toBeHidden();
  const led = midpoint(await holeCenter(page, 'G40'), await holeCenter(page, 'G41'));
  await page.touchscreen.tap(led.x, led.y);
  await expect(components(page)).toHaveCount(1);

  await touchDrag(page, await holeCenter(page, 'A45'), await holeCenter(page, 'A50'), await cellSize(page));
  await expect(wires(page)).toHaveCount(1);
  await expect(page.getByText('導線 1', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
});
```

`e2e/single-file.spec.ts`：

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { importFixture, openEditor, wires } from './helpers';

const SINGLE_FILE = resolve(process.cwd(), 'dist-single/breadboard-lab.html');

test('單一 HTML 檔用 file:// 開啟可以完成一次 AI 佈線', async ({ page }) => {
  const html = readFileSync(SINGLE_FILE, 'utf8');
  expect(html).not.toMatch(/<script[^>]*\ssrc=/i);
  expect(html).not.toMatch(/<link[^>]*\shref=/i);

  const external: string[] = [];
  page.on('request', (request) => {
    if (!/^(file|blob|data):/.test(request.url())) external.push(request.url());
  });

  await openEditor(page, pathToFileURL(SINGLE_FILE).href);
  await importFixture(page, 'acceptance.breadboard.json');
  await expect(page.getByText('導線 7', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'AI 標準佈線' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Standard Routing Complete')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(wires(page, 'w_').first()).toBeAttached();
  expect(external).toEqual([]);
});
```

`e2e/files.spec.ts`：

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { importFixture, openEditor } from './helpers';

test('存到瀏覽器、重新整理後自動還原、開啟清單、匯出三種檔案', async ({ page }) => {
  await openEditor(page);
  await importFixture(page, 'acceptance.breadboard.json');
  await expect(page.getByText('已匯入「驗收電路」')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText('導線 7', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();

  await page.keyboard.press('Control+s');
  await page.getByLabel('名稱').fill('第一次實驗');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '確定' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('已儲存「第一次實驗」')).toBeVisible();

  await expect
    .poll(() => page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem('breadboard-lab:autosave')?.includes('第一次實驗') ?? false))
    .toBe(true);
  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.getByText('導線 7', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '檔案' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: '開啟' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: '開啟「第一次實驗」' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText('學校電腦可能會清除瀏覽器資料，重要的電路請匯出 JSON')).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '關閉' }).click();

  const exports: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['匯出 JSON', '第一次實驗.breadboard.json'],
    ['匯出 SVG', '第一次實驗.svg'],
    ['匯出 PNG', '第一次實驗.png'],
  ];
  for (const [item, filename] of exports) {
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: '檔案' }).click();
    const download = page.waitForEvent('download');
    await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: item }).click();
    expect((await download).suggestedFilename()).toBe(filename);
  }
});
```

`e2e/performance.spec.ts`：

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { board, components, importFixture, openEditor } from './helpers';

test('效能參考：50 個元件、100 條導線時拖曳元件', async ({ page }) => {
  await openEditor(page);
  await importFixture(page, 'dense.breadboard.json');
  await expect(page.getByText('導線 100', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();

  const box = await board(page).locator('[data-component-id="RA0"]').boundingBox();
  if (!box) throw new Error('RA0 is not visible');
  const start = { x: box.x + box.width / 2, y: box.y + box.height / 2 };
  await page.mouse.move(start.x, start.y);
  await page.mouse.down();
  const times: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 1; i <= 40; i++) {
    const t0 = performance.now();
    await page.mouse.move(start.x + i * 3, start.y);
    times.push(performance.now() - t0);
  }
  await page.mouse.up();

  const average = times.reduce((sum, t) => sum + t, 0) / times.length;
  const note = `指標移動處理時間：平均 ${average.toFixed(1)} ms，最長 ${Math.max(...times).toFixed(1)} ms（目標 < 16 ms，僅供參考）`;
  test.info().annotations.push({ type: 'performance', description: note });
  console.log(note);
  await expect(components(page)).toHaveCount(50);
});
```

Run: `npm run test:e2e`
Expected: 5 個測試全部 PASS；效能測試印出一行「指標移動處理時間：…」。若 Edge 無法啟動（例如不是 Windows），先徵求使用者同意下載 Chromium，再重跑。

失敗時用 `npx playwright show-trace test-results/<測試>/trace.zip` 檢查；修正的是應用程式的錯誤就回頭修對應的檔案並補單元測試，不要為了讓端對端測試通過而放寬斷言。
